The endpoint gap at this scale is substantially a training-maturity artifact: robustness matures later than accuracy, and stopping rules tuned to accuracy undersample it. Third, forcing cross-row redundancy down by half the structural separation between the distilled and fine-tuned conditions produces no detectable robustness response under two registered ways of matching accuracy. Verified transfer, a gap that closes while the structure never moves, and a null under direct intervention are together consistent with the deficit residing in features, not in the visible attention structure. This is elimination plus intervention, and its scope is the regime we measured. In this regime, attention overlays show where a model looks, not what it knows.

</details>


### [83] [JSL-DC: A Word-Level Japanese Sign Language Dataset with Linguist-Derived Descriptions for Distinguishing Confusable Signs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18412)
*Ken Takaki,Asuka Ando,Misa Suzuki,Uiko Yano,Masaya Tsujimoto,Bill Neubauer,Ananay Vikram Gupta,Rose Shao,Matthias Hoppe,Sahir Shahryar,Celeste Mason,Kai Kunze,Yohei Oseki,Yoshihiro Kawahara,Thad Starner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了JSL-DC，迄今最大的日语手语（JSL）数据集，包含19位手语者、36.7K视频和270个词汇，全部以聋人为中心构建，并基于语言学家提供的易混淆手语描述设计了识别模型，在易混淆子集上比SOTA方法提升9.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 95%的聋儿出生于听力正常的父母家庭，而这些父母通常不精通手语，手语识别技术可以赋能学习工具促进亲子沟通。然而日语手语缺乏大规模、多手语者的数据集，阻碍了能泛化到新用户的模型开发。

Method: 构建JSL-DC数据集：词库（270个JSL单词）由聋人和Coda语言学家选定以促进亲子沟通；所有参与者均为日常使用JSL的聋人；数据经过聋人语言学家的两阶段审查。同时提供语言学家推导的区分易混淆手语的描述，并基于这些描述设计识别模型。

Result: 所提出的受语言描述启发的模型在易混淆子集上比最先进的识别方法高出9.8%的准确率。数据集和语言描述将在CC-BY 4.0许可下发布。

Conclusion: JSL-DC填补了日语手语大规模多手语者数据集的空白，以聋人为中心的数据构建流程和语言学指导的模型设计展示了提升手语识别泛化能力的有效路径，为手语识别研究提供了新资源和新方向。

Abstract: Effective sign language (SL) acquisition is crucial for deaf children, yet 95% are born to hearing parents who often lack proficiency in SL. SL recognition can power learning tools to help parents communicate with their children. However, Japanese Sign Language (JSL) lacks large-scale, multi-signer datasets, hindering the development of models that can generalize to new users. To address this gap, we introduce JSL-DC, the largest JSL dataset by video count, comprising 36.7K videos from 19 signers. The entire process was Deaf-centric: the lexicon comprising 270 JSL words was selected by Deaf and Coda linguists to facilitate parent-child communication, all participants were Deaf individuals who use JSL daily, and the data underwent a two-stage review process involving Deaf linguists. Moreover, we provide linguist-derived descriptions for distinguishing confusable signs. We demonstrate that the proposed model inspired by the descriptions outperforms state-of-the-art recognition methods by 9.8% on the confusable subset. The dataset, along with its linguistic description that inspires new models, will be released under a CC-BY 4.0 license to accelerate research in SL recognition.

</details>


### [84] [CoMVS-GS: Collaborative Multi-View Stereo and 3D Gaussian Splatting for Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18413)
*Shihan Chen,Junjing Zhang,Qingsong Yan,Haibing Liu,Haofan Ren,Fei Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoMVS-GS是一个将多视图立体（MVS）与3D高斯泼溅结合的通用表面重建框架，通过MVS点云初始化、PatchMatch-3DGS互监督和Delaunay图割网格提取，在弱观测和遮挡区域提升几何重建精度与网格紧凑性，同时保持高质量渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯泼溅虽然能高效实现新视角合成，但在弱观测和遮挡区域难以准确重建网格，高斯原语可能生长成不稳定或几何不一致的结构，现有方法缺乏足够的几何先验来约束这些区域。

Method: 1) 从稠密多视图立体点云初始化高斯原语，具有预展平尺度和法线对齐方向，提供比稀疏SfM初始化更强的几何先验；2) 引入PatchMatch-3DGS互监督机制：高斯渲染的深度和法线初始化PatchMatch细化，细化后的PatchMatch深度反过来监督高斯优化；3) 用Delaunay图割网格管线替代TSDF体素融合来进行表面提取，降低对体素分辨率的敏感性并保留可见性一致的表面证据。

Result: 在DTU、GauU-Scene V2和MatrixCity数据集上的实验表明，CoMVS-GS在物体级重建上保持竞争力，在户外场景中显著提升几何精度和网格紧凑性，同时保持高渲染质量。

Conclusion: CoMVS-GS验证了多视图立体先验与高斯泼溅结合的有效性，为弱观测和遮挡区域的表面重建提供了一种鲁棒且通用的解决方案，兼顾了重建精度与渲染质量。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting enables efficient novel view synthesis, but accurate mesh reconstruction remains difficult in weakly observed and occluded regions, where Gaussian primitives may grow into unstable or geometrically inconsistent structures. We propose CoMVS-GS, a general surface reconstruction framework that combines Multi-View Stereo with Gaussian splatting. CoMVS-GS initializes Gaussian primitives from dense multi-view stereo points with pre-flattened scales and normal-aligned orientations, providing stronger geometric priors than sparse structure-from-motion initialization and reducing ambiguity during early optimization. It further introduces PatchMatch-3DGS Mutual Supervision, where Gaussian-rendered depths and normals initialize PatchMatch refinement, and refined PatchMatch depths supervise Gaussian optimization to improve weakly constrained geometry. For surface extraction, CoMVS-GS replaces truncated signed distance field voxel fusion with a Delaunay graph-cut meshing pipeline, reducing sensitivity to voxel resolution while preserving visibility-consistent surface evidence. Experiments on DTU, GauU-Scene V2, and MatrixCity show that CoMVS-GS remains competitive on object-level reconstruction and improves geometric accuracy and mesh compactness in outdoor scenes while maintaining high rendering quality.

</details>


### [85] [COSTA: A Cluster-Centric Paradigm for Annotation-Free Open-Set Semantic Segmentation of Aerial Point Clouds with Domain Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18479)
*Yanghong Lin,Li Fang,Tianyu Li,Shudong Zhou,Wei Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出COSTA，一种面向航空点云语义分割的测试时开放集自适应方法。COSTA将传统的闭合集逐点自适应范式转变为聚类中心的开放集语义传播范式：通过测试时自适应桥接域差距，将目标域点云聚类为语义簇，再借助开放词汇视觉语言模型获取的高置信度伪标签通过簇级投票传播到所有点。该方法无需额外训练即可对源标签空间之外的目标专属类别进行按需分割，在DALES作为源域时，跨三个异构航空点云基准实现了最高70.09% mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的测试时自适应方法受限于闭合集标签假设和不可扩展的逐点分割流程，难以应对航空点云在不同域转移下的语义偏移问题。作者希望在不进行额外训练的前提下，仅利用推理阶段的测试时自适应，使任意预训练模型适应发生域转移的目标域，并能按需分割源标签空间之外的目标专属类别。

Method: COSTA将问题从闭合集逐点自适应重构为聚类中心开放集语义传播。首先通过已验证的测试时自适应方法桥接域差距；然后在自适应特征空间中基于相似度分布将每批目标域点分组为少量语义簇；最后利用开放词汇视觉语言模型获得的高置信度伪标签，通过簇级投票将标签传播至所有点。核心发现是测试时自适应后航空点云的丰富特征分布可被蒸馏为紧凑且可跨标签空间迁移的语义质心集合。

Result: 以DALES为源域，COSTA在三个具有不同域和异构类别空间的航空点云基准上实现了按需分割，在新设定下达到最高70.09% mIoU，验证了该方法在显著域差距和混合语义偏移下的测试时自适应能力。

Conclusion: COSTA通过聚类中心的开放集语义传播范式，突破了闭合集逐点自适应在航空点云测试时分割中的局限，实现了跨异构标签空间的可扩展开放集分割。该工作表明，经过有效测试时自适应后，航空点云的语义特征可被压缩为可迁移的语义质心，从而支持无需重新训练的按需类别分割。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation of aerial point cloud is trapped in a generalization crisis under distinct domain shifts. While test-time adaptation offers a privacy-preserving and computationally efficient way to adapt pre-trained models to unlabeled target-domain data during inference, existing methods, bound to closed-set label assumptions and non-scalable point-wise segmentation pipelines, still struggle with semantic shifts. We ask: can we adapt any given pre-trained aerial point cloud segmentation model to a shifted target domain at the inference phase alone, without additional training, while segmenting target-specific categories beyond the source label space on demand? This paper introduces COSTA, which breaks this limitation by shifting from closed-set point-wise adaptation to cluster-centric open-set semantic propagation. Our core discovery is that, once effectively adapted at test time, the rich feature distribution of aerial point clouds can be distilled into a compact set of well-separated semantic centroids that are transferable across label spaces. COSTA leverages this to reformulate open-set semantic segmentation as a cluster-level propagating process: it first bridges the domain gap through proven test-time adaptation, then groups each batch of target-domain points into a small set of semantic clusters based on the similarity distribution in the adapted feature space, and finally propagates high-confidence pseudo labels obtained from an open-vocabulary vision-language model to all points through cluster-level voting. This cluster-centric paradigm enables test-time adaptation of aerial point clouds under significant domain gaps with mixed semantic shifts. With DALES as the source domain, COSTA enables on-demand segmentation across three aerial point cloud benchmarks with distinct domains and heterogeneous category spaces, achieving up to 70.09% mIoU under this new setting.

</details>


### [86] [Partition the Support, Reconstruct the Residual: Training-Free Sparse Attention for Video Generation and World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18484)
*Pardis Taghavi,Reza Langari,Gaurav Pandey*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SparsePR，一种无需训练的块稀疏注意力加速方法。它结合响应耦合分区（Response-Coupled Partitioning）与探针拟合残差重建（Probe-Fitted Residual Reconstruction），通过采样查询键响应形成K/V分组并建立共享路由坐标，再用少量精确查询行校准仿射校正。在四个异构视频生成与世界模型中，该方法以22.0-26.0%的实际执行对密度保持生成质量，实现1.48x-2.61x端到端加速，并持续降低注意力重建误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练无关的块稀疏注意力虽能加速视频Transformer，但行级注意力集中本身并不能直接指定可执行的稀疏算子。共享同一块路由的查询其支持集可能重叠度低，且仅保留的注意力质量无法决定跳过交互后的softmax后误差。作者希望解决分区几何对池化支持与残差可预测性的影响问题。

Method: SparsePR包含两个核心组件：1) Response-Coupled Partitioning：采样查询键响应形成配对的K/V组，利用其质心为共享路由建立查询-响应坐标；2) Probe-Fitted Residual Reconstruction：用少量精确查询行在探针残差观测的输出子空间中，校准一个针对当前调用的仿射校正（由稀疏输出映射到完整输出）。

Result: 在四个异构视频生成与世界模型上，SparsePR持续降低注意力重建误差。消融实验表明探针拟合贡献了大部分误差降低，而响应耦合分区在有限探针预算下降低硬丢弃误差并改善重建。SparsePR在22.0-26.0%的实际执行对密度下保持生成质量，实现1.48x-2.61x的端到端加速。

Conclusion: SparsePR通过将分区几何与残差重建结合，使得训练无关的块稀疏注意力在视频Transformer中既高效又可预测。分区质量影响残差可预测性，而探针拟合的仿射校正能有效补偿稀疏输出与完整输出之间的差距，为视频生成与理解模型提供了一种实用的加速方案。

Abstract: Training-free block-sparse attention can accelerate video transformers, but row-wise attention concentration does not by itself specify an executable sparse operator. Queries sharing a block route may have poorly overlapping supports, while retained attention mass alone does not determine the post-softmax error from skipped interactions. We show that partition geometry affects both pooled support and the predictability of the remaining residual from the sparse output. We introduce SparsePR, which combines Response-Coupled Partitioning with Probe-Fitted Residual Reconstruction. Sampled-query key responses form paired K/V groups, whose centroids induce query-response coordinates for shared routing. A small set of exact query rows then calibrates a call-specific affine correction from the sparse output within the output subspace observed in the probe residuals. Across four heterogeneous video generation and world models, SparsePR consistently reduces attention-reconstruction error. Ablations show that probe fitting accounts for most of this reduction, while response-coupled partitioning lowers hard-drop error and improves reconstruction under a finite probe budget. SparsePR preserves generation quality at 22.0-26.0% realized executed-pair density while achieving 1.48x-2.61x end-to-end speedups. Project page: https://pardistaghavi.github.io/SparsePR-website/

</details>


### [87] [DyG$^2$T: Modeling Object Dynamics with 3D Gaussian Temporal-Spatial Particle Graph Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18498)
*Yansong Wang,Zhaobo Qi,Xinyan Liu,Beichen Zhang,Shuhui Wang,Weigang Zhang,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DyG^2T是一种动力学建模框架，通过空间补全和时间判别关键点表示，并在粒子图上建模多尺度交互，从而实现从有限视觉观测中进行准确的物体运动轨迹预测和外观预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动力学建模方法在将粒子表示压缩为稀疏关键点后，使用局部约束交互建模其演化，这会丢弃细粒度局部细节，并模糊空间和时间尺度上的判别性交互建模，导致轨迹漂移和外观预测不准确。

Method: DyG^2T通过三个核心组件实现：空间上聚合相邻原始粒子位置以丰富关键点表示并编码关键点间相对偏移增强几何结构感知；时间上引入时间解缠网络(TDN)在潜空间中识别主导的帧间变化并放大帧间差异，再通过时间注意力聚合帧级时间演化线索；交互建模上使用粒子图Transformer通过全局注意力保持关键点间的判别性长程依赖，缓解局部约束建模导致的表示同质化。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上的实验表明，DyG^2T实现了准确的动力学建模和推理，并展现出强大的跨物体和真实世界泛化能力。

Conclusion: DyG^2T通过空间补全与时间判别相结合的策略，有效解决了关键点表示中的细节丢失和交互建模模糊问题，为从有限视觉观测进行物体运动轨迹预测提供了鲁棒且可泛化的解决方案。

Abstract: Modeling object dynamics from limited visual observations is a fundamental problem for enabling accurate motion trajectory prediction in embodied interaction scenarios. Existing dynamics modeling methods first compress reconstructed particle representations into sparse Key Points and model their evolution using locally constrained interactions, thereby discarding fine-grained local details and obscuring discriminative interaction modeling across spatial and temporal scales, leading to drifting trajectories and inaccurate appearance prediction. To tackle these issues, we propose DyG$^2$T, a dynamics modeling framework that infers object motion trajectories by spatially completing and temporally discriminating Key Point representations and modeling multi-scale interaction over particle graphs. Spatially, DyG$^2$T enriches each Key Point by aggregating neighboring raw particle positions to recover fine-grained local details, while explicitly encoding relative offsets among Key Points to enhance geometric structure perception. Temporally, we introduce a Temporal Disentangling Network (TDN) to identify dominant cross-frame variations in latent space and amplify inter-frame differences, yielding temporally discriminative representations that are subsequently aggregated via Temporal Attention to capture frame-wise temporal evolution cues. For comprehensive interaction modeling, a Particle Graph Transformer leverages global attention to preserve discriminative long-range dependencies among Key Points, mitigating representation homogenization induced by locality-constrained modeling and providing a robust basis for accurate trajectory prediction. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that DyG$^2$T achieves accurate dynamics modeling and reasoning, and exhibits strong cross-object and real-world generalization.

</details>


### [88] [Cross-Modal MRI Ovary Segmentation in Endometriosis Using Unpaired TVUS Prototype Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18515)
*Xingjian Kang,Lina Felsner,Dominik Perrin,Daiqi Liu,Jasmin Arjomandi,Franziska Mathis-Ullrich,Alexandra Stoll,Katharina Breininger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于MedSAM3和TVUS原型银行的双分支框架，实现跨经阴道超声(TVUS)和磁共振成像(MRI)的卵巢分割，相比SOTA方法在子宫内膜异位症数据上取得超5个百分点的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TVUS和MRI对子宫内膜异位症影像分析提供互补信息，但现有研究多集中于单模态分析或疾病分类，跨模态卵巢分割研究匮乏。且MRI中卵巢目标小、与周围盆腔结构边界模糊，分割难度大。

Method: 提出双分支框架，一个分支处理TVUS，另一个分支处理MRI。通过将TVUS导出的原型库(prototype bank)适配到MedSAM3模型，在跨模态间对齐解剖学一致的特征表示，利用源域TVUS的知识辅助目标域MRI的卵巢分割。

Result: 在子宫内膜异位症相关的TVUS和MRI数据集上进行大量实验，所提出的双分支方法相比多种SOTA方法在定量和定性指标上均取得超过5个百分点的改进。消融实验验证了原型库组件和TVUS源域暖启动预训练的重要性。

Conclusion: 跨模态特征对齐策略（利用TVUS原型库适配MedSAM3）能有效缓解MRI中卵巢小而边界模糊的分割难题，为超声和MRI的跨模态卵巢分割提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Transvaginal ultrasound (TVUS) and magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) provide complementary information for endometriosis image analysis, yet existing studies mainly focus on single-modality analysis or disease classification, leaving cross-modal ovarian segmentation largely unexplored. In this work, to tackle the increased difficulty of ovary segmentation in MRI due to ovaries' small target size and ambiguous boundaries with surrounding pelvic structures, we propose a dual branch framework for ovary segmentation across TVUS and MRI. More specifically, by adapting MedSAM3 with TVUS-derived prototype bank, we aim to align anatomically consistent feature representations across both modalities. Extensive experiments are conducted on endometriosis-related TVUS and MRI datasets. We observe quantitative and qualitative improvements of over 5 percentage points for the proposed dual-branch approach compared with multiple state-of-the-art methods. Furthermore, our ablation study shows the contribution of individual components such as the prototype bank and the importance of warm-up pretraining in the source TVUS domain.

</details>


### [89] [OptiModNet: A UNet-Transformer Hybrid with Grouped-Query and Channel Attention for Optic Disc and Cup Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18516)
*Soumili Ghosh,Debapriya Roy,Aryan Das,Bikash Santra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OptiModNet，一种轻量级混合架构用于青光眼筛查中的视盘和视杯分割。该模型在多个网络阶段集成多样注意力机制以增强局部和全局特征表示，并采用聚合金字塔损失来监督多解码器深度的预测。在REFUGE2数据集上达到最先进性能，超过现有方法2.5%以上，仅需3.73 GFLOPs和1.93M参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 青光眼的早期检测和大规模筛查需要对视盘和视杯进行精确分割，但现有深度学习模型（如UNet、ViT、扩散模型）要么局部特征捕获受限，要么计算开销巨大。低计算需求的方法对于资源受限的临床环境和快速大规模筛查至关重要，因此需要一种既高效又高性能的轻量级分割架构。

Method: 提出OptiModNet，一种轻量级混合架构，在网络多个阶段集成不同类型的注意力机制以同时增强局部和全局特征表示；设计聚合金字塔损失（Aggregated Pyramid Loss）在多个解码器深度监督预测，促进梯度流动和结构一致性。

Result: 在REFUGE2数据集上对视盘和视杯分割任务均取得最先进性能，超过现有方法2.5%以上，同时仅需3.73 GFLOPs和1.93M参数，实现了高性能与低计算复杂度的平衡。

Conclusion: OptiModNet有效解决了视盘/视杯分割中性能与计算效率之间的权衡问题，为青光眼大规模筛查和资源受限临床环境提供了实用的轻量级解决方案，且代码已开源。

Abstract: Precise segmentation of the optic disc and cup is critical for the early detection and diagnosis of glaucoma. However, achieving consistently high performance across datasets while maintaining low computational requirements remains a significant challenge. In glaucoma detection, low-computation methods are crucial for enabling rapid, large-scale screening and facilitating deployment in resource-limited clinical environments. While deep learning models such as UNets, Vision Transformers (ViTs), and Diffusion models have demonstrated strong segmentation performance but these methods often come with substantial computational overhead. UNets are efficient at capturing local features but are limited in modeling global contextual information. Conversely, ViTs excel at long-range dependency modeling but are computationally intensive. Hybrid architectures, such as UNetR, which combine transformer-based encoders with UNet-style decoders, have shown improved performance but while incurring additional complexity. Considering these, in this work, we propose OptiModNet, a light weight novel hybrid architecture tailored for optic disc and cup segmentation. The model integrates diverse attention mechanisms at multiple stages of the network to enhance both local and global feature representation. We include an Aggregated Pyramid Loss that supervises predictions at multiple decoder depths, to promote better gradient flow and structural consistency. We evaluate OptiModNet on the REFUGE2 dataset for both optic disc and cup segmentation tasks. Our method achieves state-of-the-art performance, exceeding existing approaches by over 2.5\%, while maintaining high efficiency with only 3.73 GFLOPs and 1.93M parameters. The code is available at https://github.com/SG1947/OptiModNet.

</details>


### [90] [Prior-Conditioned Gaussian Discriminants for Generalizable AI-generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18523)
*Shashank Kotyan,Makoto Shing,Yuki Imajuku,Rujikorn Charakorn,Tarin Clanuwat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究AI生成图像检测问题，将其建模为由训练先验、冻结编码器特征空间和决策规则组成的迁移系统，提出基于高斯判别的封闭解分类头（"先验条件高斯判别阶梯"），在覆盖39个公开数据集的Percept-Lens统一协议（710万张图像）上进行系统性评估。结果显示，最优级别的简单分类头在与先验和编码器匹配时，常常能媲美甚至超越已发布的检测器头，并揭示了对训练先验的强烈敏感性和矩估计头的数据高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测器在生成器、提示词/风格和源领域同时变化时性能明显下降，且缺乏对"分类器头训练到底在何时真正带来额外价值"的受控分析。作者希望理解在已有现代冻结特征的基础上，简单的统计分类头是否已经足够，从而为检测器的可迁移性研究提供更清晰的分析基线。

Method: 提出一种"先验条件高斯判别阶梯"（prior-conditioned Gaussian discriminant ladder），即基于一阶和二阶特征统计量、在不同嵌套协方差假设下构建的封闭解分类头。设计统一的评测协议Percept-Lens，覆盖39个公开数据集共710万张图像，系统对比不同梯级（rung）的分类头与已发布的检测器头，并控制训练先验与编码器变量。

Result: 在Percept-Lens协议下，最佳梯级的封闭解分类头在与训练先验和编码器匹配时，性能常与已发布的AI生成图像检测器头相当甚至更优；同时量化了训练先诱导漂移的强敏感性、矩基分类头的数据效率，以及高斯迁移度量对特征表示的依赖性。

Conclusion: 研究结论表明，简单、可解析的特征统计分类头在许多情况下已能匹配甚至超越复杂训练得到的检测器头，应鼓励在(先验、编码器、头)三个层面进行报告，并倡导采用更强的解析基线作为AIGI迁移研究的参照标准。

Abstract: Diffusion-based generators have made synthetic images ubiquitous, but detectors often fail under simultaneous shifts in generator, prompt/style, and source-domain. We study AI-generated image detection as a transfer system described by training prior, frozen encoder feature space, and decision rule, and ask when classifier head training adds value beyond what is already separable in modern features. As a controlled diagnostic, we fit a prior-conditioned Gaussian discriminant ladder: closed-form heads built from first- and second-order feature statistics under nested covariance assumptions. On Percept-Lens, a unified protocol over 39 public datasets (7.1 million images), the best rung is frequently competitive with, and sometimes exceeds, released AI-generated image detector heads when matched on both prior and encoder. We further quantify strong sensitivity to the training prior, data-efficiency of moment-based heads, and representation dependence of Gaussian shift metrics, motivating (prior, encoder, head)-level reporting and stronger analytical baselines for AIGI transfer.

</details>


### [91] [StateTrace: An Object-Centric Framework for Hidden-State Spatiotemporal Reasoning in Long Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18532)
*Yu Han,Wenhao Li,Yichao Cao,Hongyan Xu,Shuo Yang,Shan You,Xiu Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出StateTrace，一个面向视频大语言模型的对象中心框架，用于解决长视频中的"隐藏状态时空推理"问题——即目标物体在长时间不可见时段内，模型需从上下文交互推断其状态变化。同时构建了包含1,427个视频QA样本的诊断基准HSR-Bench。实验表明StateTrace在多个VideoLLM上持续提升性能（如VideoLLaMA3从39.6提升到64.2）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频大语言模型在长视频时空推理中表现不佳，尤其当目标物体变得不可见时，模型常常错误地将"不可见"视为"未知"。这暴露了现有模型缺乏对物体在遮挡或消失期间状态演变的推理能力，需要显式的隐藏状态推理机制。

Method: StateTrace构建了一个可复用的对象中心时空状态记忆，组织物体轨迹、物体间关系和状态转换事件为结构化推理基底。推理时，检索与问题相关的状态演化轨迹，转换为紧凑的推理线索，使模型能显式推理物体为何消失、不可见期间状态如何演化、以及查询时刻该状态是否应持续。同时构建HSR-Bench诊断基准，包含1,427个视频问答样本、1,384个独特视频。

Result: 在多个VideoLLM和多个基准（包括公开基准和HSR-Bench）上的大量实验表明，StateTrace持续提升性能。例如，在HSR-Bench上，将VideoLLaMA3从39.6提升到64.2。

Conclusion: StateTrace通过引入显式的隐藏状态时空推理机制，有效解决了VideoLLM在长视频中物体不可见时的推理缺陷，为视频理解中的长期时空推理提供了新的对象中心范式。

Abstract: Existing VLMs have achieved strong performance in video understanding, yet they struggle with long-video spatiotemporal reasoning when target objects become invisible, often mistaking "invisible" for "unknown". We define this challenge as hidden-state spatiotemporal reasoning: inferring object states during prolonged invisible intervals from context interactions. To address this, we propose StateTrace, a novel object-centric framework that endows VideoLLMs with an explicit mechanism for hidden state reasoning in long videos. StateTrace builds a reusable spatiotemporal state memory that organizes object trajectories, inter-object relations, and state-transition events into a structured reasoning substrate. At inference time, it retrieves question-relevant state-evolution trajectories and converts them into compact reasoning cues, enabling the model to explicitly reason about why an object disappears, how its state evolves while invisible, and whether that state should persist at query time. We further build HSR-Bench, a diagnostic benchmark for hidden-state reasoning, containing 1,427 video-QA samples from 1,384 unique videos. Extensive experiments across multiple VideoLLMs show that StateTrace consistently improves performance on both public benchmarks and HSR-Bench (e.g., improving VideoLLaMA3 from 39.6 to 64.2 on HSR-Bench).

</details>


### [92] [Zero-Shot SAM2 Segmentation and Vision Transformer-Based Recognition of Elamite Cuneiform Symbols from Degraded Tablet Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18544)
*Utsav Poudel,Rasik Bhattarai,Siddhartha Pathak,Raghavendra Ramacharna,Gaurav Jaswal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EpigraphNet，一种融合SAM2-Large分割引导和ViT-B/16分类的流水线，用于自动识别古代楔形文字。在Persepolis Fortification Archive的1,239张标注图板上，该方法达到86.41%的top-1准确率，较最强CNN基线提升17.21个百分点，并显著缓解了长尾分布导致的类别不平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 楔形文字自动识别面临三重信号退化挑战：粘土板三维浮雕造成的空间不均匀光照和投射阴影、表面侵蚀引入与真实符号印记重叠的结构噪声、以及141个符号类别间的严重类别不平衡。这些问题使传统CNN方法难以可靠识别古代楔形文字，亟需一种能同时解决光照退化、噪声干扰和类别失衡的稳健识别方案。

Method: EpigraphNet采用两阶段流水线：(1) 分割阶段——使用亮度自适应形态学预处理结合零样本SAM2-Large分割，从原始图板中生成干净的二进制符号掩码；(2) 分类阶段——在掩码图像上微调Vision Transformer (ViT-B/16)，并使用逆频率类别加权（inverse-frequency class weighting）来应对类别不平衡，最终在132类基准上进行分类。

Result: 在132类基准上EpigraphNet达到86.41%的top-1准确率，较最强CNN基线ResNet-101（69.20%）提升17.21个百分点，较DeiT-B/16、Swin-B、ConvNeXt-B、EfficientNet-B4四种现代主干网络提升5.31-12.91%。完整流水线在NVIDIA A100 GPU上单符号推理约18毫秒。较低的Spearman相关系数表明在频繁和稀有类别间识别更加均衡。

Conclusion: 分割引导的Transformer流水线有效解决了楔形文字识别中长期存在的信号退化与类别不平衡问题，充分证明了将SAM2零样本分割与ViT迁移学习相结合在文化遗产数字化的考古符号识别任务中的巨大潜力，为古代文字自动化解读提供了高效、均衡的解决方案。

Abstract: Automated recognition of ancient cuneiform script poses a compound signal-degradation problem: the three-dimensional relief of clay tablets creates spatially varying illumination and cast shadows, surface erosion introduces structured noise that overlaps with genuine sign impressions, and severe class imbalance across 141 sign categories undermines classifier reliability. We introduce EpigraphNet, a segmentation-guided transformer pipeline evaluated on the Persepolis Fortification Archive. From 1,239 annotated tablet images, brightness-adaptive morphological preprocessing and zero-shot SAM2-Large segmentation generate clean binary symbol masks, which a fine-tuned Vision Transformer (ViT-B/16) with inverse-frequency class weighting then classifies. EpigraphNet reaches 86.41% top-1 accuracy on a 132-class benchmark, a 17.21 percentage-point gain over the strongest CNN baseline (ResNet-101, 69.20%) and 5.31-12.91% over four modern backbones (DeiT-B/16, Swin-B, ConvNeXt-B, EfficientNet-B4) under identical conditions. The full pipeline runs at approximately 18 ms per sign on an NVIDIA A100 GPU. A lower Spearman correlation between sign frequency and per-class performance indicates more balanced recognition across frequent and rare classes. Implementation is available at: github.com/r11up/sam-guided-vit

</details>


### [93] [PATE-Forensics: Perception-as-Tool for Explainable Deepfake Forensics with General-Purpose MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18573)
*Yaqi Li,Jielun Peng,Yabin Wang,Jincheng Liu,Xiaopeng Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PATE-Forensics，一种"感知即工具"（Perception-as-Tool）范式的可解释深度伪造取证方法。该方法通过DINOv3构建一个将伪造检测与定位紧密耦合的取证感知工具，并将感知结果作为结构化上下文交给通用MLLM生成解释，无需针对任务的微调。在DDL-X Track 3上取得0.89的官方最佳成绩，领先第二名0.19分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释深度伪造取证方法依赖任务适配的MLLM同时处理检测、定位与解释，存在架构耦合不清晰、需要任务特定微调等问题。作者受智能体工具使用范式启发，提出将感知（检测与定位）与解释生成解耦的新范式，以提升各环节的专业性和整体性能。

Method: PATE-Forensics采用"感知即工具"范式：(1) 基于DINOv3构建取证感知工具，其中多粒度检测模块整合全局、patch级和segment级证据，并通过cue-guided定位模块将patch与segment级证据空间化为伪造得分图以指导密集掩码预测，实现检测与定位的紧密耦合；(2) 工具输出的原始图像与取证感知结果构成结构化取证上下文，交由通用MLLM在提示约束下生成解释，MLLM无需任务特定微调。

Result: 在DDL-X Track 3竞赛中，PATE-Forensics获得0.89的官方最佳得分，超过第二名团队0.19分，验证了该架构在检测、定位与解释三方面联合性能上的优势。

Conclusion: 将检测/定位与解释生成进行架构解耦、同时紧密耦合检测与定位的"感知即工具"范式是有效的，能够在无需MLLM微调的情况下获得显著优于现有方法的可解释深度伪造取证性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Existing explainable deepfake forensic methods typically rely on task-adapted MLLM to jointly address detection, localization, and explanation. Inspired by agent-style tool use, we instead introduce a Perception-as-Tool paradigm and instantiate it as PATE-Forensics, which architecturally decouples detection and localization from explanation generation while coupling detection and localization as tightly as possible within a forensic perception tool. The DINOv3-based tool couples a multi-granularity detection module that integrates global, patch-level, and segment-level evidence with a cue-guided localization module by spatializing the patch-level and segment-level evidence into forgery score maps that guide dense mask prediction. The original image and forensic perception outputs produced by the tool form structured forensic context for a general-purpose MLLM, which is guided by prompt constraints to generate explanations without task-specific fine-tuning. On DDL-X Track 3, PATE-Forensics achieves the best official score of 0.89, outperforming the second-ranked team by 0.19 points. Our code is available at https://github.com/yqli00000/PATE-Forensics.

</details>


### [94] [MR-IQA-2: Faithful Image Quality Reflection via Fine-Grained Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18579)
*Yuan li,Youyuan Lin,Chenhui Chu,Shin'ya Nishida*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MR-IQA-2框架，通过actor-editor-judge的推理-编辑-反思机制，解耦质量推理与质量评分的监督信号，利用编辑后的图像对比提供可验证的反思监督，从而提升盲图像质量评估中推理的忠实性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM用于IQA时，多数方法仅通过人工评分监督推理过程，但评分准确并不能保证推理忠实反映图像质量；此外共享奖励会模糊监督来源，在评分偶然正确时可能强化不忠实的推理。

Method: 提出MR-IQA-2框架，包含actor（生成质量推理）、editor（根据识别出的质量因素编辑图像）和frozen judge（比较原始与编辑图像，提供反思性监督）三个角色。通过细粒度信用分配解耦推理与评分的监督：judge反馈监督推理，人工评分监督预测评分；采用掩码token特定更新区分两类信号，同时保持推理到评分的因果链。

Result: 在多个IQA基准上，MR-IQA-2取得了与人类评价具有竞争力的评分对齐效果。视觉反思机制还提供了超出评分的更丰富、更忠实的视觉理解，可服务于图像质量优化及下游任务。

Conclusion: MR-IQA-2通过解耦推理和评分的信用分配，并提供基于视觉编辑的可验证监督，有效提升了盲IQA中推理的忠实性，在保持评分准确性的同时增强了可解释性和可靠性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong potential for image quality assessment (IQA) by improving consistency between quality ratings and their underlying reasoning. However, most approaches supervise reasoning through human-provided ratings and rarely examine whether it faithfully reflects image quality. Rating accuracy alone does not ensure faithful reasoning; a shared reward also obscures supervision sources and may reinforce unfaithful reasoning when a correct rating occurs by chance. To improve the faithfulness and reliability of blind IQA, we aim to (1) decouple credit assignment for reasoning and rating and (2) provide verifiable supervision for faithful reasoning. We introduce MR-IQA-2, an actor-editor-judge framework that operationalizes reasoning-editing-reflection. The actor generates quality reasoning for an input image, and the editor revises the image according to the identified quality factors. A frozen judge compares the original and edited images and provides reflective supervision for the actor's reasoning. MR-IQA-2 further uses fine-grained credit assignment to decouple reasoning and rating supervision. Judge feedback supervises reasoning, whereas human ratings supervise the predicted rating. Masked token-specific updates distinguish these signals while preserving the causal relation from reasoning to rating. Across IQA benchmarks, MR-IQA-2 achieves competitive rating alignment with humans. Visual reflection also enables richer and more faithful visual understanding beyond rating, which may inform image-quality optimization and related downstream tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/RobinY99/MR-IQA-2.

</details>


### [95] [SPARC: Subspace Position-Aware Robust Few-Shot Calibration for Distribution-Shifted Industrial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18585)
*Seokhee Han,Seungjun Chu,Mateusz Nowak,Peter Chin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SPARC，一种仅需少量已验证正常图像（k≤8）的少样本校准方法，通过代数饱和秩在编码器的原生patch网格上进行逐单元子空间投影，拦截patch特征并移除部署时的环境干扰（光照、夹具位置、传感器差异等）。该方法无需梯度或权重更新，适用于记忆库、密度、原型和互信息等各类检测器，在移位基准上将七个检测器的Image AUROC提升13.8个百分点，AU-PRO_0.3提升3.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于视觉的工业异常检测器在校准时基于一种数据分布训练，但部署时可能面临光照、夹具位置或传感器特性不同的分布，导致原本准确的检测器性能严重下降。虽然对来批次进行适应性校准是自然思路，但标注的异常样本稀缺，现有方法需要反向传播、检测器特定调参或对特征方向的选择，这些在少量校准样本下难以成立。

Method: SPARC在编码器与检测器之间拦截patch特征，利用k个经过验证的正常图像，通过闭式解的逐单元子空间投影移除部署时的环境干扰。其核心是用代数饱和秩r=k-1，在编码器的原生patch网格上估计并移除干扰方向。整个校正过程无需梯度更新或权重修改，可与记忆库、密度、原型、互信息等各类检测器兼容。

Result: 在移位基准上，SPARC为所有七个图像分数依赖于校正后patch特征的检测器，将池化的Image AUROC提升13.8个百分点，AU-PRO_0.3提升3.5个百分点；在无工程化移位的基准上，变化较小且方向混合。控制实验表明性能增益来自逐单元子空间结构而非仅靠校准图像；进一步消融实验支持饱和秩选择并刻画了其对骨干网络和校准条件的敏感性。

Conclusion: SPARC是一种简单、高效且通用的少样本异常检测校准方法，仅需少量正常图像即可通过逐单元子空间投影消除部署时的分布偏移，显著恢复检测器在移位环境下的性能，且无需对检测器做任何结构调整或训练。

Abstract: Vision-based industrial anomaly detectors are calibrated on one distribution but may be deployed on another that differs in illumination, fixture placement, or sensor characteristics, sharply degrading an otherwise accurate detector. Adapting to the incoming lot is a natural response, but labeled anomalies are scarce. We therefore consider calibration using only a handful of verified-normal images available before scoring the rest of the lot. Existing fixes require backpropagation, detector-specific tuning, or choices about feature directions that few calibration samples cannot justify. We present SPARC, a few-shot calibration method that intercepts patch features between encoder and detector and removes a closed-form, spatially indexed estimate of deployment-time nuisance through per-cell subspace projection. It needs only $k \le 8$ verified-normal images and uses the algebraic saturation rank $r{=}k{-}1$ on the encoder's native patch grid. The correction requires no gradient or weight updates and works with memory-bank, density, prototype, and mutual detectors. On the shift-prone benchmarks, SPARC improves pooled Image AUROC and AU-PRO$_{0.3}$ for all seven detectors whose image scores depend on corrected patch features by $+13.8$ and $+3.5$ percentage points (pp), respectively; on benchmarks without engineered shift, the changes are small and mixed. Controls that give competing corrections the same calibration images attribute these gains to the per-cell subspace structure rather than the images alone. Further ablations support the saturation-rank choice and characterize sensitivity to backbone and calibration conditions.

</details>


### [96] [OmniHandwritingOCR: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Evaluating Multimodal LLMs in Handwritten OCR Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18586)
*Zinuo Guo,Min Zhang,Bo Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniHandwritingOCR，一个用于诊断多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）和OCR系统在真实手写识别场景中表现的基准测试，涵盖手写文本识别和手写数学表达式识别六个子任务、十二个子集，共77.57K张标注图像，并评估了13个开源与闭源系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs越来越多地被用作文档和知识处理管线中的OCR系统，但其对手写内容的忠实读取能力尚未被充分探索。现有OCR基准大多聚焦于印刷文本或干净的单行输入，对多语言手写、书写者错误及结构复杂的数学表达式等真实手写OCR场景覆盖不足。

Method: 构建OmniHandwritingOCR基准，包含手写文本识别和手写数学表达式识别两大任务，细分为六个子任务和十二个子集，总计77.57K张标注图像（来自公开数据集和新收集的学生手写样本）。关键组成部分是难度分层的多行公式语料库，用于测试模型在结构复杂度递增下的鲁棒性。作者在统一协议下使用五种互补指标评估了13个开源和闭源系统。

Result: 实验结果显示当前系统在手写识别上仍远未达到忠实转录：在复杂多行公式上性能急剧下降；模型排名在不同语言和公式设置下存在差异；多个生成模型出现幻觉现象，产生看似合理但视觉上无依据的更正。

Conclusion: OmniHandwritingOCR为诊断多模态模型在手写OCR场景中的语言、内容、结构和视觉接地失败模式提供了一个具有挑战性的测试平台。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used as OCR systems in document and knowledge-processing pipelines, but their ability to faithfully read real handwriting remains underexplored. Existing OCR benchmarks focus largely on printed text or clean single-line inputs, leaving limited coverage of realistic handwritten OCR scenarios such as multilingual handwriting, writer errors, and structurally complex mathematical expressions. We introduce OmniHandwritingOCR, a diagnostic benchmark for evaluating MLLMs and OCR systems on handwritten OCR. It covers handwritten text recognition and handwritten mathematical expression recognition across six subtasks and twelve subsets, totaling 77.57K labeled images from public datasets and newly collected student writings. A key component is a difficulty-stratified multi-line formula corpus designed to test robustness under increasing structural complexity. We evaluate thirteen open- and closed-source systems with five complementary metrics under a unified protocol. Results show that current systems remain far from faithful transcription: performance drops sharply on complex multi-line formulas, model rankings vary across language and formula settings, and several generative models hallucinate plausible but visually unsupported corrections. OmniHandwritingOCR provides a challenging testbed for diagnosing language, content, structural, and visual-grounding failure modes of multimodal models in handwritten OCR scenarios.

</details>


### [97] [FD-CanKD: Frequency-Decoupled Cross-Attention Distillation as a Refinement Prior for Compact Object Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18590)
*YoungJae Cheong,Jhonghyun An*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FD-CanKD 是一种面向紧凑目标检测器的频域解耦跨注意力知识蒸馏框架，在三个互补层面（预测监督、非局部上下文传递、频域选择性对齐）传递教师知识，在 COCO 数据集上达到 48.87 mAP50:95，且部署时学生模型参数不变（19.7M）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑型目标检测器受限于资源约束，其表示能力与大型模型存在精度差距。现有检测器蒸馏方法通常仅依赖预测级监督或单一特征对齐目标（如响应、分布、相关性或频域匹配），缺乏多层面的互补知识传递，导致紧凑检测器的精度提升有限。

Method: FD-CanKD 提出三层面知识蒸馏框架：(1) 头级预测监督传递分类和回归知识；(2) 关系级通过跨注意力机制让学生特征聚合教师侧空间上下文，实现非局部上下文迁移；(3) 频域级采用组件选择性对齐，保留互补的结构性和细节敏感线索。蒸馏模块在训练后被完全移除，不影响部署。

Result: 在控制变量的 COCO 实验和固定50轮从头训练条件下，FD-CanKD 与代表性检测器蒸馏基线保持竞争力。蒸馏后继续微调20轮达到 48.87 mAP50:95、65.84 mAP50 和 53.40 mAP75，优于仅检测器微调的学生模型。训练后移除所有蒸馏模块，部署学生模型参数保持19.7M不变，在YOLOv12教师-学生设置中作为紧凑检测器案例验证。

Conclusion: FD-CanKD 通过多层面（预测、关系、频域）互补的知识蒸馏有效提升紧凑目标检测器精度，且蒸馏过程不增加部署开销，为资源受限场景下的紧凑检测器提供了一种可即插即用的蒸馏方案。

Abstract: Compact object detectors are suitable for resource-constrained visual perception, but their limited representation capacity creates an accuracy gap relative to large models. Conventional detector distillation often relies on prediction-level supervision or a single feature-alignment target, such as response, distribution, correlation, or frequency-domain matching. Frequency-Decoupled Cross-Attention Knowledge Distillation (FD-CanKD) is presented as a detector-oriented framework that transfers teacher knowledge at three complementary levels: head-level prediction supervision, relation-level non-local context transfer, and frequency-level component-selective alignment. Student features first aggregate teacher-side spatial context through cross-attention-based relation transfer, after which frequency-aware alignment preserves complementary structural and detail-sensitive cues. Under controlled Microsoft Common Objects in Context (COCO) experiments, fixed 50-epoch from-scratch comparisons show that FD-CanKD remains competitive with representative detector knowledge distillation baselines. Post-distillation continued fine-tuning further produces a stronger refinement-ready student than detector-only fine-tuning, reaching 48.87 mean average precision (mAP) at intersection-over-union thresholds from 0.50 to 0.95 (mAP50:95), 65.84 mAP50, and 53.40 mAP75 after 20 additional epochs. All distillation modules are removed after training, leaving the deployed student unchanged at 19.7M parameters. The framework is instantiated and evaluated in a controlled YOLOv12 teacher-student setting as a representative compact-detector case study.

</details>


### [98] [ReX-Shot: Single-Image Rephotography via Geometry- and Camera-Grounded Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18593)
*Ruiqi Zhang,Hao Zhu,Wenhao Zhang,Qi Zhang,Junqi Shi,Ming Lu,Xun Cao,Zhan Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReX-Shot 是一个统一的单图重摄影生成框架，能够从单张参考图像联合控制视点、焦距和参数化的摄影效果，通过隐式变换基础模型特征、几何引导的超分辨率以及3D感知的外观编辑，实现首个联合控制这三要素的统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单图重摄影方法通常将视点、焦距和摄影效果分开处理，难以联合控制。新视角合成在焦距变化下会产生几何畸变，超分辨率和指令编辑局限于2D，无法在视角变化下可靠扩展细节恢复和外观控制。作者将这些限制归因于单图3D重建不完美以及连续焦距放大存在采样极限。

Method: 1) 使用隐式变换的基础模型特征，减少几何误差导致的投影偏差，提供鲁棒的目标视角引导；2) 将焦距放大建模为几何引导的超分辨率问题，利用生成式细节先验恢复稀疏3D重采样丢失的细节；3) 在3D感知生成骨干上，将摄影效果控制从2D滤波提升到3D感知的外观编辑，保持跨视点和焦距的内容一致性。

Result: 实验表明ReX-Shot在视点、焦距和摄影效果三类控制上均优于代表性基线方法，并支持近实时的交互式重摄影。

Conclusion: ReX-Shot是首个从单张图像联合控制视点、焦距和参数化摄影效果的统一框架，为单图重摄影提供了一个几何与相机锚定的生成式解决方案。

Abstract: Single-image rephotography aims to synthesize new shots of a scene from a single reference image with specified viewpoints, focal lengths, and photographic effects, which are intrinsically coupled in imaging. Existing methods typically treat these factors separately and struggle under joint control: novel-view synthesis may introduce geometric distortions under focal-length changes, while super-resolution and instruction-guided editing remain confined to 2D and cannot reliably extend detail restoration or appearance control to novel viewpoints. We attribute these limitations to imperfect single-image 3D reconstruction and the sampling limit of continuous focal-length enlargement. To reduce projection bias from geometric errors, we use implicitly transformed foundation-model features for robust target-view guidance. We further formulate focal-length enlargement as a geometry-guided super-resolution problem and exploit generative detail priors to recover details lost during sparse 3D resampling. Built on this 3D-aware generative backbone, we lift photographic-effect control from 2D filtering to 3D-aware appearance editing, preserving content consistency across viewpoints and focal lengths. These components form ReX-Shot, a geometry- and camera-grounded generative framework for single-image rephotography. To our knowledge, ReX-Shot is the first unified framework to jointly control viewpoint, focal length, and parameterized photographic effects from a single image. Experiments show that ReX-Shot outperforms representative baselines across all three controls while enabling near-real-time interactive rephotography.

</details>


### [99] [Teach a Molmo2Fish: Towards interactive fish tracking with natural language guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18602)
*Kai Van Brunt,Justin Kay,Sara Beery*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于多模态大语言模型的交互式预测校正工作流（Molmo2Fish），用于改进声纳鱼类追踪中的不完美预测结果。通过人机对话方式对轨迹进行修正，初步实验表明该方法在鱼类追踪与轨迹校正任务上表现良好，但自然语言引导的融合仍有提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生态学研究中计算机视觉被广泛用于自动化识别任务，但多目标追踪等复杂任务仍具挑战。研究者探索通过人机协同流程利用不完美的预测结果，但目前缺乏有效的交互式校正方法，尤其缺乏利用多模态大语言模型进行对话式轨迹校正的研究。

Method: 提出Molmo2Fish，一种将交互式预测校正建模为与多模态大语言模型对话的工作流。针对声纳鱼追踪数据集，在引导式（guided）和非引导式（unguided）任务中测试其校正自身预测轨迹以及外部轨迹的能力。

Result: Molmo2Fish在鱼类追踪和轨迹校正任务上取得了较高的性能，但在融入自然语言引导方面仍有较大的改进空间。

Conclusion: 基于多模态大语言模型的对话式轨迹校正是生态学视觉任务中处理不完美预测的有前景方向，代码与数据已公开，未来需进一步优化自然语言引导的融合策略。

Abstract: Computer vision is increasingly used to automate recognition tasks in large ecological datasets, but more complex tasks such as multi-object tracking continue to pose challenges. As researchers seek to incorporate vision models in ecology workflows, various lines of research have explored how to make imperfect predictions useful through human-in-the-loop processes. We propose a new approach to working with imperfect tracking predictions through an interactive prediction correction workflow taking place as a conversation with a multimodal large language model, which we tailor to a sonar fish tracking dataset as an initial proof of concept. We investigate the performance of the tool, Molmo2Fish, across guided and unguided tasks, correcting its own predicted tracks and external tracks. We find that Molmo2Fish achieves high performance on fish tracking and track correction tasks, but there is still much room to improve on incorporating natural language guidance. The code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/tidalove/molmo2fish.

</details>


### [100] [VA-Judger: Reward Modeling from Human Preference Feedback for Joint Video-Audio Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18607)
*Yinming Huang,Shuyuan Tu,Xi Yan,Zihan Yang,Jianhua Han,Xu Hang,Yu-Gang Jiang,Zuxuan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VA-Judger，一个用于联合视频-音频生成的思维链全方位奖励模型，基于大规模人类偏好数据集VAPref-10K训练，通过分维度强化学习提供更密集、更全面的人类对齐奖励信号，有效缓解了现有度量指标的奖励黑客问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频-音频生成模型的强化学习后训练依赖单一质量维度度量（如音频质量、视觉保真度、同步性）的组合作为奖励信号，但这些度量分别评估感知维度，无法捕捉文本提示、视频、音频之间的整体语义与时间连贯性，导致奖励黑客问题——模型在高分但人类感知不连贯或失真的内容上过拟合。

Method: 1) 构建VAPref-10K大规模人类偏好数据集，包含9K提示和10.3K细粒度配对比较；2) 提出VA-Judger-Bench基准，覆盖域内与域外模型比较；3) 提出VA-Judger三阶段训练：首先在有明显质量差距的样本对中学习结构化输出与粗略偏好判别，然后通过拒绝采样结合人类注释验证蒸馏出可靠偏好解释用于近似质量比较，最后进行分维度强化学习将人类反馈分解为各质量维度以获得比单一二元偏好标签更密集的奖励信号。

Result: 实验表明VA-Judger在域内和域外评估中预测人类偏好均优于度量基线；将其人类对齐奖励用于音频-视频生成模型后训练，显著提升了生成质量。

Conclusion: VA-Judger通过思维链推理和分维度强化学习，提供了一个更全面、更符合人类偏好的奖励模型，有效解决了现有度量指标在多模态生成评估中的局限性，并验证了人类对齐奖励信号对联合视频-音频生成模型后训练的显著增益。

Abstract: Using reinforcement learning to post-train joint video-audio generation models requires a reward signal. Existing methods construct this reward by combining metrics for individual quality dimensions, including audio quality, visual fidelity, and synchronization. However, these metrics evaluate perceptual dimensions separately and fail to capture the overall semantic and temporal coherence among the text prompt, video, and audio that shapes human preferences. Optimizing models against these metrics encourages reward hacking, generating video-audio content that achieves high scores on these metrics yet appears incoherent or unfaithful to human viewers. To address this problem, we first construct a large-scale human-preference dataset VAPref-10K for joint video-audio generation, comprising 9K prompts and 10.3K fine-grained paired comparisons from open-source generation models. We also introduce the VA-Judger-Bench benchmark with both in-domain and out-of-domain model comparisons to evaluate whether reward models truly align with human preferences. We further propose VA-Judger, a chain-of-thought omni-reward model for joint video-audio generation. In particular, VA-Judger first learns from pairs with clear quality gaps to establish structured output and coarse preference discrimination, then distills reliable preference explanations for harder near-quality comparisons via rejection sampling verified against human annotations, and finally performs dimension-wise reinforcement learning that decomposes human feedback into individual quality dimensions for denser reward signals than a single binary preference label. Experiments show that VA-Judger outperforms metric baselines in predicting human preferences on both in-domain and out-of-domain evaluations. Using its human-aligned rewards for post-training audio-video generation model also yields significant improvements in generation quality.

</details>


### [101] [CDGP: Contrastive Dual Gaussian Processes for Weakly Supervised Anomaly Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18614)
*Seungjun Chu,Seokhee Han,Mateusz Nowak,Peter Chin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了对比双高斯过程（CDGP），一种弱监督工业缺陷分割框架，通过建模正常与异常诱导变量预测分布，利用后验优势统计量标准化预测均值差，结合层次化正常重建残差实现定位，在MVTec AD~2、KSDD2和VisA上表现优异，且无需像素级标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业视觉检测需要同时判断缺陷存在并定位缺陷，但像素级掩码标注成本高昂。现有异常分割方法仅从无缺陷图像学习并评分偏离程度，但真正的缺陷与异常但正常的区域都可能产生显著偏离并获得相似的高分，难以区分，因此需要一种无需像素标注且能更好区分真实缺陷与正常变异的弱监督方法。

Method: CDGP采用弱监督框架，对密集token建模正常与异常两类诱导变量的高斯过程预测分布，提出后验优势统计量：将预测均值之差除以联合预测不确定性进行标准化，同时提供空间证据和图像级置信度。该证据与层次化正常重建残差互补后用于精细定位。所有校准仅使用训练数据，无需人工像素标注或测试时拟合。通过分解和匹配线性头控制实验界定线性核G P公式的贡献与适用范围。

Result: 在MVTec AD~2、KSDD2和VisA上，CDGP在所有评估方法中的MVTec AD~2定位指标上排名第一，在KSDD2和VisA上取得第一或具有竞争力的结果。因子化与匹配线性头控制实验揭示了线性核高斯过程公式的贡献范围。

Conclusion: CDGP通过对比双高斯过程的后验优势统计量，有效区分真实缺陷与异常但在正常范围内的变异，在无需像素标注的条件下实现了领先的异常分割性能，且方法具有良好的可解释性与可控性。

Abstract: Industrial visual inspection must both decide whether a product is defective and localize the defect, yet pixel-level masks are costly to collect at scale. Most anomaly-segmentation methods learn only from defect-free images and score deviations from normality. A true defect and an unusual-but-normal region, however, can both deviate substantially and receive similarly high scores. We propose Contrastive Dual Gaussian Processes (CDGP), a weakly supervised framework that models normal and anomaly inducing-variable predictive distributions over dense tokens. Its posterior-dominance statistic standardizes their predictive-mean difference by the joint predictive uncertainty, providing both spatial evidence and image-level confidence. This evidence complements hierarchical normal-reconstruction residuals for fine localization. All calibration uses training data only, without human pixel annotations or test-time fitting. Across MVTec AD~2, KSDD2, and VisA, CDGP ranks first among the evaluated methods on all MVTec AD~2 localization metrics and is first-place or competitive on KSDD2 and VisA. Factorized and matched linear-head controls delimit the contribution and scope of the linear-kernel Gaussian process (GP) formulation.

</details>


### [102] [SPARC: Slice-to-volume Pipeline for Automated Reconstruction of gated 3D+time fetal Cardiac MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18616)
*Arnaud Boutillon,Naomi Clarke,Tomas Woodgate,Daniel West,Alina Schneider,Rachael Franklin,Anthony Price,Jo Hajnal,Kuberan Pushparajah,David Lloyd,Maria Deprez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍SPARC管线，一种用于胎儿心脏MRI（fCMR）的自动化切片到体素重建（SVR）管道，结合了基于物理信息的SVR算法（利用多普勒超声门控切片）与深度学习模型进行胸部分割和解剖学重定向，实现了较现有方法10倍的重建加速和质量提升，并在大规模临床队列中验证了其临床部署可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胎儿心脏MRI能提供与超声心动图互补的诊断信息，尤其对复杂先天性心脏病。然而，由于胎儿不可预测的运动以及缺乏自动化、稳健且适合临床部署的处理工具，从2D+time切片重建3D+time电影体积仍然极具挑战，这成为该技术临床转化的主要瓶颈。

Method: SPARC管线将以下组件集成在一起：（1）基于物理信息的切片到体素重建（SVR）算法，利用多普勒超声（DUS）门控的切片堆叠进行重建；（2）用于胸腔分割的深度学习（DL）模型，采用集成聚合策略；（3）用于解剖学重定向的DL模型。整个管线以端到端自动化方式运行。

Result: SVR算法较现有逐帧方法重建时间降低10倍（4.8±1.0 vs 49.0±14.1分钟，p<0.0001），同时重建质量有所提升；胸腔分割的集成聚合性能超过评分者间一致性（Dice 84.7±3.9% vs 81.4±7.7%，p<0.05）；解剖重定向成功率达90.1%；在大规模临床队列（n=121）中82.6%的病例实现全自动处理，平均运行时间7.1±1.3分钟。

Conclusion: SPARC管线在重建效率、分割精度和自动化程度上均显著优于现有方法，其运行时间与自动化率满足临床部署要求。该管线已以Docker容器形式公开并提供机构临床使用，有望推动胎儿心脏MRI在临床实践中的落地应用。

Abstract: Fetal cardiac MRI (fCMR) provides valuable diagnostic information complementary to echocardiography, particularly for complex congenital heart disease (CHD). Dynamic cine imaging captures cardiac motion essential for assessment of cardiac function; however, the reconstruction of 3D+time cine volumes from 2D+time acquired slices remains challenging due to unpredictable fetal motion and the absence of automated and robust processing tools suitable for clinical deployment. We present the SPARC pipeline (Slice-to-volume Pipeline for Automated Reconstruction of gated 3D+time fetal Cardiac MRI) which combines physics-informed slice-to-volume reconstruction (SVR) of Doppler ultrasound (DUS) gated stacks of slices, assisted by deep learning (DL) models for thoracic segmentation and anatomical reorientation. The proposed SVR algorithm achieves a tenfold reduction in reconstruction time relative to existing frame-wise approaches ($4.8 \pm 1.0$ vs $49.0 \pm 14.1$ min, $p < 0.0001$) while improving the reconstruction quality. Thoracic segmentation performance using ensemble aggregation exceeded inter-rater agreement (Dice $84.7 \pm 3.9\%$ vs $81.4 \pm 7.7\%$, $p<0.05$), while anatomical reorientation achieved a success rate of $90.1\%$. End-to-end evaluation on a large held-out clinical cohort ($n = 121$) demonstrated fully automatic processing in $82.6\%$ of cases with a mean runtime of $7.1 \pm 1.3$ min, compatible with clinical deployment. The complete SPARC pipeline is publicly available as a Docker container https://hub.docker.com/r/aboutill/sparc and is currently deployed at our institution as a clinical research tool.

</details>


### [103] [PALATE: Personalized Aesthetic Learning through Adaptive Taste Evolution for Multi-User Portrait Retouching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18622)
*Jingxuan Wang,Yifan Mei,Yuxia Niu,Chaowan Jiao,Qijin Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PALATE，一种共享奖励进化框架，通过将用户奖励分解为全局骨干、类别残差和轻量用户适配器，实现无需微调编辑模型的个性化人像修图偏好选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人像修图的目标本质上是主观的，同一张肖像可以有多个专业有效的结果，用户对最佳结果意见不一。现有方法要么优化群体美观标准无法捕捉个体品味，要么为每个用户微调编辑模型导致高昂的训练、存储和数据成本。

Method: PALATE将每个用户的奖励分解为三层：所有用户共享的全局骨干、美学相似用户的类别级残差、以及轻量用户适配器，并加入抗坍缩正则化保持三层互补。采用循环双级蒸馏方案，先将用户特定偏好蒸馏为类别奖励，再将类别知识整合到全局骨干中并重新分配以初始化下一轮进化。

Result: 在PPR10K专家修图候选上，对未见用户和未见图像获得72.83%的成对偏好预测准确率，超越所有奖励、美学和图像质量基线（最强基线PickScore仅58.06%）。每个新用户仅需512字节的用户特定参数和毫秒级打分。

Conclusion: PALATE通过共享奖励进化框架实现了低成本的个性化修图偏好建模，随着进化轮次推进共享初始化逐步改善，未见用户仅需少量排序即可完成校准。

Abstract: Automatic portrait retouching has advanced rapidly, yet its objective is inherently subjective: the same portrait admits multiple professionally valid results, and users disagree about which one is best. Most existing methods optimize a population-level aesthetic standard and therefore cannot capture individual taste, while fine-tuning a separate editing model for every user incurs prohibitive training, storage, and data costs. We propose PALATE, a shared reward-evolution framework that keeps the image editor fixed and instead personalizes the selection among retouched candidates of the same source portrait. PALATE decomposes the reward for each user into a global backbone shared by all users, category-level residuals shared by aesthetically similar users, and a lightweight user adapter, with anti-collapse regularizers keeping the three levels complementary.A cyclic dual-level distillation scheme first distills user-specific preferences into category rewards and then consolidates the resulting category-level knowledge into the global backbone, which is redistributed to initialize the next evolution round. In this way, the shared initialization improves progressively across rounds, enabling unseen users to be calibrated from only a few rankings. On expert-retouched candidates from PPR10K with held-out users and held-out images, PALATE attains 72.83% pairwise preference-prediction accuracy, surpassing all reward, aesthetic, and image-quality baselines, of which the strongest, PickScore, reaches 58.06%. Each new user costs only 512 bytes of user-specific parameters and millisecond-level scoring.

</details>


### [104] [Evaluation of Image Matching Methods for Visual Odometry on UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18624)
*Gašper Spagnolo,Luka Čehovin Zajc,Matej Dobrevski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了最新的深度学习图像匹配方法在无人机视觉里程计（VO）任务中的表现，使用向下摄像头和合成数据集进行实验。结果表明，最新的RoMa匹配器效果最佳，但传统SIFT特征仍可超越部分最新深度学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机依赖GNSS导航在信号不可用或受干扰时会遭遇灾难性故障，因此需要探索视觉里程计作为替代导航组件。尽管已有大量基于深度学习的图像匹配方法被提出，但尚未被集成到完整的VO系统中进行验证，这促使作者评估这些方法在无人机位置跟踪任务中的实际表现。

Method: 作者在自建的合成数据集上，针对配备向下摄像头（downwards-facing camera）的无人机位置跟踪任务，系统评估了多种最新（state-of-the-art）图像匹配方法在视觉里程计中的性能，并与传统SIFT特征方法进行对比。

Result: 实验结果表明，RoMa匹配器生成的VO结果最佳；然而传统SIFT特征在某些情况下能够超越部分最新深度学习方法。

Conclusion: 最新的深度学习图像匹配方法（如RoMa）确实能提升VO性能，但传统特征方法（如SIFT）仍具有竞争力，说明在为无人机VO系统选择图像匹配方法时需要综合考虑精度与效率等因素。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) are becoming a powerful tool for many environmental monitoring and transport applications. Yet, their reliance on Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS) technology for navigation makes them susceptible to catastrophic failures in scenarios where the positioning signal is unavailable or disrupted. This work explores Visual Odometry (VO) as a crucial navigation component. Recently, numerous deep-learning-based methods for image matching have been proposed that are yet to be implemented in a fully-fledged VO system. In this paper, we evaluate recent state-of-the-art image matching methods for the task of VO for UAV position tracking, with a downwards-facing camera, on our synthetic dataset, and find that while the best results are generated by the recent RoMa matcher, SIFT features can outperform some recent state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [105] [PCQA-R1: Advancing Generalized 3D Point Cloud Quality Assessment with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18627)
*Kangning Ye,Yunhao Li,Sijing Wu,Yucheng Zhu,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PCQA-R1，首个用于三维点云质量评估的强化学习大语言模型（LMM）。基于组相对策略优化（GRPO），通过逆向推理策略构建思维链数据集PCQA-CoT作为冷启动训练数据，并引入高斯近邻奖励机制防止校准漂移。实验表明PCQA-R1在五个基准上实现了SOTA级跨数据集泛化，同时保持有竞争力的域内精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无参考点云质量评估（PCQA）近年来活跃，但大语言模型（LMM）在该领域探索不足。现有LMM方法依赖监督微调直接预测数值质量分数，在异构MOS量表和不充分标注条件下缺乏跨数据集泛化能力。绝对MOS回归在不同评分尺度和失真分布下不稳定，而相对质量排序更鲁棒。

Method: PCQA-R1采用GRPO强化学习策略。首先通过逆向推理策略构建思维链数据集PCQA-CoT，作为LMM生成推理过程的冷启动训练数据；随后引入高斯近邻奖励，将分数预测锚定到源MOS范围以防止校准漂移，从而同时建模质量理解与评分。

Result: 在五个基准上实现了SOTA级跨数据集泛化，并取得有竞争力的域内准确度。消融实验验证了排序机制、高斯奖励和冷启动推理痕迹各自的贡献。

Conclusion: PCQA-R1验证了强化学习框架结合思维链冷启动与高斯奖励机制，能够有效解决LMM在PCQA任务中跨数据集泛化不足的问题，为该领域提供了新的方法论方向。

Abstract: No-reference point cloud quality assessment (PCQA) has been an active topic in recent years and is used to measure and optimize the visual experience of point clouds. However, large multimodal models (LMMs) have rarely been explored in this area. Previous LMM-based methods mainly rely on supervised fine-tuning to directly predict numerical quality scores, lacking the ability to generalize across datasets with heterogeneous MOS scales and limited annotations. A key difficulty is that absolute MOS regression can be brittle across datasets with different score scales and distortion distributions, whereas relative quality ranking is more stable under such shifts. In this paper, we present PCQA-R1, the first reinforcement learning LMM for 3D point cloud quality assessment to simultaneously model quality understanding and scoring. Built upon the group relative policy optimization (GRPO) strategy, PCQA-R1 first constructs a chain-of-thought dataset, PCQA-CoT, which serves as cold-start training data through a reverse reasoning strategy that teaches the LMM to generate its reasoning process. We further introduce a Gaussian proximity reward that prevents calibration drift by anchoring score predictions to the source MOS range. Experimental results demonstrate that PCQA-R1 achieves state-of-the-art cross-dataset generalization across five benchmarks and competitive in-domain accuracy. Ablation studies support the role of ranking, Gaussian reward, and cold-start traces.

</details>


### [106] [When Safety Overrides Vision: Exploring Dynamics between Vision Influence and Safety Alignment in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18628)
*Mehak Gupta,Tanmoy Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了对齐视觉语言模型（VLM）中安全对齐抑制感知接地的问题。发现模型在安全约束指令下即使视觉证据仍被内部保留，也会拒绝回答本可正确回答的问题。作者通过解码动力学分析、表征组织对比和激活级干预，证明感知接地在拒绝行为中基本保留，而安全指令主要改变晚期隐藏状态动力学朝向拒绝解码，且通过抑制拒绝相关表征可恢复接地回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对齐VLM旨在平衡视觉推理与安全生成，但在安全约束下模型经常对本可正确回答的问题选择弃答。这引出一个关键问题：安全对齐是否抑制了感知接地本身，还是视觉证据在内部仍可用而生成被转向拒答？此前这一失败模式尚未被充分探索。

Method: 1）跨多种架构和多个多模态基准，系统分析安全诱导拒绝时的内部解码动力学；2）对比安全约束与默认指令下隐状态和视觉证据对生成的影响；3）对比不同架构中拒绝相关表征的组织方式；4）通过针对性的激活级干预（抑制拒绝相关表征）来验证能否恢复接地回答。

Result: 1）拒绝生成的整个解码过程中仍持续受视觉证据影响，表明感知接地基本保留；2）不同架构间的拒绝表征组织存在显著差异，但安全约束指令一致地改变晚期隐藏状态动力学朝拒绝定向解码；3）抑制拒绝相关表征可在无需重训练或修改视觉输入的情况下可靠地恢复接地回答行为。

Conclusion: 安全对齐可以覆盖接地视觉表达，即使感知证据在内部仍然保留。这揭示了对齐VLM中一个此前未充分探索的失败模式：安全性与视觉接地的冲突可能发生在生成控制层面而非感知层面。

Abstract: Aligned vision-language models (VLMs) are designed to balance grounded visual reasoning with safe generation behavior. However, we observe a striking phenomenon: under safety-constrained instruction, models frequently abstain from answering questions that remain correctly answerable under default instruction despite receiving identical image-question inputs. This raises a fundamental question: does safety alignment suppress perceptual grounding itself, or does visual evidence remain internally available while generation is redirected toward abstention? In this work, we investigate the internal decoding dynamics underlying safety-induced abstention in aligned VLMs. Across multiple architectures and multimodal benchmarks, we show that abstained generations remain consistently influenced by visual evidence throughout decoding, indicating that perceptual grounding is largely preserved despite refusal behavior. We further demonstrate that, although the representational organization of refusal differs substantially across architectures, safety-constrained instruction consistently alters late-stage hidden-state dynamics toward refusal-oriented decoding. Finally, through targeted activation-level interventions, we show that suppressing refusal-related representations reliably restores grounded answering behavior across models without retraining or modifying visual inputs. Together, these findings reveal a previously underexplored failure mode in aligned VLMs: safety alignment can override grounded visual expression even when perceptual evidence remains internally preserved.

</details>


### [107] [SAM2Dual: Training-Free, Dual Memory for Long-Term Video Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18640)
*JeongRae Kim,Changwon Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SAM2Dual，一种无需训练的即插即用推理期增强方法，通过双记忆设计与文本感知记忆机制解决长视频目标分割中因漂移导致的误差累积问题，在MOSEv2和LVOSv2等长视频基准上取得稳定的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频目标分割（VOS）在长时间遮挡、物体重现和场景变化等情况下面临误差累积的严峻挑战。虽然SAM2具备强大的零样本性能，但其流式记忆机制在长时序下会被近期不可靠的预测主导，导致漂移放大。现有方法缺乏在不更新模型权重的前提下增强长视频鲁棒性的手段。

Method: SAM2Dual包含两个核心组件：(1) Dual Memory设计，显式分离短期记忆（用于快速局部自适应）与长期记忆（通过间隔采样构建以保留全局身份线索），并通过门控融合策略整合二者；(2) Text-Aware Memory (TAM)机制，从早期帧提取紧凑的词级语义线索，利用文本嵌入依据语义兼容性对记忆贡献进行重新加权，从而在视觉证据微弱或模糊时支持身份保持。

Result: 在长视频基准上，SAM2Dual一致地提升了长视频稳定性，在MOSEv2上将J&F从49.33提升至50.65，并在LVOSv2上取得一致的增益。

Conclusion: SAM2Dual通过解耦长短时记忆并引入文本感知的语义加权机制，为长视频VOS提供了一种无需训练、可即插即用的推理期增强方案，有效缓解了SAM2类方法在长时序下的漂移问题。

Abstract: Long-term video object segmentation (VOS) remains challenging due to error accumulation under extended occlusions, re-appearance, and scene changes. Although SAM2 provides strong zero-shot performance, its streaming memory can amplify drift over long horizons when recent, unreliable predictions dominate the memory state. We propose SAM2Dual, a training-free, plug-and-play inference-time enhancement that improves long-video robustness without updating model weights. SAM2Dual introduces a Dual Memory design that explicitly separates (i) short-term memory for rapid local adaptation and (ii) long-term memory built via interval-based sampling to preserve global identity cues, combined through a gated fusion strategy. In addition, we present Text-Aware Memory (TAM), which extracts a compact word-level cue from early frames and uses text embeddings to reweight memory contributions based on semantic compatibility, supporting identity preservation when visual evidence becomes weak or ambiguous. Across long-term benchmarks, SAM2Dual consistently improves stability on long videos, raising J&F from 49.33 to 50.65 on MOSEv2 and achieving consistent gains on LVOSv2.

</details>


### [108] [Clinically Structured Surrogate Rewards for Post-SFT Medical Image Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18654)
*Hyun Jun Kim,Heeseung Shin,Changwon Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种面向医学图像描述生成（post-SFT）的临床结构化替代奖励框架，结合生物医学语义、短程词汇保真、图像邻域对齐和临床图一致性四种奖励，通过GDPO优化，在ImageCLEFmedical Caption两个赛道及三个视觉-语言主干上全面超越SFT基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像描述生成需要将异质视觉证据转化为精确的临床描述，发现结果、断言状态或解剖关系的错误会改变临床含义，即使表面流畅。现有序列级策略优化的奖励依赖全局文本相似度、图像-字幕直接兼容性或无序概念重叠，未显式建模视觉邻域和临床声明结构，导致优化目标与临床语义要求存在差距。

Method: 提出临床结构化替代奖励框架：(1)四种奖励——生物医学语义相似度、短程词汇保真、分布性图像邻域对齐（匹配参考和生成字幕诱导的医学图像库分布）、临床图一致性（对实体、断言状态和类型化关系进行最大权重一对一匹配）；(2)四种奖励在各自rollout组内独立归一化；(3)以固定相对权重组合，采用GDPO（广义直接偏好优化）进行优化。

Result: 在组织者评估的隐藏测试集上，覆盖Standard和Synthetical两个ImageCLEFmedical Caption赛道及三种视觉-语言主干，全部六种主干-赛道组合均优于匹配的SFT基线。Overall、Relevance、Factuality平均相对增益分别为3.4%、2.1%、5.8%。消融和成对诊断显示结构化奖励提供互补信号，降低图像邻域散度并改善实体-断言-关系一致性。

Conclusion: 结构化的临床奖励（图像邻域对齐与临床图一致性）为医学图像描述生成提供了超越全局文本相似度的优化信号，两者与词汇级奖励互补，可系统性地提升描述的临床保真度与事实性。

Abstract: Medical image captioning requires translating heterogeneous visual evidence into concise clinical descriptions, where errors in findings, assertion states, or anatomical relations can alter clinical meaning despite surface-level fluency. Sequence-level policy optimization can directly optimize complete captions, but common rewards rely on global text similarity, direct image-caption compatibility, or unordered concept overlap, leaving visual neighborhoods and clinical-claim structure implicit. We propose a clinically structured surrogate reward framework for post-SFT medical image captioning. The framework combines biomedical semantic and short-range lexical fidelity with two structured rewards: distributional image-neighborhood alignment, which matches the medical-image-bank distributions induced by reference and generated captions, and clinical graph consistency, which applies maximum-weight one-to-one matching to entities, assertion states, and typed relations. The four rewards are independently normalized within each rollout group, combined with fixed relative weights, and optimized with GDPO. Across organizer-evaluated hidden test sets for the Standard and Synthetical ImageCLEFmedical Caption tracks and three vision-language backbones, the method improves Overall, Relevance, and Factuality over matched SFT baselines in all six backbone-track combinations, with average relative gains of 3.4%, 2.1%, and 5.8%, respectively. Ablations and paired diagnostics indicate that the structured rewards provide complementary signals, reducing image-neighborhood divergence and improving entity-assertion-relation consistency.

</details>


### [109] [Dynamic SpectraFormer for Ultra-High-Definition Underwater Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18662)
*Zhiqiang Hu,Tao Yu,Shouren Huang,Masatoshi Ishikawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Dynamic SpectraFormer，一种基于频域transformer的水下图像增强方法，通过超高清稀疏频谱注意力模块和动态频谱权重生成层，同时纠正低频（颜色/亮度）和高频（边缘/纹理）失真，在多个基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下图像因光的折射和吸收而遭受颜色失真、雾化和低可见度问题，影响AUV和海洋机器人的应用。传统方法主要专注于空间域，难以同时纠正低频（颜色、亮度）和高频（边缘、纹理）的混合失真。

Method: 提出Dynamic SpectraFormer，一个频域transformer。核心包括：(1) 超高清稀疏频谱注意力模块，在不失通用逼近能力的前提下捕获长程依赖；(2) 动态频谱权重生成层，作为自适应频谱带选择器，增强关键频带并抑制不相关频带。

Result: 大量消融研究和对比评估证明Dynamic SpectraFormer在多个水下图像增强基准上具有出色性能。

Conclusion: Dynamic SpectraFormer通过频域transformer同时处理高低频失真，显著提升了水下图像质量，为水下图像增强提供了一种有效的新方案。

Abstract: Underwater images suffer from color distortion, haze, and poor visibility due to light refraction and absorption in water. These challenges significantly impact the utilization of Autonomous Underwater Vehicles (AUVs) or marine robots. Typically, color and brightness distortions manifest at lower frequencies, while edge and texture distortions are prevalent at higher frequencies. Traditional methods struggle to concurrently rectify these mixed distortions as they primarily concentrate on the spatial domain. To address these issues, we introduce the Dynamic SpectraFormer, which enhances underwater images through a frequency domain transformer. The Dynamic SpectraFormer introduces an ultra-high-resolution sparse spectrum attention module, which could capture the long-term dependency without losing the universal approximating power. Additionally, we have developed a dynamic spectrum weight generation layer that serves as an adaptive spectrum band selector, accentuating critical frequency bands and suppressing less relevant ones. Consequently, this method significantly improves underwater image quality by addressing both high- and low-frequency distortions. Our extensive ablation studies and comparative evaluations consolidate the Dynamic SpectraFormer's efficacy across multiple underwater image enhancement benchmarks. The source code is available at https://github.com/arifence2024/DynamicSpectraFormer.git.

</details>


### [110] [Teeth2Point: A Two-Stage Dental CBCT ROI-to-Point Segmentation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18667)
*Qi Ma,Shipra Jain,Niko Benjamin Huber,Ender Konukoglu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Teeth2Point，一种基于点变换器（point transformer）的高效牙科CBCT语义分割框架，通过两阶段设计（卷积模型定位ROI + 自适应采样生成点token + 变换器分割）解决了传统密集patch方法在全局上下文与细粒度细节之间的权衡，并结合DINO风格的自监督预训练与领域特定增强策略，在异常病例（缺牙/错位牙）分割中显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牙科CBCT分割中，缺失牙或错位牙的精确标记是关键挑战。虽然Transformer理论上可利用全局解剖上下文解决此类歧义，但由于CBCT体积高分辨率及牙齿空间分布广泛，密集patch式体处理在计算开销限制下无法同时兼顾全局上下文和细粒度结构细节，存在固有取舍。

Method: Teeth2Point采用两阶段框架：(1) 用卷积模型定位牙齿周围的ROI区域；(2) 通过自适应采样将ROI转换为点token，供Transformer模型进行精确分割——点token能同时捕获全局上下文并保持高分辨率。Transformer先采用DINO风格SSL预训练（含随机token掩码和领域特定数据增强），再进行监督微调。

Result: 在四个数据集上，与最强两阶段基线相比，Teeth2Point在异常病例上的平均DSC提升1.44个百分点；相对第一阶段nnU-Net提升1.9个百分点。

Conclusion: Teeth2Point通过点表示与两阶段设计有效绕过了密集patch方法的上下文-分辨率权衡，结合SSL预训练增强了模型对复杂解剖变异的鲁棒性，在缺牙/错位牙的CBCT分割中取得了显著性能提升。

Abstract: Modern deep learning architectures have demonstrated strong performance in dental CBCT segmentation. One remaining crucial challenge is accurate tooth labeling in cases with missing or malpositioned teeth, which are highly relevant for dental practice. Transformer-based architectures should in theory be able to resolve such ambiguities using global anatomical context. However, due to the high resolution of CBCT volumes and the wide spatial distribution of teeth within volumes, dense patch-based volumetric processing faces an inherent trade-off. Computational costs limit the number of patches that can be used in self-attention and thus, one can either increase the extent of the context captured in self-attention or capture fine-grained structural details by using small patches, but not both. In this work, we present Teeth2Point, an efficient point-based transformer framework for dental CBCT semantic segmentation that can avoid this trade-off. Teeth2Point first localizes volumetric regions of interest (ROIs) surrounding teeth using a convolutional model, then converts ROIs into point tokens using adaptive sampling. A transformer model predicts accurate segmentations using the point tokens, which allow capturing global context while retaining high resolution. The transformer is first pretrained using self-supervised learning (SSL), in the style of DINO but using domain-specific augmentation strategies, followed by supervised finetuning. The SSL pretraining, which includes random token masking, provides robustness to complex anatomical variations. Compared with the strongest two-stage baseline, Teeth2Point improves abnormal-case performance by 1.44 DSC points on average across four datasets; relative to the first-stage nnU-Net, the gain is 1.9 points.

</details>


### [111] [Vision-Language Models for Egocentric Video: From Hand-Object Interaction to Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18671)
*Mohammad Zamani,Fatemeh Ziaeetabar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对用于自我中心视频理解的视觉语言模型（VLMs）进行了批判性综述，追溯了从传统识别架构到多模态基础模型及具身系统的演进过程，并围绕任务、数据集、手-物交互、时间推理等维度组织文献，指出当前模型在长期活动中的交互、动作和用户意图理解方面存在显著局限，提出了时间接地推理、交互感知监督、高效长视频处理等关键研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我中心视频为可穿戴智能、辅助系统、人机交互和具身AI提供了直接观察人类注意力、手-物交互和目标导向行为的视角，但其面临自我运动、遮挡、小目标、视角依赖外观和长时依赖等挑战。VLMs通过将视觉观察与语义知识和自然语言监督相结合，为解决这些挑战提供了有前景的基础，因此需要系统综述该领域的进展与不足。

Method: 该综述以任务、数据集、手-物交互理解、时间推理、帧与片段选择、多模态表征学习、提示、语义对齐和模型适应为组织框架，特别关注基于图和基于对象中心的推理机制，用以建模手、物体、动作与场景上下文在时间维度上的关系，并进一步考察第一人称感知与多模态基础模型如何支持可穿戴辅助、机器人技能学习、人-机器人迁移和具身决策。

Result: 综述揭示了当前文献中一个一致的局限：现有模型对可见物体的识别比对演化中的交互、动作和用户意图更可靠，尤其在长时活动场景下表现不足。作者据此识别出时间接地推理、交互感知监督、高效长视频处理、多模态融合、图增强表征、跨域泛化、隐私和可信评估作为可部署具身智能的关键优先方向。

Conclusion: 自我中心视频理解正从传统识别架构向多模态基础模型和具身系统演进，尽管VLMs为该领域奠定了坚实基础，但长时活动中交互层面与意图层面的理解仍是核心瓶颈，未来研究需优先攻克时间接地推理、交互感知监督和高效率长视频处理等方向，以推动可部署的具身智能落地。

Abstract: Egocentric video captures activities from the wearer's perspective, providing a direct view of human attention, hand--object interaction, and goal-directed behavior. This perspective is increasingly important for wearable intelligence, assistive systems, human--robot interaction, and embodied AI, yet it introduces challenges including ego-motion, occlusion, small active objects, viewpoint-dependent appearance, and long-range temporal dependencies. Vision--language models (VLMs) offer a promising foundation for addressing these challenges by linking visual observations with semantic knowledge and natural-language supervision. This survey presents a critical review of VLMs for egocentric video understanding, tracing the progression from conventional recognition architectures to multimodal foundation models and embodied systems. We organize the literature around tasks, datasets, hand--object interaction understanding, temporal reasoning, frame and clip selection, multimodal representation learning, prompting, semantic alignment, and model adaptation. Particular attention is given to graph-based and object-centric reasoning as mechanisms for modeling relations among hands, objects, actions, and scene context over time. We further examine how first-person perception and multimodal foundation models support wearable assistance, robot skill learning, human-to-robot transfer, and embodied decision making. Across the reviewed literature, a consistent limitation emerges: current models recognize visible objects more reliably than evolving interactions, actions, and user intent, especially over long activities. We therefore identify temporally grounded reasoning, interaction-aware supervision, efficient long-video processing, multimodal fusion, graph-enhanced representations, cross-domain generalization, privacy, and trustworthy evaluation as key priorities for deployable embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [112] [DynCur-Geo: Dynamic Curiosity Reward Shaping for Multimodal Active Geo-Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18673)
*Yiming Sun,Yang Zhang,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DynCur-Geo动态好奇心框架，通过距离感知门控自适应调整预测误差内在奖励，在低空无人机主动地理定位任务中平衡探索与目标收敛，超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有好奇心驱动方法在无人机主动地理定位中使用固定内在奖励权重，当智能体接近目标时仍会持续奖励新奇性，导致绕路和效率下降。多模态目标线索、受限视野和稀疏反馈使得探索与目标收敛难以平衡。

Method: 提出DynCur-Geo框架：1) 距离感知门控，根据剩余目标距离动态调整预测误差内在奖励的权重，早期鼓励探索、接近目标时转向目标导向行为；2) 基于势能的奖励塑形，提供密集的进度引导信号。

Result: 在多模态、跨场景、灾害影响及长距离设置等实验条件下，DynCur-Geo相较于主动地理定位基线方法均取得一致性能提升。

Conclusion: 动态调整好奇心奖励权重的策略能有效解决无人机主动搜索中探索与收敛的矛盾，距离感知门控结合势能奖励塑形是实现高效地理定位的有效方案。

Abstract: Active geo-localization enables low-altitude UAVs to search for specified targets from limited local aerial observations, supporting time-sensitive applications such as search and rescue and emergency inspection. However, multimodal target cues, restricted views, and sparse feedback make it difficult to balance exploration with target convergence. Existing curiosity-driven methods assign a fixed intrinsic-reward weight throughout search, which can continue rewarding novelty after the agent nears the target and induce detours. We propose DynCur-Geo, a dynamic curiosity framework that adjusts prediction-error intrinsic reward according to remaining target distance. A distance-aware gate encourages early exploration and shifts the policy toward goal-directed behavior near the target, while potential-based reward shaping supplies dense progress guidance. Experiments across multimodal, cross-scene, disaster-affected, and long-range settings show consistent gains over active geo-localization baselines.

</details>


### [113] [FRAGMENT: Factorized Graph Representations for Document Generation and Editing via Entity-Aware Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18679)
*Ayoub El Bouchtili,Guilhaume Leroy-Meline*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FRAGMENT是一个两阶段生成式框架，将文档表示为带类型的关联图，通过因子分解 p(structure) * p(content | structure) 建模结构化文档。第一阶段 Architect（因果掩码Transformer）生成图拓扑和空间关系，第二阶段 Builder（GATv2图注意力网络）生成边界框、文本和视觉样式属性。该框架提供可处理的文档级似然用于伪造检测，并通过提示条件扩展支持受控编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化文档（如发票、表格、报告、科学文章）的意义源于空间布局、文本内容和逻辑结构的交互。现有的像素级或token级生成模型难以有效捕捉这些依赖关系，因此需要一种能够显式建模文档结构-内容依赖关系的生成框架。

Method: FRAGMENT将文档表示为带类型的关联图，将分布因子化为 p(structure) * p(content | structure)。第一阶段Architect是因果掩码Transformer，以文档类别为条件，自回归生成图的拓扑结构和带类型的空间关系；第二阶段Builder基于GATv2图注意力网络，补充归一化边界框、文本和视觉样式属性。框架定义了显式似然模型，得到可处理的文档级似然作为伪造检测的异常分数。受控编辑通过提示条件扩展实现，将指令嵌入通过交叉注意力注入Builder。

Result: 在DocLayNet上训练，在FUNSD和SROIE上微调。实验在DocLayNet、FUNSD和SROIE上与代表性的自回归、仅布局和基于图的基线比较，提供了对因子化图生成框架特征与权衡的实证分析。

Conclusion: FRAGMENT通过因子化的图生成方式为结构化文档建模提供了一种替代方案，兼顾了显式似然可计算性（用于伪造检测）和可控编辑能力，并在多种文档数据集上展示了与其他基线相比的优劣权衡。

Abstract: Structured documents such as invoices, forms, reports, and scientific articles derive meaning from the interplay between spatial layout, textual content, and logical structure. Generative models operating at the pixel or token level often struggle to capture these dependencies effectively. We explore FRAGMENT, a generative framework that represents a document as a typed relational graph and factorizes its distribution as p(structure, content) = p(structure) * p(content | structure). The framework consists of two stages. The first stage, the Architect, is a causally masked Transformer conditioned on document category that autoregressively generates the graph topology and typed spatial relations. The second stage, the Builder, is a GATv2-based graph attention network that enriches the graph with normalized bounding boxes, text, and visual style attributes. Both stages define explicit likelihood models, yielding a tractable document-level likelihood that serves as an anomaly score for forgery detection. For controlled editing, a prompt-conditioned extension injects instruction embeddings into the Builder through cross-attention, enabling semantic and entity-aware modifications. We describe training on DocLayNet and fine-tuning on FUNSD and SROIE. Experiments on DocLayNet, FUNSD, and SROIE evaluate FRAGMENT alongside representative autoregressive, layout-only, and graph-based baselines, providing an empirical analysis of the characteristics and trade-offs of the proposed factorized graph generation framework.

</details>


### [114] [DocClaw: A Unified Agentic System for Intelligent Document Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18685)
*Siqi Xiang,Zhipeng Xu,Yufei Liu,Junhao Ji,Qing Liu,Zulong Chen,Zhibo Yang,Chunyan Miao,Shijian Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DocClaw是一个统一的智能体系统，将多种智能文档处理任务（OCR、DocQA、KIE）形式化为智能体与文档之间的交互过程，通过结构化文档状态组织可复用的文档知识与任务相关的交互上下文，在单一框架内实现多种任务的处理，并在多个基准上取得与通用VLM和任务专用方法相竞争的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能文档处理任务虽然目标不同，但共享感知文档内容、获取任务相关信息、逐步精化中间结果的共同需求。然而，现有方法通常将不同任务视为独立的预测问题，由特定任务的模型或流水线分别处理，缺乏统一的框架，导致资源浪费且难以跨任务复用能力。

Method: DocClaw将多种文档处理任务统一为智能体与文档之间的交互过程。给定文档和任务特定查询，智能体根据相应的文档技能迭代地识别所需信息、调用相关工具、并将观察结果整合为期望输出。在整个过程中，一个结构化的文档状态组织可复用的文档知识和任务特定的交互上下文，使智能体能够累积、回访并逐步精化信息。任务特定需求由智能体对查询目标和对应文档技能的解释来捕获，而底层的交互循环、工具空间和文档状态在所有任务间共享。

Result: 在多个智能文档处理基准上的大量实验表明，DocClaw能够在单一智能体框架内有效处理多种任务，并且相比通用视觉语言模型（VLM）和任务专用方法均取得了具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: DocClaw验证了将多样化的智能文档处理任务统一为智能体-文档交互范式的可行性与有效性，表明通过共享交互循环、工具空间和文档状态，单一框架可以胜任多种文档处理任务，同时保持与专用方法相当的性能水平。

Abstract: Intelligent document processing (IDP) encompasses a broad range of tasks, including optical character recognition (OCR), document question answering (DocQA), and key information extraction (KIE). Despite their distinct objectives, these tasks share a common need to perceive document content, acquire task-relevant information, and progressively refine intermediate results. However, they are typically formulated as separate prediction problems and addressed by task-specific models or processing pipelines. We introduce DocClaw, a unified agentic system that formulates diverse intelligent document processing tasks as a shared process of interaction between an agent and a document. Given a document and a task-specific query, DocClaw follows an appropriate document skill to iteratively identify the information required, invoke relevant tools, and integrate the resulting observations into the desired output. Throughout this process, a structured document state organizes reusable document knowledge and task-specific interaction context, allowing the agent to accumulate, revisit, and progressively refine information as the interaction proceeds. Under this formulation, task-specific requirements are captured by the agent's interpretation of the query objective and the corresponding document skill, while the underlying interaction loop, tool space, and document state are shared across tasks. Extensive experiments across multiple intelligent document processing benchmarks demonstrate that DocClaw effectively handles diverse tasks within a single agentic framework and achieves competitive performance compared with both general-purpose VLMs and task-specific methods.

</details>


### [115] [Composed Historical Image Retrieval by Modeling Temporal Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18694)
*Adrià Molina Rodríguez,Oriol Ramos Terrades,Josep Lladós Canet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Temporally Decomposable Image Representations (TDIR)，一种将历史照片嵌入分解为独立日期和内容正交子空间的方法，使时间信息可提取并注入其他图像，无需标签监督即可实现组合图像检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络嵌入空间是多维且难以解释的，虽然理论上可将嵌入压缩到单一时间维度，但这会牺牲下游任务性能。作者希望在不牺牲检索性能的前提下，学习既保留时间结构又具有足够表达能力的表征。

Method: 提出TDIR算法，通过正交子空间将历史照片的嵌入分解为日期和内容两个独立成分；定义并证明可分解条件并刻画部分满足条件时的误差；利用联合优化自然产生时间子空间与类别子空间的正交性；支持跨图像的迁移操作（提取一张图的时间信息注入另一张图）。

Result: 在历史照片的组合图像检索（Composed Image Retrieval）真实场景中验证了所有理论命题，该方法在日期估计和对象检索任务上均保持竞争性性能，同时提供可解释的嵌入几何结构。

Conclusion: TDIR证明可以构建同时具备时间可分解性和检索能力的表征空间，正交性可自发涌现而非需显式约束，为历史照片档案的可解释导航提供了理论与实践基础。

Abstract: While time evolves linearly, the geometry of neural embedding spaces is inherently multi-dimensional, often chaotic, and difficult to interpret. In principle, one could constrain an embedding space to a single temporal dimension; however, such a reduction would sacrifice performance on downstream tasks, as one-dimensional embeddings cannot retain sufficient expressive capacity. This paper asks whether it is possible to learn representations that preserve temporal structure while remaining effective for image and object retrieval, and answers this question by building the mathematical foundations of such a system.
