We propose Temporally Decomposable Image Representations (TDIR), a representation learning algorithm that decomposes historical photographs into separate date and content components through orthogonal subspaces. We define and prove the conditions under which such a decomposition is achievable, characterize the error incurred when those conditions are only partially met, and show that orthogonality between temporal and categorical subspaces emerges naturally from the joint optimization, without requiring it to be imposed explicitly. Beyond its geometric properties, TDIR enables a class of transitive operations on embedding spaces: the temporal information of one image can be extracted and injected into the representation of another, with no label supervision required. All theoretical properties are grounded and validated in the real-world problem of Composed Image Retrieval on historical photographs, where a query simultaneously specifies object content and a target time period, either through labels or through example images. This in-the-wild setting serves as a concrete backing for the propositions we derive, offering an intuitive and interpretable way to navigate photographic archives while maintaining competitive performance in both date estimation and object retrieval.

</details>


### [116] [Impact of Iterative Fine-Tuning on Transcription Accuracy in Complex Historical Sanskrit Manuscripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18696)
*Kartik Chincholikar,Kaushik Gopalan,Mihir Hasabnis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可迭代微调的本地传统OCR流水线，用于数字化具有复杂异构版式的手写历史手稿，并在三份复杂梵语手稿上引入带细粒度版式及Unicode标注的PAGE-XML数据集，同时对比了多模态大语言模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史手稿数字化对可访问性、保存和历史研究至关重要，但历史手稿常因时代特异的书写风格、页面纹理、相机噪声等因素呈现复杂异构版式和非标准外观，使其难以进行OCR。而人工标注成本高昂且需要历史领域专业知识。

Method: 提出一个本地传统OCR流水线，可在版式层面和外观层面针对目标手稿进行迭代微调，通过适应目标手稿分布来改善后续页面的预测，从而迭代减少人工标注工作量。

Result: 在三个复杂历史梵语手稿上数字化文本，发布了带细粒度版式注释和PAGE-XML格式Unicode注释的数据集，展示了迭代微调带来的量化收益，并基准测试了主流多模态大语言模型在该数据集上的表现。

Conclusion: 所提出的传统OCR流水线通过迭代微调能有效适应目标手稿分布，提升识别性能并降低人工标注成本；该工作同时为历史手稿OCR研究提供了带精细标注的基准数据集。

Abstract: Digitizing the text from handwritten historical manuscripts is required to make them easily accessible, preservable, and to enable historical scholars to study them in new ways. Historical manuscripts, however, often exhibit complex heterogeneous layouts and non-standard appearance due to period-specific writing styles, page textures, camera noise, and other nuisance factors, making them difficult to perform OCR on. To tackle this challenge, we introduce a local traditional OCR pipeline, which can be iteratively fine-tuned on the target manuscript at the layout-level and the appearance-level. By adapting to the target manuscript distribution, the proposed Traditional OCR pipeline makes better predictions on subsequent pages, causing iterative reduction in human annotation effort, which is expensive and time-consuming as it requires historical domain expertise. Using this pipeline, we digitize text from three complex historical Sanskrit manuscripts and introduce a dataset with granular layout-level annotations, along with Unicode annotations in the standard PAGE-XML format. We demonstrate quantitative gains due to iterative fine-tuning of the proposed traditional OCR pipeline, and also benchmark the performance of leading Multi-Modal Large Language Models on the introduced Dataset. Code and dataset are available at: https://github.com/flame-cai/gnn-synthetic-layout-historical/.

</details>


### [117] [A Critical Synthesis of Uncertainty Quantification and Foundation Models for Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18709)
*Steven Landgraf,Joceline Hinz,Markus Ulrich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估了将不确定性量化（UQ）方法集成到语义分割基础模型中的效果。作者在预训练的SAM2编码器上微调轻量级DPT解码器作为基线，并基准测试了四种代表性UQ方法（MC Dropout、Deep Sub-Ensemble、Test-Time Augmentation和Evidential Deep Learning），在Cityscapes、NYUv2及两个域外设置下对比了分割精度、校准质量、不确定性和推理时间，揭示了预测性能、可靠性与计算成本之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在精度和跨域泛化上取得了突破，但缺乏可解释性、过度自信以及对真实世界域偏移的敏感性使其难以应用于安全关键场景。不确定性量化（UQ）是解决这些问题的原则性方法，但尚未被系统集成到分割基础模型中，需要探索其可行性与效果。

Method: 在预训练的SAM2编码器之上微调轻量级DPT解码器，构建简单且具有竞争力的分割基线；随后集成四种代表性UQ方法：Monte Carlo Dropout、Deep Sub-Ensemble、Test-Time Augmentation和Evidential Deep Learning。在Cityscapes、NYUv2和两个具有挑战性的域外设置上，对分割精度、校准性能、不确定性质量和推理时间进行系统对比评估。

Result: 实验揭示了预测性能、可靠性和计算成本之间明确的权衡关系。不同UQ方法在分割精度、校准质量、不确定性质量和推理时间上展现出各异的优劣势，证明了不确定性感知基础模型既展现了潜力，也暴露出当前局限性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，尽管不确定性感知分割基础模型具有前景，但当前仍存在局限性。未来工作需要联合优化精度、鲁棒性和效率，以支持实际部署，特别是安全关键和任务关键应用场景。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly breaking what seemed to be impossible not long ago by enabling unprecedented accuracy and cross-domain generalization. Yet their lack of interpretability, tendency to be overconfident, and sensitivity to real-world domain shifts pose critical challenges for safety- and mission-critical applications. Uncertainty quantification (UQ) offers a principled way to address these issues, but its integration into segmentation foundation models has yet to be explored. In this paper we present the first systematic evaluation of UQ methods applied to a foundation model for semantic segmentation. We fine-tune a lightweight DPT decoder on top of the pretrained SAM2 encoder to establish a simple yet competitive baseline and benchmark four representative UQ approaches - Monte Carlo Dropout, Deep Sub-Ensemble, Test-Time Augmentation, and Evidential Deep Learning - across Cityscapes, NYUv2, and two challenging out-of-domain settings. Our analysis compares segmentation accuracy, calibration, uncertainty quality, and inference time, revealing clear trade-offs between predictive performance, reliability, and computational cost. These results highlight both the promise and the current limitations of uncertainty-aware foundation models, pointing to the need for future work that jointly optimizes accuracy, robustness, and efficiency for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [118] [CamWorldQA: Perceptual Quality Assessment of Camera-Controlled World Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18710)
*Yunhe Li,Likun Wu,Sijing Wu,Xinyu Tian,Huiyu Duan,Yixuan Gao,Yunhao Li,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了CamWorldQA，这是首个面向相机控制的世界视频生成的感知质量评估基准，包含720个由6种生成方法、20个源视频和6种相机轨迹生成的视频，并带有主观实验标注的质量分数。同时提出了CWQA无参考质量评估网络，通过空间、时间运动和光流三个互补分支联合预测质量分数，实验证明其性能优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频质量评估（VQA）方法主要针对自然视频开发，无法捕捉相机控制生成视频的独特感知特性（如视点一致性、运动连贯性和内容保持）。随着生成式视频模型的快速发展，需要一个专门针对相机控制生成的视频质量评估基准和方法。

Method: 构建了CamWorldQA基准数据集（720个视频、6种生成方法、20个源视频、6种相机轨迹，通过主观实验标注质量分数），并提出了CWQA——一种无参考质量评估网络，包含三个互补分支：空间特征提取分支、时间运动特征提取分支和光流特征提取分支，三者联合预测质量分数。

Result: 在CamWorldQA数据集上的大量实验表明，CWQA的评估性能优于现有的视频质量评估方法。

Conclusion: 本文填补了相机控制世界视频生成这一新兴领域的感知质量评估空白，通过构建专门的数据集和设计多分支融合的网络架构，有效地解决了现有VQA方法在该场景下的不足。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative video models have enabled camera-controlled world video generation, allowing models to synthesize videos under user-defined camera trajectories. However, existing video quality assessment (VQA) methods are mainly developed for natural videos and fail to capture the unique perceptual characteristics of camera-controlled generation, such as viewpoint consistency, motion coherence, and content preservation. In this work, we introduce CamWorldQA, the first benchmark for perceptual quality assessment of camera-controlled world video generation. CamWorldQA contains 720 generated videos produced by 6 representative generation methods from 20 diverse source videos under 6 camera trajectories, where each video is annotated with a human-rated perceptual quality score through subjective experiments. Furthermore, we propose CWQA, a no-reference quality assessment network with three complementary branches that extract spatial features, temporal motion features and optical flow features to jointly predict quality scores. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CWQA achieves superior performance over existing quality assessment methods on the CamWorldQA dataset.

</details>


### [119] [EgoHRV: Continuous Heart Rate Variability Estimation from Egocentric Systems for Autonomic Response and Skill Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18711)
*Berken Utku Demirel,Christian Holz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EgoHRV方法，首次从第一视角（egocentric）头戴设备的凝视摄像头视频中估计心率变异性（HRV）和心率（HR）。通过3D主干网络结合新型低-高分解模块提取血容量脉冲（BVP）信号，并利用跨域预训练对齐接触式传感器与摄像头信号在频域上的表示，实现了高时间精度的HRV恢复。在EgoExo4D数据集上，将其HRV估计与置信度整合到能力评估器中，准确率提升17.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的第一视角视觉系统只能捕捉可见行为线索，忽略了如压力、投入度和注意力等自主神经状态的生理指标。HRV作为广泛使用的无创自主神经调节标志物，反映了心跳间细微的时间差异，但此前因第一视角平台中的运动和凝视视频噪声掩盖了这种细粒度时序，导致HRV无法从第一视角估计。作者旨在填补这一空白，使第一视角系统具备生理层面感知能力。

Method: EgoHRV流程包括：(1) 3D骨干网络提取凝视视频的时空特征；(2) 新颖的低-高分解模块从凝视视频中提取血容量脉冲（BVP）信号，分离低频与高频成分以保留精细时序；(3) 跨域预训练策略，对齐接触式（如PPG/ECG）传感器信号与摄像头导出信号的频域表征，赋予模型时间精度以恢复HRV中极细微的心跳间隔波动；同时采用不确定性感知设计以提升下游行为建模的鲁棒性。

Result: EgoHRV在从第一视角视频进行HR和HRV估计的任务中达到了最先进（state-of-the-art）的准确率。将HRV估计和置信度指标整合到EgoExo4D的能力（proficiency）评估器中，使估计准确率提升17.8%。

Conclusion: EgoHRV首次证明可以从第一视角头戴设备的凝视摄像头中可靠估计HRV，解决了运动中细粒度心脏信号被噪声掩盖的关键难题。除技能评估外，连续的HRV估计还为第一视角系统打开了压力感知和唤醒度感知等应用方向，扩展了egocentric视觉的生理感知维度。

Abstract: Egocentric vision systems capture human behavior from visible cues, but overlook physiological indicators of autonomic states such as stress, engagement, and attention. Heart rate variability (HRV) is a widely used noninvasive marker of autonomic regulation under stress. HRV reflects small timing differences between successive heartbeats and has so far been out of reach for egocentric platforms, where motion and noise in gaze video mask exactly this fine-grained timing. We propose EgoHRV, a method that estimates HRV as well as heart rate (HR) from the gaze cameras that are already integrated into egocentric headsets. Our pipeline combines a 3D backbone with a novel low--high decomposition module that extracts the blood volume pulse (BVP) signal from gaze video. Our cross-domain pretraining aligns the frequency-domain representations of contact-based and camera-derived signals. This alignment gives EgoHRV the temporal precision to recover HRV from the subtle fluctuations in gaze video. EgoHRV achieves state-of-the-art accuracy for HR and HRV estimation from egocentric video, and its uncertainty-aware design improves downstream behavioral modeling. Integrating our HRV estimates and confidence measures into EgoExo4D's proficiency estimator raises accuracy by 17.8%. Beyond skill, continuous HRV estimation also opens egocentric systems to stress- and arousal-aware estimation tasks. Code: https://github.com/eth-siplab/EgoHRV

</details>


### [120] [The Impact of CutMix on Reliability and Robustness in Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18715)
*Steven Landgraf,Markus Ulrich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了CutMix数据增强策略对语义分割模型可靠性（校准和不确定性质量）的影响，发现CutMix虽对分割精度影响较小，但能显著提升模型的校准可靠性和不确定性估计质量，尤其在分布偏移场景下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自动驾驶等安全关键应用中，语义分割模型不仅要准确，还需要可靠和稳健的预测。CutMix作为广泛使用的数据增强策略，其在语义分割任务中的可靠性影响尚未被探索。此外，半监督分割方法（以CutMix为核心组件）被发现会严重损害可靠性，因此有必要系统地隔离并分析CutMix本身的作用。

Method: 隔离CutMix变量，在两种代表性架构（CNN-based的DeepLabV3+和Transformer-based的SegFormer）上进行系统评估，分别测试域内和域外场景，比较有无CutMix时在分割精度、校准性能（calibration）和不确定性质量方面的差异。

Result: 实验结果表明，CutMix对分割精度的影响较小，但能持续改善模型可靠性，尤其是在分布偏移情况下。这些改善主要体现在模型的校准和不确定性估计的信任度提升上，而非原始分割预测本身。

Conclusion: CutMix主要增强的是模型的校准和不确定性的可信度，而非原始的像素级分割精度。这一区别对安全关键的部署至关重要，因为在这些场景中，可靠的置信度估计与原始性能同等重要。

Abstract: Ensuring not only high accuracy but also reliable and robust predictions is critical for the deployment of semantic segmentation models in safety-critical applications such as autonomous driving. Despite the widespread use of CutMix - a simple yet powerful data augmentation strategy - its effect on the reliability and robustness in dense predictions tasks remains unexplored. Motivated by recent findings that semi-supervised segmentation methods, where CutMix is a core component, can severely degrade reliability, this study isolates and systematically analyzes the influence of CutMix on segmentation accuracy, calibration, and uncertainty quality. We evaluate two representative architectures, the CNN-based DeepLabV3+ and the transformer-based SegFormer, across both in-domain and out-of-domain scenarios. Our results show that CutMix has only a minor impact on segmentation accuracy but consistently improves the reliability, particularly under distribution shifts. These improvements indicate that CutMix primarily enhances the trustworthiness of the model's calibration and uncertainty rather than the raw segmentation prediction itself. This distinction is crucial for safety-critical deployment, where reliable confidence estimates are as important as raw performance.

</details>


### [121] [A Few Cases Are All You Need: An Empirical Study of Annotation-Efficient LoRA Fine-Tuning of MedSAM3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18731)
*Sachin Dudda Nagaraju,Bendik Skarre Abrahamsen,Ashkan Moradi,Mattijs Elschot*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过低秩适配(LoRA)仅用10个标注病例微调MedSAM3，即可在CT和MRI上实现与专科分割工具（如TotalSegmentator和MRSegmentator）相当的五种腹部器官分割性能，训练时间仅需3-5小时/器官，比nnU-Net快2-3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专科分割工具（如TotalSegmentator、MRSegmentator）表现优异但需要大量标注数据训练；医学基础模型通过大规模预训练可减少标注负担，但零样本性能有限。关键问题是：究竟需要多少专家标注病例才能达到临床可用的分割性能？

Method: 使用LoRA对MedSAM3进行参数高效微调，在CT和MRI上针对五个腹部器官（肝脏、肾脏、脾脏、胆囊、胰腺），仅使用1、2、5、10个标注病例进行训练，并在AMOS22数据集上评估。此外，在Whole Heart Segmentation数据集上进行外部验证以测试心臟分割的泛化能力。

Result: 仅用10个病例训练即可实现与专科系统竞争的性能：胆囊分割Dice达到CT 0.68、MRI 0.59（而现有工具几乎完全失败，Dice仅0.0004）；肝脏、肾脏、脾脏与MRSegmentator差距在5-10%以内，但标注量减少100倍以上；外部验证显示扩展到心臟分割（左心室）也仅需10个病例即可达到竞争性能；训练时间3-5小时/器官，比nnU-Net快2-3倍。

Conclusion: 十个标注病例足以实现临床有用的分割性能，可有效缓解图像标注和训练时间两大瓶颈，为医学图像分割提供了一种高效、低标注成本的新范式。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation is essential for clinical workflows such as treatment planning and disease assessment. While specialist tools like TotalSegmentator and MRSegmentator achieve strong performance, they require large annotated datasets for training. Medical foundation models offer a promising alternative through large-scale pretraining that reduces the annotation burden for new tasks, but zero-shot performance remains limited. Parameter-efficient adaptation via Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) enables efficient specialization with few trainable parameters, but a key question remains: how many expert-annotated cases are needed to achieve clinically useful segmentation performance? We address this by adapting MedSAM3 with LoRA for five abdominal organs (liver, kidneys, spleen, gallbladder, and pancreas) in CT and MRI using only 1, 2, 5, and 10 annotated cases, evaluating on AMOS22 dataset. With just 10 cases, models achieve performance competitive with specialist systems trained on orders of magnitude more data. Notably, this includes reliable gallbladder segmentation (Dice 0.68 CT, 0.59 MRI) where existing tools fail almost completely (Dice 0.0004), while remaining within 5--10% of MRSegmentator for liver, kidneys, and spleen using over 100 times fewer annotations. Furthermore, external validation on the Whole Heart Segmentation dataset shows that the approach extends to cardiac segmentation, a use case beyond the scope of TotalSegmentator (MRI) and MRSegmentator, achieving competitive left ventricle (LV) performance with only 10 annotated cases. Training requires only3--5,hours per organ on a single GPU, approximately 2--3 times faster than nnU-Net. These findings suggest that ten annotated cases are sufficient for clinically useful segmentation, effectively reducing bottlenecks for both image annotation and training time.

</details>


### [122] [CL4D: Contrastive Language-4D Pretraining for Vision-Language Reasoning in Dynamic Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18734)
*Kumal Hewagamage,Isuranga Senavirathne,Sasika Amarasinghe,Hasitha Gallella,Dulanga Weerakoon,Vigneshwaran Subbaraju,Ranga Rodrigo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CL4D，第一个直接处理动态点云的4D基础视觉编码器，通过对比学习对齐时空几何表示与自然语言描述，支持零样本运动-文本双向检索；并在此基础上构建4DVLM，首个无需2D图像/视频或静态3D点云、直接基于4D点云进行语言生成的4D视觉语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉编码器要么限于静态2D图像或3D点云缺乏时间建模，要么限于2D视频缺乏精确的几何深度推理，无法同时捕捉动态场景中的空间结构与运动演化。为此需要一种能够直接处理动态4D点云的基础编码器，联合建模时空几何与语言语义。

Method: 1) CL4D：基于对比学习目标，将4D时空几何表示与自然语言描述对齐到共享嵌入空间；2) 4DVLM：在CL4D编码器基础上构建，直接以4D点云条件化语言生成；3) 构建新数据集DynAction4D，涵盖多样人体动作、物体交互和场景环境，用于训练CL4D及4DVLM。

Result: 在多个4D人体动作基准上，CL4D达到最先进性能，相较先前方法提升约16.75%；4DVLM在相同场景下，即使只输入4D点云、而对比模型输入对应的RGB视频序列，仍优于Gemini和GPT-5等前沿视频VLM。

Conclusion: CL4D和4DVLM证明了直接基于动态点云的4D基础视觉编码及视觉语言建模的有效性，为具身智能体在动态物理环境中的4D理解与推理提供了新的基础范式。

Abstract: 4D understanding and reasoning is a fundamental capability for embodied AI agents operating in dynamic physical environments. However, existing vision encoders are largely limited to static 2D images or 3D point clouds without temporal modeling, or to 2D videos that lack accurate geometric depth reasoning. Consequently, current approaches fail to jointly capture spatial structure and motion evolution in dynamic scenes. We present CL4D, the first foundational 4D vision encoder that directly operates on dynamic point clouds, trained with a contrastive learning objective to align spatio-temporal geometric representations with natural language descriptions. By learning a shared embedding space between text and 4D scene dynamics, CL4D enables zero-shot motion-to-text and text-to-motion retrieval in dynamic environments and serves as a foundational 4D vision encoder for downstream 4D vision-language tasks. Building on this encoder, we introduce 4DVLM, a 4D vision-language model that conditions language generation on dynamic geometric representations. 4DVLM is the first VLM designed to operate directly on 4D point clouds without relying on 2D images, 2D videos, or static 3D point clouds. We train CL4D and subsequently 4DVLM on a newly constructed dataset termed DynAction4D capturing diverse human motions across varying object interactions and scene environments. Extensive experiments across multiple 4D human action benchmarks demonstrate that CL4D achieves state-of-the-art performance, with improvements of approximately ~16.75% over prior methods. Furthermore, 4DVLM outperforms frontier video VLMs such as Gemini and GPT-5 even when these models are provided with RGB video sequences corresponding to the same scenes represented as 4D point clouds for 4DVLM.

</details>


### [123] [SED-FOD: Scattering-Aware Expert Decomposition for Few-Shot Cross-Sensor SAR Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18755)
*Shu Yang,Zhen Chen,Zhiyu Jiang,Yanlei Li,Xingdong Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种散射感知的共享-特定特征分解框架（Scattering-Aware Shared-Specific Feature Decomposition）用于小样本SAR域适应目标检测。该框架将检测特征分解为共享路径和多个软门控散射特定专家路径，共享路径学习可迁移的目标结构信息并用于非对称域对齐，而散射特定专家自适应补偿异构SAR响应。实验在FARAD-X/FARAD-Ka和MiniSAR之间的四个双向异构SAR检测任务上验证了所提方法的优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAR目标检测在跨域场景（不同频带、分辨率、背景杂波和目标散射响应）下性能会显著退化。现有域适应方法大多追求域不变特征对齐，抑制了对目标检测有用的传感器依赖散射特性。尤其在少样本场景下，仅有极少量带标注的目标域SAR图像可用，这一问题更具挑战性。

Method: 提出散射感知的共享-特定特征分解框架：(1) 将检测特征分解为共享路径和多个软门控散射特定专家路径；(2) 共享路径学习可迁移的目标结构信息，用于非对称域对齐；(3) 散射特定专家自适应补偿异构SAR响应；(4) 引入路由域辅助损失鼓励特定专家捕获传感器依赖的路由偏好；(5) 引入专家平衡损失防止路由崩塌。

Result: 在FARAD-X/FARAD-Ka和MiniSAR之间的四个双向异构SAR检测任务上，在不同小样本设置下进行了大量实验，结果表明该方法在前向和反向适应方向均取得了优越性能。

Conclusion: 提出的散射感知共享-特定特征分解框架有效解决了小样本SAR域适应目标检测问题。通过区分性地利用域不变的结构信息与域特定的散射信息，避免了传统域适应方法因过度对齐而损失有用散射特征的问题，在双向适应场景下均表现优异。

Abstract: Synthetic aperture radar (SAR) object detection is an important part of remote sensing interpretation. However, because of variations in frequency band, resolution, background clutter, and target scattering responses, the performance of existing detectors often degrades when training and testing data are acquired from different SAR domains. Although domain adaptation methods offer a promising paradigm for solving this problem, most of them mainly pursue domain-invariant feature alignment and suppress sensor-dependent scattering characteristics that are useful for object detection. This problem becomes more challenging in few-shot scenarios, where only a few fully annotated target-domain SAR images are available. To address this issue, we propose a scattering-aware shared-specific feature decomposition framework for few-shot SAR domain adaptation object detection. We decompose detection features into a shared path and several soft-gated scattering-specific expert paths. The shared path learns transferable object structural information and is used for asymmetric domain alignment, while the scattering-specific experts adaptively compensate heterogeneous SAR responses. In addition, routing-domain auxiliary loss is introduced to encourage specific experts to capture sensor-dependent routing preferences, and an expert balancing loss is used to prevent routing collapse. Extensive experiments on four bidirectional heterogeneous SAR detection tasks between FARAD-X/FARAD-Ka and MiniSAR under different few-shot settings have been conducted and experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves superior performance in both forward and reverse adaptation directions.

</details>


### [124] [MIFR: A Modality-Invariant and Fair Representation Framework for Skin Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18774)
*Asonyu Senge Njih,Yvan Guifo Fodjo,Vianney Kengne Tchendji,Jerry Lacmou Zeutouo,Kerol Djoumessi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模态不变公平表示框架（MIFR），用于皮肤病分类，通过配对临床照片与皮肤镜图像，结合ViT编码器和五分量多目标损失函数，同时解决单模态诊断局限和跨肤色性能差异问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮肤病是全球重大公共卫生负担，但现有机器学习诊断工具存在两个关键局限：仅依赖单一模态进行诊断，以及在不同肤色间存在系统性性能差异。已有方法分别处理这两个挑战，尚缺乏统一解决方案。

Method: MIFR框架采用ViT编码器对临床照片和皮肤镜图像进行配对处理，通过模态特定的投影头将输入映射到高维嵌入空间。训练使用五分量多目标损失：加权交叉熵用于分类、混淆损失和肤色分类损失用于公平性、逐模态监督对比损失用于类别对齐、模态不变损失用于临床与皮肤镜模态对齐。

Result: 在HIBA+Derm7pt配对数据集及外部PAD-UFES-20和ISIC 2019数据集上的实验表明，模态不变表示学习相比相关基线模型具有竞争力的预测性能和内部数据集上的公平性。t-SNE可视化证实同一疾病的临床与皮肤镜嵌入在几何上对齐，验证了联合目标的有效性。

Conclusion: 模态不变表示学习能够同时兼顾预测性能与跨肤色公平性，为皮肤病分类提供了一种多模态融合的公平诊断方案。

Abstract: Skin diseases represent a major global public health burden, yet machine learning tools developed to assist in their diagnosis suffer from two critical limitations: reliance on only one modality for diagnosis and systematic performance disparities across skin tones. While existing approaches address each challenge separately, this work proposes a modality-invariant framework with fair representation (MIFR) for skin disease classification. The architecture pairs clinical photographs with dermoscopic images using ViT-based encoders, projecting each input into a high-dimensional embedding space via modality-specific projection heads. The resulting model is trained with a five-component multi-objective loss including weighted cross-entropy for classification, confusion and skin-type classification losses for fairness, per-modality supervised contrastive loss for class alignment, and a modality-invariance loss for clinical and dermoscopic modality alignment. Experiments on the HIBA+Derm7pt paired dataset and the external PAD-UFES-20 and ISIC 2019 datasets showed that modality-invariant representation learning provides competitive predictive performance compare to relevant baseline models and competitive fairness on the internal dataset. t-SNE visualizations confirmed that clinical and dermoscopic embeddings of the same disease are geometrically aligned, validating the joint objectives.

</details>


### [125] [EfficientSync: Real-Time Lip Synchronization via Deformation-Based Reference Texture Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18832)
*Fa-Ting Hong,Runzhen Liu,Luchuan Song,Hongmin Cai,Chuhua Xian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EfficientSync提出一种基于形变的实时口型同步框架，通过动态纹理混合器、时空移位自适应掩码和STAR采样三个模块，在166 FPS下实现SOTA的视觉质量和身份保持，避免GAN/扩散解码器重新生成口内细节带来的纹理幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统音频驱动口型同步方法用GAN或扩散解码器重建整个下半脸，不仅延迟高，且会幻觉生成牙齿、唇纹等口内细节，破坏真实纹理。作者认为身份保持的瓶颈并非参考帧不足，而是缺乏一种能从参考帧忠实传递真实纹理的机制。

Method: 提出三个核心组件：(1) 动态纹理混合器(Dynamic Texture Mixer)，将多参考帧融合重构为通道级选择问题，在全局上下文中评估每个空间对齐的参考帧并通过通道加权求和聚合，低开销保持纹理完整性；(2) 时空移位自适应掩码(Spatio-Temporal Shifted Adaptive Masking)，将源帧分解为唇部生成条件和独立背景先验，抑制下半脸泄漏并将合成嘴部无缝融合到背景；(3) STAR采样(STAR Sampling)，一种零开销预处理步骤，检索最清晰且拓扑多样性最高的参考帧。

Result: 在HDTF和VFHQ数据集上，EfficientSync实现了SOTA的视觉质量和身份保持效果，在单GPU上达到166 FPS的实时推理速度。

Conclusion: 该工作证明口型同步这类局部编辑任务无需重建整个下半脸，通过形变保留参考纹理而非重新合成，能在保持身份真实性的同时大幅提升推理效率，为实时口型同步提供了高效且高保真的新范式。

Abstract: Audio-driven lip synchronization manipulates the mouth region of a talking-face video to match the driving audio while preserving head pose, identity, and background. Although the task is inherently local editing, prevailing approaches reconstruct the entire lower face with heavy GAN- or diffusion-based decoders, incurring substantial latency and, more critically, hallucinating intra-oral details such as teeth and lip wrinkles instead of preserving authentic textures. We contend that the bottleneck in identity preservation is not the scarcity of reference frames, but the lack of a mechanism that faithfully transfers the genuine textures they already contain. We therefore present EfficientSync, a real-time deformation-based framework that retains reference textures rather than resynthesizing them. First, the Dynamic Texture Mixer reformulates multi-reference fusion as channel-wise selection, evaluating each spatially aligned reference in a global context and aggregating them by channel-wise weighted summation, preserving textural integrity at low cost. Second, Spatio-Temporal Shifted Adaptive Masking decomposes the source frame into lip-generation conditions and an independent background prior, suppressing lower-face leakage while blending the synthesized mouth seamlessly into the background. Third, STAR Sampling, a zero-overhead pre-processing step, retrieves the sharpest and most topologically diverse reference frames. Experiments on HDTF and VFHQ show state-of-the-art visual quality and identity preservation at 166 FPS on a single GPU. Video demos: https://alunaticat.github.io/EfficientSync/index.html.

</details>


### [126] [EVADE: Evidence-Verified Agentic Diagnosis with Escape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18833)
*Mohaimenul Azam Khan Raiaan,Nur Mohammad Fahad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EVADE是一种推理型、无需训练的方法，通过在不同图像视角间验证门控一致性来增强冻结医学视觉语言模型（VLM）的安全性和可靠性。当模型不确定时，它定位最具有诊断相关性的区域，在放大视图上重新作答，仅当整图和放大视图的回答一致时才承诺答案，否则弃权。实验表明该方法同时改善校准和选择性风险，ECE最多降低45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉语言模型虽能达到高准确率但不可靠：它们系统性过度自信，几乎不因测试时推理受益，且缺乏可靠地校准自身响应信任度的能力。现有自检方法（如链式思维、自一致性、自验证）都存在至少一个轴上的失败。因此需要一种不依赖模型自身文本重读的推理方法，以增强单一冻结VLM部署的安全性。

Method: EVADE是一种推理型、非训练方法，核心流程为：模型首先对整图回答问题；当不确定时，定位最具有诊断相关性的区域；在放大的局部视图上重新作答；仅当整图和放大视图的响应一致时才提交答案，否则弃权。核心创新在于跨不同图像视角验证门控一致性，而非重新读取模型自身的文本输出，从而直接规避单模型自检中的验证幻觉问题。

Result: 在VQA-RAD、SLAKE和PathVQA上使用Qwen2.5-VL-7B的实验表明，EVADE是唯一同时改善校准和选择性风险并保持准确率的方法，相比零样本将期望校准误差（ECE）降低最多45%。链式思维、自一致性和自验证方法在至少一个评估指标上失败。接地分析显示自提议区域在诊断结构定位上优于中心裁剪或随机裁剪，但7B VLM无法利用该定位来修正答案，因此可靠性收益主要来自一致性门控和校准性弃权机制。

Conclusion: EVADE证明了在不训练模型的情况下，通过跨视图一致性验证和校准性弃权即可显著提升医学VLM的可靠性和安全性。可靠性收益来自一致性门控和校准弃权，而非区域定位本身带来的答案修正能力。该方法为冻结VLMs在医学场景中的安全部署提供了可行路径，但也揭示了当前规模模型在利用局部信息修正答案方面的局限。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) can achieve high accuracy but remain unreliable: they are systematically overconfident, benefit little from test-time reasoning, and lack the ability to reliably calibrate trust in their own responses. We introduce EVADE (Evidence-Verified Agentic Diagnosis with Escape), an inferential, non-training method that enhances the safety of deploying a single frozen VLM. EVADE responds and, when uncertain, localises the region most diagnostically relevant, re-answers on a zoomed view, and commits only when both the entire image and the zoomed view responses agree; otherwise, it abstains. To directly address verification hallucination in single-model self-checking, our main idea is to verify gate consistency across different image views rather than re-reading the model's own text. Experimental evaluation on VQA-RAD, SLAKE, and PathVQA using Qwen2.5-VL-7B reports that EVADE is the only method that simultaneously improves both calibration and selective risk while maintaining accuracy, reducing expected calibration error (ECE) by up to 45% compared to zero-shot. Chain-of-thought, self-consistency, and self-verification all fail at least one axis. A grounding analysis reports that self-proposed regions perform better at diagnostic structure localisation than centres or random crops. However, a 7B VLM cannot use this localisation to revise answers. Therefore, reliability gains come from the consistency gate and calibrated abstention.

</details>


### [127] [RVLoss: Runoff Vote Loss for Self-Supervised LiDAR Scene Flow Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18864)
*Shiming Wang,Liangliang Nan,Julian Kooij,Holger Caesar,Yancong Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RVLoss是一种用于LiDAR场景流的自监督损失函数，通过两阶段runoff vote机制将运动刚性约束融入损失设计，通过聚类刚性流和自由流作为伪标签训练，在Argoverse2 2026 Challenge及四个跨数据集上达到SOTA性能，比基线提升20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自监督LiDAR场景流方法（如Chamfer loss）依赖最近邻距离，不强制运动刚性，导致物体实例内流动不一致。已有的正则化项虽然试图解决此问题，但特别在大型物体上点间流一致性仍有限。作者观察到最近邻搜索得到的逐点运动可聚类为少数候选流，欲通过投票机制将运动刚性内建于损失函数中。

Method: 提出RVLoss，核心是两阶段runoff vote机制：(1) top-k投票：将逐点运动通过最近邻搜索获得的候选流聚类为少量支配性流候选；(2) top-1投票：用这些候选流补偿源点云，选出最能代表底层刚体运动且具有最高共识的流。基于此构建聚类刚性流和自由流作为伪标签，可无缝集成到现有前馈架构中。

Result: 在Argoverse2 2026 Challenge上，使用RVLoss训练的模型在自监督方法中达到SOTA性能，比使用其他损失设计的基线模型提升20%；跨数据集实验在四个额外数据集上均保持一致性能提升。

Conclusion: RVLoss通过投票机制将运动刚性"by design"地融入自监督损失，有效提升了大型物体上的流一致性，且具有良好的通用性和可迁移性，可作为现有自监督LiDAR场景流框架的即插即用模块。

Abstract: LiDAR scene flow estimates point-wise motion between two consecutive scans, referred to as the source and target. Leading self-supervised methods typically minimize the Chamfer loss, the nearest neighbor distance between the flow-compensated source and the target. However, nearest-neighbor search does not enforce motion rigidity, often leading to inconsistent flows within object instances. Existing approaches address this issue with additional regularization terms, but flow consistency among points remains limited, especially for large objects. We propose RVLoss, a self-supervised loss that incorporates motion rigidity by design through a runoff vote mechanism. Our key observation is that the point-wise motion, calculated from nearest neighbor search, can often be grouped into a small set of dominant flow candidates by voting (top-k voting). Furthermore, when compensating the source by these candidates, the flow that best represents the underlying rigid motion often yields the highest consensus after a second voting (top-1 voting). Based on this insight, we incorporate the two-stage runoff vote into loss design and create cluster-wise rigid flows and free-form flows as pseudo-labels for self-supervised learning. RVLoss can be seamlessly integrated into existing feedforward architectures. Experiments on the Argoverse2 2026 Challenge show that models trained with RVLoss achieve state-of-the-art performance among self-supervised approaches, outperforming baseline models trained with alternative loss designs by 20%. Moreover, cross-dataset evaluations demonstrate consistent performance improvements across four additional datasets. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [128] [Falcon Perception-HD: High Density Perception via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18881)
*Sofian Chaybouti,Yasser Dahou,Ngoc Dung Huynh,Reda Alami,Hilde Kuehne*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探讨将强化学习（GRPO）应用于自回归感知模型的post-training，以直接对齐评估指标（如精确率和召回率）而非代理目标（逐token交叉熵）。在Falcon Perception基础上，设计了针对感知任务的RL框架，包括集合结构输出的奖励设计和多头采样控制。RL带来多项收益：在密集场景（500个物体）中达到SOTA性能、修复mask重复等问题、大幅减少NMS和坐标去重需求、在指代分割任务上全面改进，并通过简单奖励保持物体存在性判断能力（MCC），无需负样本训练。还开发了两种混合自标注pipeline分别针对困难指代表达和密集场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归感知模型在开放词汇定位场景下通常采用SFT+MLE训练，但per-token交叉熵这一代理目标与感知评估指标（如精确率、召回率）根本不对齐。此外，现有系统在非常密集的场景下性能急剧下降甚至崩溃，这些感知特有的挑战需要新的训练范式来解决。

Method: 基于Falcon Perception模型，设计了post-training RL框架（采用GRPO），具体包括：1) 为集合结构输出设计奖励函数（简单惩罚假阴性和假阳性）；2) 多head采样控制策略；3) 两种混合自标注pipeline分别针对困难指代表达和密集场景数据生成。通过RL直接将训练目标与精确率/召回率等感知指标对齐。

Result: RL后训练在多个维度带来改进：在含高达500个物体的密集场景中达到SOTA性能；显著修复mask重复问题，几乎完全消除NMS和坐标去重的需求（提升效率和性能、免去超参数调优）；在PBench和SACO-Gold上的指代分割任务各难度级别均有提升；能够在无需负样本训练的情况下保持物体存在性判断（MCC指标）；两种自标注pipeline均对RL训练有正面贡献。

Conclusion: 简单的、对假阳性和假阴性进行惩罚的奖励函数足以驱动感知模型的RL对齐，并为多重感知问题（密集场景性能崩溃、mask重复、NMS依赖）提供统一解决方案，同时保持物体存在性判断能力。模型权重和数据集将公开发布。

Abstract: Autoregressive perception models trained to localize visual entities under the open-vocabulary setting are mostly trained using Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with maximum likelihood, yet it optimizes a proxy objective (per-token cross-entropy) that is fundamentally misaligned with perception metrics such as precision and recall. In this paper, we explore post-training reinforcement learning (RL), specifically GRPO, to directly align these models with their evaluation metrics. Building up on the recently introduced Falcon Perception, we design an RL framework that addresses perception-specific challenges: reward design for set-structured outputs and multi-head sampling control. We discover multiple benefits from RL for perception: first, RL unlocks state-of-the-art performance in very dense scenes (up to 500 objects per scene), a regime where most existing systems degrade sharply or collapse; furthermore it fixes common issues in autoregressive perception models like mask repetitions and removes almost entirely the need for NMS and coordinate deduplication, which improve both performance and efficiency and remove the need for hyperparameters tuning; overall, we notice improvements on all levels of difficulties in referring expression segmentation (on PBench and SACO-Gold), and we find an elegant way to preserve the knowledge of whether an object exists or not (as evaluated by MCC) without training on negative samples. We show that a simple reward that penalizes false negatives and positives is sufficient. We develop two hybrid self-annotation pipelines, respectively tailored for difficult referring expressions and very dense scenes, and show their benefits on RL-training. Model weights are released as a Falcon Perception revision~\footnote{https://huggingface.co/tiiuae/Falcon-Perception}. Datasets will be published.

</details>


### [129] [Learning-State-Aware Dynamic Generative Data Augmentation on Small-Scale Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18907)
*Ting Xiang,Chenxi Deng,Jinhui Zhao,Bingting Jiang,Ke Zhang,Changjian Chen,Zhuo Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出学习状态感知的动态生成式数据增强（LSADA）方法，根据每个样本的损失和损失下降率确定样本特定的增强强度，并通过解耦数据增强与扩散融合策略在增强图像多样性同时保持类别语义。在9个公开数据集上超过现有SOTA动态GDA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式数据增强（GDA）方法采用任务无关的增强策略，忽略了下游模型的需求；而动态GDA方法虽引入模型反馈，但难以可靠地确定样本特定的增强强度，也无法在不同图像区域间灵活调整增强策略，难以在图像多样性和类别语义之间取得平衡。

Method: LSADA包含两个核心组件：(1) 基于每个样本当前的损失和损失下降率构建学习状态，并映射到样本特定的增强强度；(2) 引入解耦的数据增强与扩散融合策略，对类别相关区域施加强度可控的变换，同时生成多样化的类别无关区域，并通过渐进式融合提升图像多样性且保持类别语义。

Result: 在9个公开数据集上的实验表明，LSADA在6个自然图像数据集上平均超过现有SOTA动态GDA方法4.5%，在3个医学图像数据集上平均超过2.5%。

Conclusion: LSADA通过学习状态感知的增强强度控制和区域自适应的解耦融合策略，有效解决了动态GDA方法在增强强度确定和区域策略适应上的不足，在小样本图像分类任务上取得了显著性能提升。

Abstract: Small-scale image classification is often limited by the scarcity of training data. Generative data augmentation (GDA) based on pretrained generative models has emerged as an effective solution. However, existing methods rely on task-agnostic augmentation strategies that overlook downstream model needs. Although recent dynamic GDA methods incorporate model feedback to guide augmentation, they still struggle to reliably determine sample-specific augmentation strengths and adapt augmentation strategies to different image regions while balancing image diversity and class semantics.
  To address these issues, we propose learning-state-aware dynamic generative data augmentation (LSADA). Specifically, LSADA constructs a learning state for each sample based on its current loss and loss-decrease rate, which is then mapped to a sample-specific augmentation strength. Furthermore, LSADA introduces a decoupled data augmentation and diffusion fusion strategy that applies strength-controlled transformations to class-relevant regions and generates diverse class-irrelevant regions, progressively fusing them to improve image diversity while preserving class semantics. Experiments on nine public datasets show that LSADA outperforms the existing SOTA dynamic GDA method by an average of 4.5% on six natural image datasets and 2.5% on three medical image datasets.

</details>


### [130] [Simple, Safe, and Overlooked: Reclaiming Sustainable Domain Generalization with Statistical Color Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18915)
*Sebastian Doerrich,Francesco Di Salvo,Shyam Nandan Rai,Marco Lents,Christian Ledig*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Colorist，一种基于经典统计颜色匹配的高效数据增强策略，直接在RGB颜色空间进行全局均值-标准差匹配，无需训练且完全可解释，能安全生成结构完整的域变化，在多种临床影像数据集上超越深度生成式风格迁移模型和现有域泛化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件变化、颜色差异及患者特征变化常导致训练好的医学图像分类器在部署时失效。现有解决方法存在不足：标准颜色抖动多样性不足，深度生成式风格迁移则会产生虚假特征、破坏临床相关结构并消耗大量计算资源。

Method: 重新审视经典的统计颜色匹配方法，将其改造为Colorist数据增强策略：直接在RGB颜色空间中进行全局均值-标准差匹配，无需训练、完全可解释，且可无缝集成到标准数据加载器中。

Result: 在组织病理、外周血、皮肤病理和视网膜等分布外数据集中，相比最先进的域泛化正则化方法平衡精度提升最高+9%，相比无增强基线提升最高+13%；在结构保真度和颜色对齐方面优于深度生成模型。

Conclusion: 统计颜色匹配是深度架构之外的一种安全、可解释但被忽视的临床稳健性替代方案，能在保持解剖结构完整性和最小化碳足迹的同时提升模型鲁棒性。

Abstract: Hardware shifts, color variations, and changing patient characteristics between development and deployment routinely break trained medical image classifiers. Existing remedies fall short: standard color jittering provides insufficient diversity, while deep generative style transfer algorithms hallucinate features, destroy clinically relevant structures, and waste massive compute resources. To address this, we revisit classical statistical color matching and repurpose it as Colorist, a highly efficient data augmentation strategy that applies global mean-standard deviation matching directly in the RGB color space. We demonstrate that this training-free, fully interpretable approach safely generates structurally intact domain variations, outperforming deep generative models in structural fidelity and color alignment. Across out-of-distribution histopathology, peripheral blood, dermatology, and retinal datasets, it improves balanced accuracy by up to +9% over state-of-the-art domain generalization regularizers and by +13% over an unaugmented baseline. Moreover, by avoiding neural networks in the augmentation loop, Colorist preserves anatomical structure, minimizes carbon footprint, and integrates seamlessly into standard dataloaders. Together, these findings establish statistical matching as a safe, interpretable, yet overlooked alternative to deep architectures for clinical robustness. Source code is available at https://github.com/sdoerrich97/colorist.

</details>


### [131] [Institutional Books - Visual Elements: An open-source pipeline for extracting, classifying, deduplicating, and captioning visual elements from digital book collections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18957)
*Jimmy Mendez,Matteo Cargnelutti,David Lowry-Duda,Catherine Brobston,Salwa Ismail,Greg Leppert,Amanda Watson,Jonathan Zittrain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"制度书籍-视觉元素"（Institutional Books - Visual Elements），一个开源端到端流水线，用于从历史书籍收藏中检测、分类、去重和生成说明文字。作者同时发布了从哈佛图书馆98.3万册扫描卷中提取的2260万个视觉元素数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史书籍收藏中蕴含丰富的视觉元素（插图、照片、版画、装饰艺术等），但在大规模数字化项目中往往被忽视。尽管OCR已标准化了文本内容的提取，这些视觉组件所包含的细微语境信息在自动化文本提取工作流中仍未被充分利用。

Method: 构建一个端到端流水线，涵盖视觉元素的检测、分类、去重与说明文字生成四个环节，并将其应用于哈佛图书馆数据集中的983,004册扫描卷。

Result: 释放了包含22.6百万视觉元素的数据集，这些元素从哈佛图书馆扫描藏书中提取；同时发布了完整流水线工具。

Conclusion: 该工作促进了一线社群为数字化图书馆馆藏提供计算访问的持续努力，为AI模型训练和数字人文研究等新用例铺平道路。

Abstract: Historical book collections contain rich visual elements - such as illustrations, photographs, engravings, and decorative art - that are frequently under-explored in large-scale digitization projects. While Optical Character Recognition (OCR) has standardized the extraction of textual content, these visual components offer a layer of nuance and context that remains largely untapped by automated text extraction workflows. This technical report introduces Institutional Books - Visual Elements, an open-source end-to-end pipeline for detecting, classifying, deduplicating, and captioning visual elements from historical book collections. Alongside this pipeline, we release an initial dataset of 22.6 million visual elements extracted from the 983,004 scanned volumes that comprise the Institutional Books: Harvard Library dataset. This work contributes to ongoing, community-wide efforts to enable new use cases for digitized library collections through computational access, from artificial intelligence model training to digital humanities research.

</details>


### [132] [Frozen DINO Localizes Image Edits Without a Localizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18968)
*Zane Kumar,Vishal Jain,Bernhard Kainz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRAIL是一种训练无关的图像编辑定位方法，通过向冻结的DINO编码器施加一次全局Haar扰动，利用patch token间的余弦漂移生成定位图，在CocoGlide测试集上达到0.903的patch AUROC，无需掩码监督或生成器信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部图像编辑可能改变照片含义而保留大部分真实内容，法医分析需要精确定位编辑发生的位置。现有方法多依赖掩码监督训练或生成器信息，作者希望探索冻结的自监督DINO编码器本身是否蕴含定位信号，从而实现无需训练的编辑定位。

Method: 对输入图像施加一次全局Haar扰动，送入冻结的DINO编码器，计算扰动前后对应patch token之间的余弦漂移，将漂移量作为局部化映射。该方法无需微调或掩码监督，仅依赖预训练DINO编码器内部的patch级扰动响应。

Result: 在80张源不相交的CocoGlide测试图像上，TRAIL达到0.903的patch AUROC，接近掩码监督的Detective SAM（0.912）；固定阈值Dice为0.619对0.709，但使用oracle阈值可将TRAIL提升至0.790。迁移到Poisson图像插值场景仍达0.855 AUROC。最佳DINO块位于归一化深度0.80-0.94。全局扰动优于局部扰动（0.857）和独立裁剪（0.735）。

Conclusion: 冻结的DINO patch token在深层蕴含强定位信号，该信号的可见性取决于扰动方式与全局上下文的保留程度。全局Haar扰动配合完整图像上下文可产生接近监督方法的定位性能，且该线索不依赖于特定生成器。

Abstract: Localized image edits can change a photograph's meaning while leaving most of it authentic, so forensic analysis must identify where an edit occurred. We show that patch-level perturbation responses from frozen DINO encoders are themselves localization maps. Training-free Localization of AI-image Edits from patch-token Drift (TRAIL) applies one global Haar perturbation and maps cosine drift between corresponding patch tokens. On 80 source-disjoint CocoGlide test images, TRAIL reaches .903 patch AUROC versus .912 for the mask-supervised Detective SAM; fixed-threshold Dice is .619 versus .709, while an oracle threshold raises TRAIL to .790. Transferred unchanged to Poisson image interpolation, TRAIL reaches .855 AUROC versus .864, showing that the cue persists without a generator. Across sixteen DINO encoders, the best block lies at normalized depth .80-.94. Global context matters: AUROC falls from .903 globally to .857 for local-in-canvas perturbations and .735 for independently encoded crops. Frozen DINO patch tokens therefore contain a strong late-layer localization signal whose visibility depends on the perturbation and preserved context. Code: https://github.com/VishalJ99/trail-image-edit-localization.

</details>


### [133] [When Simplicity Wins: Bottleneck-Aware Context Modeling for Lightweight Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18979)
*Mian Muhammad Naeem Abid,Nancy Mehta,Zongwei Wu,Radu Timofte*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SiConMo，一个轻量级语义分割框架，包含RGB-only和GME增强两个变体。其核心设计发现是：在极低计算预算下，瓶颈阶段是整合局部与全局上下文最有效的阶段。SiConMo通过三个互补组件（Token金字塔提取模块、Transformer分支深度可分离卷积块、特征合并模块）在ADE20K、PASCAL Context、Cityscapes和COCO-Stuff上实现了轻量级模型中最优的精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率图像的语义分割需要在准确性、效率和可扩展性之间取得平衡。CNN擅长局部模式建模但难以捕捉长距离依赖，ViT虽能捕获全局上下文但计算成本高昂。现有研究大多聚焦于编码器设计，而瓶颈阶段作为上下文聚合和信息流动的核心环节却相对被忽视。

Method: SiConMo采用三种互补组件：(1) Token金字塔提取模块，用于层次化多尺度表示；(2) Transformer分支深度可分离卷积块，用于瓶颈感知的上下文建模；(3) 特征合并模块，在保留空间结构的同时增强语义一致性。框架包含RGB-only（SiConMo）和GME增强（SiConMo†）两个变体。

Result: 在ADE20K、PASCAL Context、Cityscapes和COCO-Stuff四个基准上的大量实验表明，SiConMo在轻量级语义分割模型中取得了最先进的精度-效率权衡（state-of-the-art accuracy-efficiency trade-off）。

Conclusion: 结果验证了"简洁性"作为强大设计原则的有效性，同时揭示了在极低计算预算下瓶颈阶段是整合局部和全局上下文最有效的位置，为轻量级语义分割的设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation demands a careful balance between accuracy, efficiency, and scalability, which remains difficult to achieve for high-resolution imagery. Convolutional networks effectively model local patterns but struggle with long-range dependencies, whereas Vision Transformers capture global context at a high computational cost. While recent work largely focuses on encoder design, the bottleneck stage, central to contextual aggregation and information flow, has been relatively overlooked. We propose SiConMo, a lightweight yet effective framework, implemented in two variants: an RGB-only model (SiConMo) and a GME-enhanced variant (SiConMo$_\dagger$). We show that simplicity arises from a key design principle: at very low computational budgets, the bottleneck is the most efficient stage to integrate local and global context. SiConMo integrates three complementary components: a Token Pyramid Extraction Module for hierarchical multi-scale representation, a Transformer-Branched Depthwise Convolution block for bottleneck-aware context modeling, and a Feature Merging Module that preserves spatial structure while enhancing semantic consistency. Extensive experiments on ADE20K, PASCAL Context, Cityscapes, and COCO-Stuff demonstrate that SiConMo achieves a state-of-the-art accuracy-efficiency trade-off among lightweight semantic segmentation models, highlighting simplicity as a powerful design principle.

</details>


### [134] [Uncertainty-Aware Art-Historical Dating with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18984)
*Stefanie Schneider,Peter Bell*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了博物馆和档案数据集并非真实反映历史艺术生产，而是折射出收藏、保存、编目和数字化等制度性条件的偶然历史。作者将这种现象称为"时间纠缠"（temporal entanglement），并通过将艺术品断代建模为基于冻结图像嵌入的不确定性感知回归任务，在时间受控的Wikidata语料上评估多个预训练视觉模型，发现视觉-语言模型（VLMs）优于纯视觉自监督基线，但定性分析表明这些时间知识受多种偏差影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为预训练图像模型编码的时间信息反映的是艺术创作的历史时间，但作者指出数据集的构建过程本身受制于收藏、保存、编目和数字化等制度性实践，这些条件可能被模型误编码为"历史时间"，从而扭曲对模型表征的理解。这一现象尚无系统性研究。

Method: 作者将艺术品断代问题形式化为一个基于冻结图像嵌入（frozen image embeddings）的不确定性感知回归任务（uncertainty-aware regression）。他们在时间受控的Wikidata艺术品语料库上，评估多种预训练视觉模型，包括纯视觉自监督基线和视觉-语言模型（VLMs），并进行定量评估和定性偏差分析。

Result: 预训练视觉模型确实包含可用的时间断代信息；视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在断代任务上表现优于纯视觉自监督基线；但定性分析揭示这些模型的时间知识受到多种偏差（制度性、收集性等）的塑造，并非纯粹反映历史创作时间。

Conclusion: 预训练图像表征中看似编码的"历史时间"实际上可能是制度性条件（收藏、编目、数字化过程）的产物。研究者需要谨慎对待此类模型输出的时间信息，意识到"时间纠缠"现象的存在及其对下游任务解释的影响。

Abstract: Museum and archival datasets do not mirror historical artistic production, but materialize the contingent histories of collecting, preservation, cataloging, and digitization. This has direct consequences for interpreting pretrained image representations: they may appear to encode historical time while actually encoding the institutional conditions under which objects become visible as data. We describe this phenomenon as temporal entanglement and investigate it by formulating artwork dating as an uncertainty-aware regression task over frozen image embeddings. We evaluate several pretrained vision models on a temporally controlled Wikidata corpus of artworks. Our results show that these models contain usable temporal information, with Vision-Language Models (VLMs) outperforming purely visual self-supervised baselines. However, a qualitative analysis indicates that this temporal knowledge is shaped by various biases.

</details>


### [135] [X-LMC: Cross-View Spatiotemporal Collateral Circulation Scoring from DSA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18986)
*Maedeh Hafezi Moghadas,Hakim Baazaoui,Lukas Bastian Otto,Susanne Wegener,Björn Menze,Ezequiel De la Rosa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: X-LMC是一个用于自动化侧支血管（LMC）评分的时空深度学习框架，从时间分辨的双平面DSA中对ASITN/SIR进行自动分级，在多中心134名M1段闭塞患者数据集上取得了优于现有基线的性能（QWK 0.398，宏F1 0.711）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DSA是评估软脑膜侧支循环（LMC）的金标准，其ASITN/SIR分级结果对卒中患者治疗策略、神经康复和回顾性研究具有关键临床价值。然而，传统LMC分级依赖人工目视检查，存在主观性强、变异性大的问题，亟需自动化客观评分方法。

Method: 采用DINOv2骨干网络编码空间帧表示，通过token级跨视角注意力模块融合正交投影（正位+侧位）信息，再利用循环神经网络建模对比剂团注动态的时间序列特征，从而构建时空多视角自动化分级框架。

Result: 在多中心134例M1段闭塞患者的5折交叉验证中，X-LMC超过静态架构和现有血管造影时空基线，取得QWK 0.398（对比最佳基线0.322）和二分宏F1 0.711（对比0.663），且与临床观察者间一致性（QWK 0.314）吻合。

Conclusion: 作为首个尝试自动化LMC评分的DSA研究，X-LMC证明多视角时序深度学习能够捕捉侧支特异性对比剂动力学特征，为卒中队列的客观血流动力学表型分析建立了可复现的基准，同时揭示了自动化ASITN/SIR分级的临床模糊性与性能边界。

Abstract: Digital subtraction angiography (DSA) is the reference standard for leptomeningeal collateral (LMC) assessment, providing critical prognostic insights to guide secondary treatment strategies, neurorehabilitation planning, and retrospective stroke research. However, clinical LMC grading via the ASITN/SIR scale relies on manual, highly variable visual inspection. We introduce X-LMC, a spatiotemporal framework for automated collateral scoring from time-resolved biplane DSA. The proposed architecture encodes spatial frame representations through a DINOv2 backbone, fuses orthogonal projections via a token-level cross-view attention module, and models representations of contrast bolus dynamics using a recurrent network architecture. We evaluate our framework on a multicenter dataset of 134 patients with M1-segment occlusions. In a 5-fold cross-validation setting, X-LMC yields higher point estimates than static architectures and spatiotemporal baselines adapted from related angiographic tasks, achieving a Quadratic Weighted Kappa (QWK) of 0.398 (vs. 0.322) and a dichotomized macro-F1 score of 0.711 (vs. 0.663) against the best-performing baseline. X-LMC performance also aligns with the observed clinical inter-rater agreement (QWK: 0.314). As the first DSA study attempting to automate LMC scoring, we demonstrate that multi-view temporal deep learning can capture collateral-specific contrast kinetics. Ultimately, these benchmarks delineate the clinical ambiguities and achievable performance boundaries of automated ASITN/SIR grading, establishing a reproducible foundation for objective hemodynamic phenotyping in stroke cohorts. Code is available at https://github.com/maedehafezi/X-LMC.

</details>


### [136] [ForeSightGuide: An Anticipatory Framework toward Accurate and Low-Redundancy Guidance for the Visually Impaired](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18993)
*Zhiyuan Wang,Xu Li,Shikang Guo,Wei Meng,Quan Liu,Jie Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ForeSightGuide是一个面向视障人士的前瞻性辅助导航框架，利用视觉语言模型（VLM）的推理能力将场景语义理解与预测性危险评估相结合，通过预测障碍物运动并过滤非威胁物体，减少误报和信息过载。在公开基准和自建动态交通场景数据集上取得SOTA性能，将冗余警报降至每次输出0.299，同时将漏报率保持在0.112的较低水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子出行辅助工具对视障人士的独立出行至关重要。现有基于VLM的系统虽然能提供丰富的环境理解，但在动态场景中往往产生过多误报，导致视障用户认知过载。现有系统多为被动反应式，缺乏对障碍物运动的预测能力，因此需要一种前瞻性的辅助框架来解决这一问题。

Method: 提出ForeSightGuide框架，核心创新在于将VLM的场景理解能力与预测性危险评估相结合。不同于传统的反应式系统，该方法利用VLM的推理能力主动预测障碍物的运动趋势，从而有效过滤非威胁性物体，仅提供简洁、可执行的导航指导。此外，还构建了一个在复杂动态真实交通场景中采集的新数据集，用于基准测试预测能力。

Result: 在公开基准和自建数据集上均达到SOTA性能。具体而言，冗余警报降至每次导航输出仅0.299个，显著缓解信息过载；同时漏报率保持较低水平（0.112），证明了安全步行辅助的有效性。

Conclusion: ForeSightGuide通过将被动感知升级为主动预判，有效平衡了信息精简与安全保障之间的权衡，为视障人士的安全步行辅助提供了一种实用且高效的解决方案，证明了预测性评估在辅助导航中的价值。

Abstract: Electronic travel aids are pivotal for the independent mobility of the visually impaired. While Vision-Language Models (VLMs) offer rich environmental understanding, they often suffer from excessive false positives in dynamic scenarios, leading to cognitive overload. To address this, we present ForeSightGuide, an anticipatory assistive guidance framework that couples semantic scene understanding with predictive hazard assessment. Unlike reactive systems, ForeSightGuide leverages the reasoning capabilities of VLMs to anticipate obstacle motion, effectively filtering out non-threatening objects to provide concise, actionable guidance. To validate our approach, we introduce a novel dataset captured in complex, dynamic real-world traffic scenes, designed to benchmark predictive capabilities. Extensive experiments on both public benchmarks and our proposed dataset demonstrate that ForeSightGuide achieves state-of-the-art performance. Notably, it significantly mitigates information overload by reducing redundant alerts to 0.299 per guidance output while maintaining a low missed-hazard rate of 0.112, proving its efficacy for safe walking assistance.

</details>


### [137] [GrabVG: Graph-Attentive Binding for Visual Grounding in UAV Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18996)
*Chaowei Wang,Yan Di,Jingjun Sun,Baozhe Liu,Jiaxu Tian,Yuheng Li,Guangqian Guo,Shan Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GrabVG，一种受人类视觉搜索启发的无人机影像视觉定位框架，将定位任务显式分解为前注意假设搜索和图注意力特征绑定两个阶段，通过蒸馏引导的提议归纳和文本感知过滤生成紧凑的候选假设，再利用图注意力机制联合绑定语言引导的实例内视觉线索和实例间拓扑关系，在AerialVG和AerialSense数据集上分别达到67.31%和80.34%的Acc@0.5，较基线提升10.55和8.76个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机影像中的视觉定位面临两大挑战：大量小尺寸、密集分布且视觉相似的物体会造成高视觉冗余；重复的局部配置会产生强烈的拓扑歧义。现有方法主要关注视觉-语言特征对齐或密集上下文交互，但难以区分细微的实例间差异，也无法有效利用空间拓扑结构，在高度拥挤场景下定位不准确。

Method: GrabVG将视觉定位显式分解为两个顺序阶段：(1) 前注意假设搜索——通过蒸馏引导的提议归纳和文本感知假设过滤，生成紧凑可靠的物体候选集，减少背景干扰和语义不匹配；(2) 图注意力特征绑定——将候选假设组织为稀疏图，通过图注意力机制联合绑定和传播语言引导的实例内视觉线索与实例间拓扑关系，实现高效的空间推理和目标定位。

Result: 在AerialVG和AerialSense数据集上，GrabVG分别达到67.31%和80.34%的Acc@0.5，较对应基线分别提升10.55和8.76个百分点，实现了良好的精度-速度权衡。

Conclusion: 通过模仿人类视觉搜索机制，将视觉定位解耦为前注意假设搜索和图注意力特征绑定两个阶段，GrabVG能够有效解决无人机鸟瞰场景中因物体密集、视觉相似和拓扑歧义导致的定位不准问题，为高拥挤场景下的视觉定位提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: Visual grounding in Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) imagery aims to localize a target object in complex bird's-eye-view scenes according to a natural language description. However, the abundance of small, densely distributed, and visually similar objects creates high visual redundancy, while repetitive local configurations give rise to strong topological ambiguity. Existing approaches mainly focus on visual--language feature alignment or dense contextual interaction, yet they struggle to distinguish subtle inter-instance differences and effectively exploit spatial topological structures, leading to inaccurate grounding in highly crowded scenarios. To address these challenges, we propose $\textbf{GrabVG}$, a novel visual grounding framework inspired by human visual search. GrabVG explicitly decomposes grounding into two sequential stages: $\textit{preattentive hypothesis search}$ and $\textit{graph-attentive feature binding}$. Specifically, we first generate a compact set of reliable object hypotheses through distillation-guided proposal induction and text-aware hypothesis filtering, substantially reducing background distractions and semantic mismatches. These hypotheses are then organized into a sparse graph, where language-guided intra-instance visual cues and inter-instance topological relationships are jointly bound and propagated via graph attention, enabling efficient spatial reasoning and accurate target localization. Extensive experiments on AerialVG and AerialSense show that GrabVG achieves a favorable accuracy--speed trade-off, reaching 67.31$\%$ and 80.34$\%$ Acc@0.5 and outperforming the corresponding baselines by 10.55 and 8.76 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [138] [Mise-en-Scène: Implicit Layout Emergence in Diffusion Transformers for Human-AI Design Co-Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19000)
*Zipeng Xu,Ryan Murdock,Umberto Michieli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Mise-en-Scène两阶段框架来自动化图形设计合成：第一阶段通过微调的扩散Transformer（配合knockout选择的LoRA）在渲染画布中隐式生成布局；第二阶段通过确定性的匹配-放置步骤将原始高清素材精确放置到草稿位置，兼顾整体构图连贯性与素材保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法用语言模型预测显式边界框坐标再粘贴素材，将空间规划与视觉合成分离，导致构图僵硬、素材缩放失真的问题。作者提出让布局在预训练图像编辑扩散Transformer内部隐式涌现，以解决该方法论的缺陷。

Method: 两阶段框架。第一阶段：对预训练扩散Transformer进行最小化适配（仅用小规模knockout筛选的LoRA），使元素排列与渲染画布联合涌现，无需专门的多元素条件机制。第二阶段：确定性匹配-放置步骤，将原始高分辨率素材移动到草稿对应位置，保证素材绝对保真并生成可编辑的分层设计。

Result: 在PrismLayersPlus大规模基准上，Mise-en-Scène生成的设觉质量与真实标注最接近，以大幅优势超越LLM布局规划器和专用布局Transformer；匹配-放置阶段进一步弥合了与真实合成图之间的保真度差距。

Conclusion: 预训练图像编辑扩散Transformer只需极小的适应即可隐式完成多元素布局与构图，辅助确定性匹配-放置步骤可在保证素材保真度的同时产出可编辑的分层设计，为自动化图形设计合成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Automating graphic design synthesis from user-provided elements requires both a coherent overall composition and the exact preservation of each asset. Existing methods predict a layout as explicit bounding-box coordinates with a language model and then paste the assets into it, which separates spatial planning from visual synthesis and tends to produce rigid, mis-scaled compositions. We instead ask whether the layout can emerge implicitly inside a pretrained image-editing diffusion transformer. We present Mise-en-Scène, a two-stage framework. In the first stage, a diffusion transformer adapted with a small, knockout-selected LoRA drafts a complete design in which the arrangement of the elements emerges jointly with the rendered canvas. In the second stage, a deterministic match-and-place step moves the original high-resolution assets to the drafted positions, which guarantees exact asset fidelity and yields an editable, layered design that a designer can keep refining rather than a flat image. Notably, a minimal adaptation of the pretrained transformer already suffices, without the specialized conditioning machinery commonly introduced for multi-element generation. On the large-scale PrismLayersPlus benchmark, the designs produced by Mise-en-Scène are the closest to the ground truth in perceived quality among all compared methods, by a wide margin over both an LLM layout planner and a specialized layout transformer, while our match-and-place stage bridges the remaining fidelity gap to the ground-truth composites.

</details>


### [139] [One-Stage Object Detectors in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19014)
*Jonel Roman,Ryan Sirjue,Peter Nguyen,Daniel Krutky,Juan Jesus,Sudip Dhakal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于自动驾驶场景下单阶段目标检测器的全面综述，系统回顾了从YOLOv1到YOLOv10的主要单阶段检测器架构，涵盖设计选择、特征融合策略、损失函数、部署权衡和基准性能对比，并总结了常用数据集、评估指标、开放挑战及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需要快速可靠的实时感知系统来检测车辆、行人、骑行者、交通标志等道路目标。单阶段目标检测器因其在速度与精度之间的良好平衡而成为关键解决方案，但目前缺乏对该领域发展脉络和关键权衡的系统性梳理。

Method: 采用综述性研究方法，系统回顾和比较YOLOv1、SSD、RetinaNet、EfficientDet等经典单阶段检测器，以及FCOS、CenterNet等无锚框检测器和YOLOv10等最新实时模型，从架构设计、特征融合、损失函数、部署权衡等维度进行横向对比分析。

Result: 本文揭示了单阶段检测器在速度、精度、效率和鲁棒性之间的权衡关系，梳理了各主要架构的设计演进路径，并指出当前基准测试结果与实际自动驾驶部署性能之间仍存在显著差距。

Conclusion: 单阶段检测器是自动驾驶感知系统的核心组成部分，虽然已取得显著进展，但在真实世界的鲁棒性和可靠性方面仍有不足，未来研究需要进一步缩小基准测试与真实驾驶场景之间的性能鸿沟。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles depend on fast and reliable perception systems to detect surrounding vehicles, pedestrians, cyclists, traffic signs, and other road objects in real time. This paper presents a comprehensive survey and analysis of one-stage object detectors for autonomous driving rather than an implementation of a new detection system. The survey reviews the evolution of major one-stage detectors, including YOLOv1, SSD, RetinaNet, EfficientDet, anchor-free detectors such as FCOS and CenterNet, and recent real-time models such as YOLOv10. It compares these architectures through their design choices, feature-fusion strategies, loss functions, deployment trade-offs, and reported benchmark performance. The paper also summarizes commonly used autonomous-driving datasets, evaluation metrics, open challenges, and future research directions. Overall, this survey highlights how one-stage detectors balance speed, accuracy, efficiency, and robustness, while also emphasizing the remaining gap between benchmark results and dependable real-world autonomous-driving performance.

</details>


### [140] [Orthogonal Polynomial Approximation for Matrix Log Normalization in Global Covariance Pooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19021)
*Md Rifat Ur Rahman,Md Raihan Khan,Md Sakib Hossain Shovon,Pietro Liò,Mohammad Ali Moni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出用矩阵多项式（尤其是切比雪夫多项式）近似矩阵对数，替代谱分解计算矩阵对数用于全局协方差池化（GCP），消除了数值不稳定性，同时比矩阵平方根近似更忠实于SPD流形，在多个细粒度识别基准和ImageNet-1k上更快更准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局协方差池化依赖于对协方差矩阵的归一化操作。虽然矩阵对数（MLN-COV）是SPD流形上最忠实的映射选择，但其基于特征分解的梯度数值不稳定，导致实践中被弃用而改用矩阵平方根。作者认为这种不稳定性是谱分解计算方式的伪影而非对数本身的问题，希望通过消除特征分解来恢复矩阵对数的使用。

Method: 用有限多项式在协方差矩阵上近似矩阵对数，使前向和反向传播都只包含通用矩阵乘法（GEMM）。关键创新是均值特征值预归一化，将谱中心移至1附近，避免log函数在0处的奇异性，并用标量后补偿以闭合形式恢复log(A)的奇异部分。推荐使用8阶切比雪夫展开，通过三项矩阵递推求值，并配合反向递推计算梯度。同时以Legendre、Laguerre、Taylor和Padé展开作为对照，隔离基函数和目标函数各自的作用。

Result: 在三个细粒度识别基准和ImageNet-1k上，无分解的对数方法比谱对数及平方根近似方法更快且更准确。在匹配基和阶数时，log目标优于平方根目标，证明性能提升来自忠实的黎曼映射而非更好的多项式族。

Conclusion: 矩阵对数在GCP中的数值不稳定性可以完全通过多项式逼近消除，不依赖特征分解。忠实于SPD流形几何的矩阵对数归一化比矩阵平方根近似具有本质优势，且计算效率更高，有望使MLN-COV重新成为GCP的首选归一化方案。

Abstract: Global Covariance Pooling (GCP) improves deep networks by capturing second-order feature statistics, and is especially effective for fine-grained recognition. Because covariance matrices live on the Symmetric Positive Definite (SPD) manifold, a normalization step is required before the Euclidean classifier. The faithful choice is the matrix logarithm (MLN-COV), which maps the SPD manifold to its tangent space; in practice it was abandoned in favour of the matrix square root because its eigendecomposition-based gradient is numerically unstable. We show that this instability is an artifact of computing the logarithm spectrally, not of the logarithm itself. Approximating the logarithm with finite polynomials in the covariance matrix removes the eigendecomposition from both passes: every operation becomes a General Matrix Multiplication (GEMM), the gradient stays bounded on the spectral support of the pre-normalized covariance, and the unstable 1/(lambda_i-lambda_j) term never appears. The key ingredient is a mean-eigenvalue pre-normalization that centres the spectrum near 1, away from the singularity of log, with a scalar post-compensation that returns the singular part of log(A) in closed form. Our recommended normalizer is a degree-8 Chebyshev expansion evaluated by a three-term matrix recurrence, with a matching reverse recurrence for the backward pass; Legendre, Laguerre, Taylor and Pade expansions are studied as controls that isolate the roles of the basis and of the target function. On three fine-grained benchmarks and ImageNet-1k the decomposition-free logarithm is both faster and more accurate than the spectral logarithm and than the square-root approximations it replaces, and at matched basis and degree the log target beats the square-root target, confirming that the gain comes from the faithful Riemannian map rather than from a better polynomial family.

</details>


### [141] [Counterfactual Contrastive Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19032)
*Yunlong He,Pietro Gori*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于对比分析（Contrastive Analysis, CA）的分类器无关视觉反事实解释（VCE）方法。该方法直接作用于数据分布而非决策边界，利用StyleGAN2的特征空间F（而非常用的W空间）更好地保留细节，并克服了传统CA方法只能处理单一显著因子的限制，允许每个数据集有多个显著因子。在三个医学影像数据集上验证了优于现有方法的反事实生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VCE方法本质上是分类器依赖的，因此容易受到分类器偏见和失效模式的影响（如对捷径特征的敏感性和校准误差）。为了提供更鲁棒、模型无关的反事实解释，需要一种不依赖特定分类器的方法。

Method: 1) 基于对比分析框架，对两个类别（如健康与患者）的数据集进行生成因子解耦，分离共同因子与各自数据集的显著因子，通过交换显著因子生成反事实图像。2) 使用StyleGAN2，并采用特征空间F而非W空间以提高细节保留能力。3) 改进了传统CA框架和损失函数，使其支持每个数据集存在多个显著因子，而非仅假设单一数据集具有显著因子。

Result: 在三个医学影像数据集上的实验表明，该方法相比现有VCE方法生成了更高质量的反事实图像。

Conclusion: 通过直接建模数据分布而非决策边界，该方法提供了对分类器偏见不敏感的模型无关VCE，扩展了对比分析在反事实生成中的应用范围。

Abstract: Visual Counterfactual Explanations (VCEs) aim to explain image classifiers by generating minimally edited and realistic versions of an input image that change the classifier's prediction. Existing VCE methods are inherently classifier-dependent and therefore susceptible to classifier biases and failure modes, such as sensitivity to shortcut features and calibration errors. In this paper, we propose a classifier-free approach for visual counterfactual generation based on Contrastive Analysis (CA). Given two datasets corresponding to different classes (e.g., healthy and patients), we disentangle the generative factors that are common across the two datasets from those that are salient to each dataset, and generate counterfactual images by swapping only the salient factors. By operating directly on data distributions rather than decision boundaries, our method provides model-agnostic VCEs that are less sensitive to classifier biases. Our approach leverages the high-quality synthesis and well-structured latent space of StyleGAN2. We use the feature space F, instead than the usual W-space, to improve detail preservation. Unlike conventional CA approaches, which typically assume salient factors in only one dataset, we introduce an adapted framework and loss functions for VCE that allow multiple salient factors in each dataset. We evaluate our method on three medical imaging datasets and demonstrate superior counterfactual generation quality compared to existing approaches.

</details>


### [142] [USR-Drive: Unified Driving Scene Representation via Joint Denoising of 3D Gaussians and Boxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19036)
*Li-Heng Chen,Haokai Pang,Chengye Su,Jiarun Liu,Qifeng Chen,Ziqian Ni,Jianxin Huang,Shi-Sheng Huang,Hongbo Fu,Sheng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: USR-Drive是一个统一的条件生成框架，在共享场景表示中联合恢复动态稠密几何（3D高斯）和实例级对象布局（3D边界框）。通过双重化的多模态扩散Transformer和多模态位置编码，两个模态相互约束、互相增强，在nuScenes和VKitti数据集上同时取得动态重建和3D检测的最新结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将动态重建（稠密3D高斯表示）和实例级感知（3D对象检测）视为独立任务，但实际上它们共享相同的底层3D世界状态估计目标。这种分离导致动态重建缺乏约束（under-constrained），而3D检测缺少几何信息支撑，两者未能互相利用。

Method: 提出统一条件生成框架USR-Drive：(1) 将稠密3D高斯基元和稀疏3D边界框表示为两个对齐的潜在token流，通过统一的多模态扩散Transformer联合去噪；(2) 提出统一位置编码（UPE），在对齐的度量时空坐标系中对齐异质token；(3) 将boxes与几何视为相互约束的状态变量而非外部条件或解耦模块，实现两者的协同优化。

Result: 在nuScenes和VKitti数据集上，USR-Drive在动态重建和3D检测两个任务上均取得了最先进的结果（state-of-the-art），验证了统一表示与生成框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将动态重建与实例级感知统一到共享的条件生成框架中，几何证据与实例结构先验相互增强，有效解决了动态重建约束不足与3D检测缺乏几何基础的问题，为自动驾驶场景的联合3D感知与重建提供了新范式。

Abstract: Spatial representation learning for autonomous driving aims to map raw visual signals into structured 3D scene representations, where object-centric bounding boxes and rendering-oriented 3D primitives (\eg, 3D Gaussians) serve as two distinct yet highly complementary levels for scene understanding. Existing methods typically treat dynamic reconstruction and instance-level perception as separate tasks, despite their shared goal of estimating the underlying 3D world state. As a result, dynamic reconstruction is under-constrained while 3D detection lacks geometric grounding. To address this gap, we propose USR-Drive, a unified conditional generative framework that, given only posed multi-view driving videos, jointly recovers dense dynamic geometry and instance-level object layouts within a shared scene representation. Specifically, USR-Drive represents dense Gaussian primitives and sparse 3D bounding boxes as two aligned latent token streams and jointly denoises them with a unified multi-modal diffusion Transformer. Unlike prior paradigms that use boxes as external conditions or predict them with detached modules, USR-Drive treats them as mutually constrained state variables with a Unified Positional Encoding (UPE) that aligns heterogeneous tokens within a shared metric spatiotemporal coordinate. Via such unified representation and generative framework, the two modalities reinforce each other: geometry supplies dense metric evidence for box prediction, while boxes provide instance-level structural priors that help preserve spatial consistency and reduce ambiguity in sequential 3D geometric representation. Our approach successfully delivers state-of-the-art results for both dynamic reconstruction and 3D detection on the nuScenes and VKitti datasets.

</details>


### [143] [Generalized Audio-Driven Synthesis of Precise Drummer Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19055)
*Álvaro G. Iñesta,Mattia Ryffel,Amit H. Bermano,Robert W. Sumner,Martin Guay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于生成式扩散框架的音乐驱动击鼓动画合成系统，通过双目标损失函数将骨骼完整性与鼓棒精度解耦，实现厘米级鼓棒精度且不牺牲自然身体动态；同时提出两个新评估指标（打击-目标距离和音频-运动相关分数），并在自制数据集和数据增强策略下实现对非策划真实音频的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐驱动的角色动画在娱乐和互动教育中有广泛应用，但合成真实的击鼓动作仍然具有挑战性，因为高加速度动力学与极致的时空精度之间存在内在张力。现有方法（如运动匹配或依赖MIDI输入）难以泛化到多样化的真实音频，且缺乏能够区分精确击鼓与噪声运动的标准评估指标。

Method: 提出一个生成式扩散框架，其核心是双目标损失函数，将骨骼完整性与鼓棒精度解耦优化；并结合自制数据集与数据增强策略来实现对非策划、野外音频的泛化能力。此外，提出两个新指标：impact-to-target distance量化空间精度，audio-motion correlation score评估时间对齐。

Result: 定量分析和用户研究表明，该系统生成的高质量运动常与真实表演难以区分，验证了方法在精度、自然度和音频泛化方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该扩散生成框架成功解决了音乐驱动击鼓动画中精度与动态自然的矛盾，并通过新指标体系提供了更严谨的评估手段，生成结果已达到与真实现实难以区分的水平。

Abstract: Music-driven character animation enables and enhances transformative applications in entertainment and interactive education. However, synthesizing realistic drumming motion from audio remains challenging due to the inherent tension between high-acceleration dynamics and the need for extreme spatial-temporal precision. Existing approaches, often reliant on motion matching or MIDI input, struggle with generalizing to diverse real-world audio. Moreover, the field lacks standardized evaluation metrics capable of distinguishing precise drumming from noisy motion. In this paper, we introduce a generative diffusion framework featuring a dual-objective loss function that decouples skeletal integrity from drumstick precision, thus enabling centimeter-level stick precision without sacrificing natural body dynamics. Additionally, leveraging our own dataset and data augmentation strategy, the model generalizes to non-curated, in-the-wild audio. To rigorously evaluate performance, we propose two novel metrics: an impact-to-target distance to quantify spatial precision and an audio-motion correlation score to assess temporal alignment. Our quantitative analysis and user studies demonstrate that our system generates high-quality motion that is often indistinguishable from ground-truth performances.

</details>


### [144] [When Two Tracers Disagree: An Investigation of Multimodal Fusion for Clinical PET/CT Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19063)
*Jack A. Johnson,Bartłomiej W. Papież*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探讨了PSMA与FDG PET/CT双示踪剂多模态融合进行全身病灶分割的深度学习架构，对比了早期融合（单/双解码器）与中间融合（双编码器交叉注意力DECA-UNet）。结果表明示踪剂特异性单模态基线性能更强（PSMA Dice=0.93，FDG=0.81），而各融合策略均未能在两个示踪剂上同时超越单独训练的单模态模型，FDG分割性能在融合中普遍退化，提示当前融合架构仍难以有效保留示踪剂特异性表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PSMA和FDG PET/CT在前列腺癌中提供互补的生物学信息，联合使用可捕获单一示踪剂遗漏的异质性肿瘤表型，但当前缺乏有效的深度学习架构来实现这两种模态的融合，因此需要系统评估不同的图像融合策略。

Method: 使用公开的DEEP-PSMA挑战赛数据集，训练示踪剂特异性的3D nnU-Net基线模型，并比较两种融合架构：(i)早期融合——单编码器搭配单解码器（OEOD）或双解码器（OETD）；(ii)中间融合——双编码器交叉注意力U-Net（DECA-UNet），用于自动全身PET/CT病灶分割估计总肿瘤负荷。

Result: 示踪剂特异性基线表现优异（PSMA Dice=0.93，FDG=0.81）。融合结果参差不齐：OEOD在更简单的非示踪剂特异性任务上取得0.90的综合Dice，而示踪剂特异性融合模型PSMA/FDG Dice仅为0.69/0.64（OETD）和0.76/0.57（DECA-UNet）。融合虽常能提供合理的PSMA分割，但FDG性能下降，且没有一种策略能在所有指标上持续超越单示踪剂基线。

Conclusion: 在当前评估条件下，示踪剂特异性单模态模型仍是更强的基线方案；要获得有临床价值的多模态融合增益，需要设计能更好地保留各示踪剂特异性表征的架构。

Abstract: PSMA and FDG PET/CT visualise complementary biological information in prostate cancer. Combining both tracers could capture heterogeneous tumour phenotypes that may be missed by either alone, yet there is no consensus on effective deep learning architectures for fusing these modalities. We evaluated multimodal image-fusion strategies for automatic whole-body PET/CT lesion segmentation to estimate total tumour burden. Using the public DEEP-PSMA Challenge dataset, we trained tracer-specific 3D nnU-Net baselines and compared (i) early fusion with a single encoder and one decoder (OEOD) or two decoders (OETD), and (ii) intermediate fusion via a dual-encoder cross-attention U-Net (DECA-UNet). Tracer-specific baselines performed strongly (PSMA Dice = 0.93; FDG = 0.81). Fusion yielded mixed results: OEOD produced a combined Dice of 0.90 (on an easier, non-tracer-specific task), whilst the tracer-specific fusion models reached PSMA/FDG = 0.69/0.64 (OETD) and 0.76/0.57 (DECA-UNet). Whilst fusion often provided reasonable PSMA segmentation, FDG performance degraded and no strategy consistently exceeded the single-tracer baselines. Under the evaluated setting, tracer-specific models remain the stronger baseline; clinically useful gains from multimodal fusion will likely require architectures that better preserve tracer specific representations. Our code is available at: https://github.com/JackJ3636/DEEP_PSMA_code

</details>


### [145] [GS-VLA: Plug-and-Play Viewpoint Canonicalization for Frozen VLA Policies via Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19066)
*Yechan Park,HyunJin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GS-VLA，一个轻量级即插即用框架，利用4M参数的3D高斯新视角合成模块对观测空间进行视角归一化，在不重新训练VLA策略的前提下显著提升其对相机视角偏移的鲁棒性，在LIBERO基准上可恢复大部分因视角变化而丢失的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略隐含假设训练与部署时相机配置一致，但实际中微小的相机安装位移即可使LIBERO基准成功率从约90%骤降至约10%。先前方案（大规模微调、生成式数据增强）计算成本高昂且存在灾难性遗忘风险，亟需一种无需策略重训练的轻量级视角鲁棒性解决方案。

Method: 将视角偏移重构为局部化的新视角合成问题。在局部性假设（相机扰动相对于工作空间保持在小规模有界区域内）下，视角归一化被简化为一个与场景和策略无关的消遮挡任务。具体实现为：在冻结的VLA策略前添加一个4M参数的3D高斯规范化器（canonicalizer），以即插即拔方式接入。

Result: 在不修改策略权重的前提下，GS-VLA沿三个正交维度均提升了性能：（1）不同的策略架构；（2）未见过的任务套件；（3）不同规模的扰动幅度。实验表明轻量级视觉模块可以恢复因视角偏移丢失的大部分性能。

Conclusion: 通过一个轻量化的3D高斯视觉模块进行观测空间适配，无需重新训练策略即可显著恢复视角偏移造成的性能损失，为VLA策略的部署鲁棒性提供了一种高效且通用的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a lightweight, plug-and-play framework that improves robustness to viewpoint shifts in Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies without policy retraining. To our knowledge, this is the first approach to directly leverage 3D Gaussian-based novel-view synthesis for observation-space adaptation in VLA policies. Current VLA performance relies on the implicit assumption that training and deployment camera configurations are identical. Our experiments show that even a small displacement of the camera mount can reduce the success rate on the LIBERO benchmark from about 90% to about 10% in the worst case. Prior approaches, such as large-scale fine-tuning or generative data augmentation, are computationally expensive and risk catastrophic forgetting. To address this, viewpoint shifts are reformulated as a localized novel-view synthesis problem. Under a Locality assumption, that camera perturbations remain within a small bounded region relative to the workspace, viewpoint normalization reduces to a scene- and policy-independent disocclusion task. Our work implements this idea with a 4M-parameter 3D-Gaussian canonicalizer prepended to a frozen VLA policy. Without modifying policy weights, GS-VLA improves performance across three orthogonal axes: (1) Policy architectures, (2) Unseen task suites, and (3) Perturbation scales. These results show that a lightweight visual module can recover a large fraction of the performance lost under viewpoint shift, without policy retraining.

</details>


### [146] [ReWEIGH the Evidence: Calibrating Token-Level Ordinal Visual Evidence to Mitigate Hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19075)
*Jihae Jeong,Junha Choi,Hwanjo Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReWEIGH 是一种无需训练的推理期解码干预方法，通过聚合视觉位置的词汇排名证据并与从无标注图像估算的逐token参考值比较，仅对低于参考的候选施加有界惩罚，从而有效减少LVLM幻觉，在4个7B骨干上将幻觉物体提及率降低最多21.3%，平均每token仅增加1.33%延迟，且可扩展到32B参数和六个架构族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型在生成内容时经常产生幻觉，即输出图像并不支持的内容。防止这类内容需要一种针对具体候选token的度量，来衡量图像对该token的支持强度。现有的位置级视觉token读出信号因概率量级不可比而无法直接池化，词汇排名虽提供尺度不变的池化基础，但不同token在其典型排名证据上仍有系统性差异，因此需要一种能校正这种token特异性偏差的方法。

Method: ReWEIGH提出三类关键设计：(1) 利用模型视觉token状态的词汇排名作为尺度不变的证据表示，跨视觉位置聚合；(2) 从无标注图像估算每个token的特异性参考值（token-specific reference），用于消除不同token在典型排名证据上的系统性差异；(3) 在预填充阶段缓存图像证据，推理时仅对低于其参考的候选token施加有界惩罚（bounded penalty），全程无需训练。

Result: 在四个7B骨干模型上，ReWEIGH将幻觉对象提及（hallucinated object mentions）降低最多21.3%，同时基本保持甚至提升描述性与通用性能。缓存图像证据后，平均每个token仅增加1.33%延迟，且效果可推广至六个架构族、扩展到32B参数规模。

Conclusion: ReWEIGH证明了一种无需训练、低延迟的推理期干预可以有效缓解LVLM幻觉：通过基于排名聚合视觉证据并与token特异性参考对比，仅在候选证据不足时施以有界惩罚，在显著降低幻觉的同时几乎不牺牲生成质量与效率，展现了良好的架构泛化与规模扩展性。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) often hallucinate, generating content that the input image does not support. Preventing such content during decoding calls for a candidate-specific measure of how strongly the image supports the token under consideration. The model's visual-token states offer a natural source of this evidence because projecting each state through the output head reveals which vocabulary items that position favors. These position-wise readouts cannot be pooled directly because their probability magnitudes are not comparable across visual positions. Vocabulary ranks provide a scale-invariant basis for pooling, but tokens still differ systematically in their typical rank-based evidence. We propose ReWEIGH, a training-free decoding intervention that aggregates these ranks across visual positions and compares each candidate with a token-specific reference estimated from unlabeled images. At inference, ReWEIGH caches the image evidence during prefill and applies a bounded penalty only to candidates that fall below their reference. On four 7B backbones, ReWEIGH reduces hallucinated object mentions by up to 21.3% while largely preserving or improving descriptive and general performance. With evidence cached, the average added latency is 1.33% per token, and the reductions extend across six architecture families to 32B parameters.

</details>


### [147] [Subgroup performance analysis of adaptation strategies for chest X-ray foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19078)
*Dhruv Gupta,Emma A. M. Stanley,Fabio De Sousa Ribeiro,Sujal Desai,Ben Glocker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了三种参数高效微调策略（线性头、MLP、注意力池化）应用于冻结的Rad-DINO胸部X光编码器时，对病理分类性能和子群体公平性的影响，发现更丰富的表示能提升准确率，但公平性影响与表示表达能力、属性编码强度均无一致关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型越来越多地被用于下游医学影像任务，但所选的适配策略对子群体公平性的影响尚不清楚。现有研究很少直接探讨不同参数高效微调方法如何影响不同人口统计或成像视角子群体之间的性能差距，而理解这一关系对于医学影像AI的公平部署至关重要。

Method: 基于冻结的Rad-DINO胸部X光编码器，对比三种参数高效适配方法：原始CLS token上的线性头、MLP、对多层patch特征进行注意力池化。使用MIMIC-CXR数据集评估八种病理，按种族、性别和成像视角子群体，在保持患病率、人口统计均衡的测试集上评估，并探测每种适配器对受保护属性的编码强度。

Result: 注意力池化取得最强的整体判别性能，并对属性（尤其是种族）的编码最强；但整体性能的提升并不能持续减少子群体差异。属性编码更强并不对应更大的差异：早期网络层对种族编码最弱却产生了最大的子群体性能差距。不同注意力池化层组合也不显示层选择、属性编码强度与公平性之间的一致性关系。

Conclusion: 更丰富、更具表达力的表示可以提高准确率，但公平性影响是任务相关且不可预测的，必须直接且按任务评估，而不能仅从编码强度或整体性能推断。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly adapted for downstream medical imaging tasks, yet the influence of the chosen adaptation strategy on subgroup fairness remains poorly understood. We investigate how three parameter-efficient adaptation techniques, including linear heads on the raw CLS token, an MLP, and an attention-pooling module over multi-layer patch features, affect both pathology classification performance and subgroup disparities when applied to the frozen Rad-DINO chest X-ray encoder. Using MIMIC-CXR, we evaluate eight pathologies across race, sex, and imaging-view subgroups on a prevalence-preserving, demographically balanced test set, and additionally probe how strongly each adapter encodes protected attributes. We find that attention pooling achieves the strongest overall discriminative performance and encodes attributes, particularly race, most strongly, but that improved overall performance does not consistently reduce subgroup disparities. Notably, stronger attribute encoding did not correspond to larger disparities: early network layers encoded race most weakly yet produced the largest subgroup performance gaps. Exploring different attention-pooling layer combinations further revealed no consistent relationship between the layers pooled, attribute encoding strength, and subgroup fairness. Our results indicate that richer, more expressive representations can improve accuracy while leaving fairness implications task-dependent and unpredictable, which must be assessed directly and per-task rather than inferred from encoding strength or overall performance alone.

</details>


### [148] [SPK: Eliciting Structured Prior Knowledge for Interpretable Out-of-Distribution Detection in Real-Time Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19080)
*Changshun Wu,Weicheng He,Xiaowei Huang,Saddek Bensalem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出结构化先验知识（SPK）框架，通过显式地揭示预训练目标检测器中隐式的潜在先验来实现分布外（OoD）目标幻觉得分检测。SPK利用分布内数据和幻觉诱导样本作为诊断监督，提取物体级别的语义先验，并与几何和上下文先验整合成紧凑的五维表示，在多个目标检测器架构和OoD基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OoD检测方法要么直接在检测器表示上构造评分函数，要么修改检测器本身来抑制幻觉产生，但检测器表示中隐式编码的潜在先验尚未被显式解码用于OoD检测。作者旨在发掘并利用这些被忽视的潜在先验知识。被动拒绝或适配的模式忽视了检测器内部已有的丰富语义知识，因此需要一种显式地从预训练检测器中激发OoD相关先验的方法。

Method: SPK框架包含三个核心步骤：(1) 利用分布内数据和幻觉诱导样本作为诊断监督，从预训练目标检测器中显式激发物体级别的语义概念先验（part-level semantic concepts），而非仅用于拒绝或检测器适配；(2) 将激发出的语义先验与几何先验和上下文先验进行整合；(3) 形成紧凑的五维SPK表示用于OoD检测评分。
