Result: 在多种目标检测器架构和多个OoD基准上进行了广泛实验，SPK达到了最先进的OoD检测性能。实验发现预训练目标检测器编码的潜在知识远比通常用于OoD检测的丰富，并且这些知识可以被显式激发并组织为紧凑、结构化、可解释的知识空间。

Conclusion: 预训练目标检测器内部编码了丰富的隐性先验知识，通过显式激发并将这些知识组织为紧凑可解释的结构化知识空间，可以显著提升OoD检测性能。这为提升目标检测器可靠性提供了一条主动的（proactive）新路线——即通过显式揭示和利用潜在先验，而非仅被动地构造评分函数或修改检测器架构。代码和数据已公开。

Abstract: Object detectors often produce over-confident predictions for objects outside their training categories, leading to so-called out-of-distribution (OoD) hallucinations. Existing approaches for detecting or mitigating such hallucinations typically either construct scoring functions directly over learned object detector representations or modify the object detector itself to suppress hallucination emergence. However, the latent priors implicitly encoded in these representations remain largely unexplored and have not been explicitly decoded for OoD detection. To uncover and exploit these latent priors, we propose Structured Prior Knowledge (SPK), a hallucination-oriented framework that explicitly elicits OoD-relevant priors from pretrained object detectors. Specifically, SPK leverages in-distribution data and hallucination-inducing samples as diagnostic supervision to elicit part-level semantic concepts underlying object detector decision-making, rather than using them merely for rejection or object detector adaptation. The elicited semantic priors are further integrated with geometric and contextual priors to form a compact five-dimensional SPK representation for OoD detection. Extensive experiments across diverse object detector architectures and multiple OoD benchmarks demonstrate that SPK achieves state-of-the-art OoD detection. Our findings reveal that pretrained object detectors already encode substantially richer latent knowledge than is typically exploited for OoD detection. More importantly, this knowledge can be explicitly elicited and organized into a compact, structured, and interpretable knowledge space for prediction reliability analysis. This suggests a promising proactive route for improving object detector reliability by explicitly uncovering and leveraging latent priors. Code and data are available at: https://gricad-gitlab.univ-grenoble-alpes.fr/dnn-safety/spk

</details>


### [149] [Detecting Backdoors in Object Detection via Pre-NMS Prediction Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19088)
*Longtian Wang,Zhengyu Zhao,Chenhao Lin,Le Yang,Shiwei Wang,Yuhan Zhi,Xiaofei Xie,Chao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DistScan是一个基于预NMS类别分布偏移检测的后门检测框架，无需权重访问、触发知识或额外训练，在MS-COCO和PASCAL VOC上相比最优基线平均检测准确率提升27.32个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门检测方法要么依赖触发反转，要么依赖特定架构假设，无法可靠推广到场景级攻击（单个触发器同时导致场景中所有目标异常行为）。需要一个无需权重访问、无需触发知识且无需额外训练的通用检测框架。

Method: 利用此前未被探索的观察：后门注入会系统性改变模型在NMS之前的类别预测分布，即使在没有触发器的干净输入上也会偏离训练类别频率。通过在干净验证集上聚合中间类别预测，并与训练类别频率比较偏离程度来判断模型是否被植入后门。

Result: 在MS-COCO和PASCAL VOC数据集上，跨两种架构和三种场景级攻击场景，DistScan显著优于现有方法，相比最优适用基线平均检测准确率提升27.32个百分点。

Conclusion: DistScan通过利用预NMS类别分布偏移这一简单但有效的信号，提供了一种无需权重访问、触发知识或额外训练的通用后门检测方案，特别适用于场景级后门攻击。

Abstract: Object detection models deployed in safety-critical applications remain vulnerable to backdoor attacks that cause targeted misbehaviors when a hidden trigger is present. Existing detection methods either rely on trigger inversion or exploit architecture-specific assumptions, and critically, representative existing methods fail to generalize reliably to scene-level attacks, where a single trigger induces anomalous behavior across all objects in the scene simultaneously. We present DistScan, a backdoor detection framework based on a simple but previously unexploited observation: backdoor injection systematically shifts a model's pre-NMS prediction class distribution away from its training class frequencies, even on clean inputs without any trigger present. DistScan aggregates intermediate class predictions over a clean validation set and flags a model as backdoored if the resulting distribution deviates significantly from the training class frequencies, requiring no model weight access, no trigger knowledge, and no additional training. Extensive experiments on MS-COCO and PASCAL VOC across two architectures and three scene-level attack scenarios demonstrate that DistScan substantially outperforms existing methods, improving average detection accuracy over the best-performing applicable baseline by 27.32 percentage points.

</details>


### [150] [Image-Guided Pavement Defect Recognition in GPR Data with novel 3D Deep Learning Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19177)
*Yuandong Pan,Linjun Lu,Mudan Wang,Florian Noichl,Fan Xue,Brian Sheil,Lavindra de Silva,André Borrmann,Ioannis Brilakis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合正射RGB影像与3D探地雷达(GPR)数据的低成本数据制备流程，用于大规模生成带标注的3D GPR路面缺陷数据集，并设计了一种融合残差连接、混合卷积核以及深度与通道双重注意力机制的3D CNN模型，在路面修补和裂缝缺陷的二分类任务中优于基线架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPR在路面自动检测中的大规模应用面临两大挑战：一是带标注的真实世界3D GPR数据集稀缺，二是缺乏针对3D GPR数据独特特征设计的深度学习模型。现有标注方法成本高、效率低，且通用模型难以充分利用3D GPR的结构信息。

Method: 首先提出一个成本高效的数据制备流程：利用路面正射RGB影像与3D GPR扫描数据的对齐分段，以路面表观图像为参考，将表面可见缺陷的标注迁移至对应的GPR数据段，从而在运营中的高速公路路段实现大规模高效标注。其次，提出一种专用的3D CNN架构，包含残差连接、混合卷积核尺寸，以及深度注意力与通道注意力双重注意力机制，以增强特征表示和缺陷分类性能。模型在修补与裂缝两类缺陷的二元分类任务上进行评估。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的网络在多个评估指标上优于基线架构；消融研究进一步验证了各设计组件的有效性，包括残差连接、混合卷积核和注意力机制均对性能提升有贡献。

Conclusion: 该工作贡献了一个可扩展且实用的真实世界数据集生成方法，以及一个新颖的深度学习框架，为解决3D GPR路面自动化检测中的数据稀缺和模型适配两个核心问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Ground Penetrating Radar (GPR) is a widely adopted non-destructive sensing technology for subsurface inspection in civil and transportation engineering. Despite its potential for pavement condition assessment, the large-scale application of GPR in automated inspection has two key challenges: the scarcity of annotated real-world datasets and the lack of deep learning models designed for the unique characteristics of 3-Dimensional (3D) GPR data. This study addresses these limitations by firstly introducing a cost-effective data preparation pipeline that integrates orthomosaic Red Green Blue (RGB) imagery with 3D GPR scans to generate annotated 3D GPR datasets. The proposed method uses the aligned segments of RGB and GPR data, using pavement surface images as a reference to transfer labels of surface-visible defects to corresponding GPR segments, enabling efficient large-scale annotation in a real-world dataset collected on a highway section under operation. In addition to the dataset contribution, we propose a specialised 3D Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) architecture incorporating residual connections, mixed convolutional kernel sizes, and both depthwise and channelwise attention mechanisms to enhance feature representation and defect classification. The model is evaluated on binary classification tasks for detecting patch and crack defects in pavement structures. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed network outperforms baseline architectures across multiple evaluation metrics. Ablation studies further confirm the effectiveness of the designed architectural components. This work contributes a scalable and practical method for real-world dataset generation, along with a novel deep learning framework.

</details>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [AdaRare: Telemetry-Guided Joint Profile Control for Greybox Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18187)
*Jingchuan Ma,Tongan Liu,Yanhua Liu,Qiaoyun Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaRare，一个AFL++扩展，将五个内部执行机制（队列、变异、字典、能量、比较求解）协调为一个统一的有界进程内配置文件，每5秒更新一次。实验表明其在边覆盖率上优于vanilla AFL++，并在固件案例研究中发现了五个CVE内存破坏漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灰盒模糊测试工具将交互式的队列、变异、字典、能量和比较求解控制面组合在一起，而先前的自适应系统通常只优化其他决策对象或控制层，缺乏对这些内部执行机制进行统一协调的自适应方法。

Method: AdaRare作为一个AFL++扩展，将五个内部执行机制（A1-A6等）作为单一有界进程内配置文件协调，每5000ms更新一次。采用完成窗口的、动作引发的遥测数据，馈送arm本地的、近期加权线性评分器以及配置文件条件下的控制器目标。评分器借用不相关的LinUCB代数结构，但作为闭环配置文件排名机制而非校准的上下文bandit动作值估计器。

Result: 在Main阶段，AdaRare在全部8个目标上中位边覆盖率高于vanilla AFL++，其中5个Holm显著比较；在Full阶段，5个后续目标上全部优于CmpLog匹配AFL++，其中4个显著；Batch A中15个目标-控制比较中有13个Holm显著。固件案例研究中发现5个不同的内存破坏发现，均被分配CVE标识。控制器边界计算P99中位数低于6.5ms，完整边界（含同步日志）P99中位数低于14.7ms。

Conclusion: AdaRare证明了通过协调多个内部执行机制可以显著提升灰盒模糊测试的边覆盖率，并在真实固件场景中发现多个CVE级漏洞。并非所有子机制（如A6对shadow以及scarcity-bundle）都表现出独立效果，A6证据依目标而定且在批量校正下减弱。边界延迟测量表明该方法的计算开销处于可接受范围。

Abstract: Greybox fuzzers combine interacting queue, mutation, dictionary, energy, and comparison-solving control surfaces, while prior adaptive systems typically optimize other decision objects or control layers. We present AdaRare, an AFL++ extension that coordinates five internal actuation mechanisms as one bounded in-process profile updated every 5,000 ms. Completed-window, action-induced telemetry feeds an arm-local recency-weighted linear scorer and a profile-conditioned controller target. The scorer borrows the algebraic structure of disjoint LinUCB, but serves as a closed-loop profile-ranking mechanism rather than a calibrated contextual-bandit action-value estimator or statistical confidence bound.
  Across three sequential repeated-trial phases, Main provides broad integrated-system evidence: AdaRare has higher median edge coverage than vanilla AFL++ on all eight targets, with five Holm-significant comparisons. In the strongest matched result, Full AdaRare has higher median edge coverage than CmpLog-matched AFL++ on all five follow-up targets, with four Holm-significant comparisons. Batch A finds higher medians for telemetry-guided selection than fixed-context, random, and round-robin schedules in all 15 target-control comparisons, with 13 Holm-significant comparisons. The experiments do not establish independent No-A6-versus-Shadow or scarcity-bundle effects; A6 evidence is target-dependent and weakens under batch-wide correction. In an unmatched firmware case study, AdaRare-generated inputs exposed five distinct memory-corruption findings, each reproduced in a separate environment and later assigned a CVE identifier. Controller-boundary compute P99 medians are below 6.5 ms for a five-second window; complete-boundary P99 medians including synchronous logging are below 14.7 ms. These measurements characterize boundary latency, not total system overhead.

</details>


### [152] [Model Card for OpenAI Privacy Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18274)
*Charles de Bourcy,Sahra Ghalebikesabi,Avi Schwarzschild,Alex Gorbachev,Mihai Maruseac,Annie Chu,Vol Kyrylov,Tong Mu,Ally Bennett,Andy Nguyen,Casey Meehan,Jessica Gan Lee,Shane Bauer,Harold Nguyen,Rodolpho Eckhardt,Yuqi Liu,Charlie Oxborough,Marco Rougeth,Omar Chedid,Caio Costa,Yash Parikh,Yao Li,Congzheng Song,Om Thakkar,Vinnie Monaco*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: OpenAI Privacy Filter是一个紧凑的双向token分类模型，用于检测和脱敏非结构化文本中的个人身份信息（PII）和机密信息。该模型从自回归预训练检查点衍生而来，转换为双向带状注意力分类器，通过约束Viterbi解码器在八个隐私类别中生成连贯的跨度标注，支持可配置的精确率-召回率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分层隐私工作流中需要一个高效、可本地部署且可微调的PII检测与脱敏组件，平衡模型规模、推理效率和检测精度，作为可配置的数据最小化工具而非匿名化或合规保证。

Method: 从自回归预训练检查点出发，将其转换为双向带状注意力分类器（banded-attention classifier），以单次前向传播标注输入序列。采用约束Viterbi解码器生成跨八个隐私类别的连贯跨度，并提供可配置的精确率-召回率操作点。

Result: 模型总参数量15亿，每token活跃参数5000万，支持128,000 token的上下文窗口，兼顾高效本地部署与领域特定微调能力。

Conclusion: Privacy Filter被定位为分层隐私工作流中的可配置数据最小化组件，而非匿名化或合规性保证工具。

Abstract: OpenAI Privacy Filter is a compact, bidirectional token-classification model for detecting and redacting personally identifiable information (PII) and secrets in unstructured text. The model is derived from an autoregressively pretrained checkpoint and converted into a bidirectional, banded-attention classifier that labels an input sequence in a single forward pass. A constrained Viterbi decoder produces coherent spans across eight privacy categories and exposes configurable operating points for precision-recall tradeoffs. Privacy Filter has 1.5 billion total parameters, 50 million active parameters per token, and a 128,000-token context window. It is designed for efficient local deployment and domain-specific fine-tuning. Privacy Filter is intended as a configurable data-minimization component within layered privacy workflows, not as an anonymization or compliance guarantee.

</details>


### [153] [XNET: Intelligent Dynamic Sampling for High-Speed Network Security Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18349)
*Thomas Papastergiou,Karthika Subramani,Joseph Reilly,Boladji Vinny Adjibi,Pierros-Christos Skafidas,Roberto Perdisci,Manos Antonakakis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: XNET是一个基于Linux XDP技术、使用商用硬件实现线速流量监控与动态采样的系统，通过对流量按安全价值分类并动态放大高价值流量采样比例，实现高达84%的流量削减且无丢包，同时将低速率流量（如APT命令控制）的可见性提升5倍，在100Gbps下可扩展，IDS检测率可达99.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络速度快速增长（100GbE成为常态），而传统流量监控方法面临挑战：昂贵硬件、过度采样或复杂分布式部署难以扩展；随机随机性丢包导致低质量流量，尤其会漏掉隐藏在低速率流量中的安全事件（如APT/malware命令与控制通信）。

Method: XNET基于Linux XDP（eXpress Data Path）技术实现高效包处理，将数据包按安全价值分类，并根据配置策略进行动态采样。其核心思路是对低安全价值的流量进行削减采样，同时放大高安全价值流量的可见性，从而在保持线速处理能力的同时提升安全检测的覆盖范围。系统部署仅需商用硬件，具备灵活、可扩展、动态适配网络需求的特性。

Result: 在大型真实网络中部署验证，XNET可实现高达84%的流量削减且无丢包，将低速率但高安全价值流量的可见性提升5倍；受控压力测试证明其可扩展到100Gbps；在IDS应用中采样后检测率达到99.6%。

Conclusion: XNET证明了使用商用硬件和Linux XDP技术即可实现线速流量监控和安全价值驱动的动态采样，能够在显著降低流量处理压力的同时保持甚至提升安全检测能力，为现代高速网络中的安全监控提供了一种低成本、高可扩展性的解决方案。

Abstract: Growing network speeds, with 100GbE line rates becoming common in modern enterprise networks, pose challenges to operators and security applications, as they struggle to scale their operational efficiency accordingly, without relying on costly hardware, excessive sampling, or complex distributed deployments. Unintentional loss due to stochastic packet sampling often produces low-quality traffic, further risking missed detection of critical security incidents, particularly those hidden in typically low-rate traffic, such as APT/malware command-and-control communications. In this paper, we introduce XNET, a system that monitors traffic at line rate using commodity hardware and applies dynamic sampling to amplify the visibility of high security value traffic. XNET leverages Linux's XDP technology to process packets efficiently, classify them based on their security value, and sample them as per configured policies. The outcome is a reduced packet stream in which the security-relevant portion of the traffic is amplified at the expense of less interesting traffic segments. XNET is a highly flexible, scalable and dynamic system that can be adapted based on a network's needs. We deployed XNET in a large real-world network using only commodity hardware, where our results show that XNET can achieve up to 84% traffic reduction with no packet loss while increasing the visibility of otherwise negligible traffic fivefold. With controlled stress tests, we further demonstrate XNET's scalability up to 100Gbps. Additionally, we show that XNET sampling led to a detection rate of 99.6% in an IDS application.

</details>


### [154] [Task-Conditioned Least-Privilege Learning for Executable Terminal and MCP Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18351)
*Alexander Tu,Michael Tu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究通过后训练教4B参数模型在终端和MCP环境中进行"任务条件化的权限选择"，以补充传统权限门控系统。该框架在动作执行前和执行后对六个风险维度进行审计，结合任务特定的充分权限包络计算超额权限值并在后训练中优化。在Qwen3.5-4B上训练后，安全成功率从64.36%提升至98.48%，超额权限错误事件从4.56%降至0.79%，并展示了泛化能力和能力保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具调用型大语言模型代理在执行任务时可能行使用户未授予的权限或任务不需要的权限，产生"超额权限错误"。传统的权限门控系统单独用于验证代理环境是不够的，需要补充其他控制层。

Method: 提出一个框架：每个动作在执行前和执行后分别经过审计，沿六个风险维度进行评估。使用确定性验证器对完成度、证据、确切状态、禁止尝试和安全成功进行打分。结合预定义的任务特定充分权限包络（sufficient-authority envelopes），为轨迹确定任务特定的超额权限值，并在后训练阶段进行优化。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-4B上基于1500个任务训练后，选定的种子在覆盖500个保留任务的2896个评测episode中达到98.48%的安全成功率（基础策略为64.36%），超额权限错误事件从4.56%降至0.79%。外部测试显示能力保留和提示引导改进。400任务续训练研究显示泛化证据，超额权限事件减少6.99个百分点同时保持既有能力。

Conclusion: 通过最小权限感知的后训练（least-privilege aware post-training）学到的约束对工具调用代理在终端和MCP环境中作为额外控制层是有用的，但不能替代权限门控和沙箱化。

Abstract: Tool-using large language-model agents can complete a task while exercising authority that the user did not grant or the task does not need, causing excess-authority errors. Traditional permission gating systems alone for validating agent environments are insufficient. We study whether post-training can teach a 4B-parameter model to choose task-conditioned authority in executable terminal and Model Context Protocol (MCP) environments to complement those measures. We propose a framework where each action is audited before execution and again from observed effects along six dimensions of risk. This auditing is conducted using deterministic verifiers that score completion, evidence, exact state, prohibited attempts, and safe success. In conjunction with predefined task-specific sufficient-authority envelopes, we determine task-specific excess privilege values for trajectories, which are then optimized for in post-training. We find that after training using this framework on Qwen3.5-4B over 1,500 tasks, the selected seed reaches 98.48% safe success across 2,896 evaluation episodes spanning all 500 held-out tasks, compared with 64.36% for the base policy, and reduces excess-authority error events from 4.56% to 0.79%. Furthermore, external tests show capability retention and prompt-directed improvement. A 400 task continuation study also found evidence of generalization, reducing excess-authority events by 6.99 percentage points while maintaining previous capabilities. We conclude learned restraint through least-privilege aware post-training is therefore useful as an additional control layer for tool-using agents in executable terminal and MCP environments, but it does not replace permission gates and sandboxing.

</details>


### [155] [0xPass: A Secure Protocol for Universal Cross-Chain Accounts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18359)
*Bernardo David,Keon Kim,Krish Chelikavada*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出0xPass，一个用于通用跨链账户的模块化协议架构，将请求编排、交易求解和交易签名分离为可互操作层，结合阈值签名防止单一节点持有完整签名密钥，并支持委托授权、交易策略、账户恢复和分布式密钥管理。论文描述了从中心化服务逐步过渡到无许可网络的阶段性部署路径，在保持用户对交易授权控制的同时渐进式降低中心化信任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用账户允许用户通过单一界面在不同区块链生态系统中管理资产和执行操作，但引入了涉及认证、授权、交易签名、密钥托管、恢复和去中心化等方面的安全与信任挑战。现有方案未能同时解决这些多维度的信任问题。

Method: 0xPass采用模块化协议架构，将请求编排、交易求解和交易签名分离为三个可互操作层；用户批准的请求绑定到认证身份并在各层间授权；采用阈值签名防止任何单一交易节点持有完整签名密钥；支持受约束的授权委托、交易策略、账户恢复、分布式密钥管理以及独立运营子网络间的可审计通信；设计了从中心化服务到许可网络再到无许可网络的阶段性部署路径。

Result: 该架构提供了一种实用的框架，能够在扩展跨链账户功能的同时渐进式降低中心化信任，并通过阈值签名和多层授权机制保护用户对交易授权的控制权。

Conclusion: 0xPass为跨链账户提供了一套兼顾功能扩展与去中心化信任演进的实际可行的模块化架构，为未来从中心化向无许可模式的过渡提供了清晰的路径。

Abstract: Universal accounts allow users to manage assets and execute operations across heterogeneous blockchain ecosystems through a single interface, but they introduce security and trust challenges involving authentication, authorization, transaction signing, key custody, recovery, and decentralization. This paper presents 0xPass, a modular protocol architecture for universal cross-chain accounts. 0xPass separates request orchestration, transaction solving, and transaction signing into interoperable layers. User-approved requests are bound to authenticated identities and authorized across layers, while threshold signatures prevent any single transaction node from holding a complete signing key. The design also supports constrained authorization delegation, transaction policies, account recovery, distributed key management, and auditable communication among independently operated sub-networks. We describe a staged deployment path from a centrally operated service to a permissioned network and ultimately to a permissionless network with third-party modules, collateral-backed onboarding, and rotating key-management committees. The resulting architecture provides a practical framework for extending cross-chain account functionality while progressively reducing centralized trust and preserving user control over transaction authorization.

</details>


### [156] [AoNT Trap: Borromean-Entangled Mutable Chameleon Trapdoor Hash All-or-Nothing Stream Cipher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18403)
*Victor Kebande*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出BEC-Trap，一种融合Borromean互依赖、变色龙陷门哈希可变性和流式加密的新型密码构造。该构造将密钥(K)、初始化向量(V)和内部状态(St)以Borromean环结构互锁，任一组件被破解或移除都会导致整个密钥流崩溃，从而在标准密码学假设下实现计算级全有或全无(AoNT)互依赖性。集成的变色龙陷门哈希支持受控碰撞，实现无缝密钥轮换、V刷新和状态旋转而无需端点重新同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流密码缺乏组件间的强互依赖保证，密钥、IV和状态之间的关联性不足，导致部分信息泄露时可能威胁整体安全性；同时传统密钥管理需要重新同步端点，开销较高。作者旨在构造一个同时具备计算级AoNT安全性和高效密钥管理能力的统一流密码框架。

Method: 采用Borromean环拓扑结构将密钥(K)、初始化向量(V)和内部状态(St)三者互锁，使得攻击任一组件均会导致整个keystream坍塌；集成变色龙陷门哈希提供受控碰撞能力，使rekeying、V刷新和状态旋转无需端点重同步；在标准密码学假设下论证其计算级AoNT安全属性。

Result: 安全分析表明BEC-Trap对密钥恢复攻击、状态泄露攻击和去同步攻击具有抵抗性，且计算开销低，适用于高吞吐安全消息、物联网通信和隐私保护的区块链通道。

Conclusion: BEC-Trap作为融合Borromean互依赖与陷门可变更性的流密码原语，为下一代安全通信提供了兼具保密性、前向保密性和自适应密钥管理的稳健构造。

Abstract: This work introduces the Borromean-Entangled Chameleon Trapdoor Hash All-or-Nothing (AoNT) Stream Cipher (BEC-Trap), a novel construction that merges Borromean interdependence, trapdoor-enabled mutability, and streaming encryption into a unified framework. The (BEC-Trap) cipher links key (K), initialization vector (V ), and internal state (St) in a Borromean structure, ensuring that breaking, guessing, or removing any one component collapses the entire keystream, providing a computational (AoNT) interdependence under standard cryptographic assumptions. A chameleon trapdoor hash is integrated to permit controlled collisions, enabling seamless rekeying, (V ) refresh, and state rotation without resynchronizing endpoints. This design provides confidentiality, forward secrecy, and adaptive key management with low computational overhead, making it suitable for high-throughput secure messaging, IoT communications, and privacy-preserving blockchain channels. Security analysis of the (BEC-Trap) shows that the construction is resistant to key-recovery attacks, state compromise, and desynchronization attempts, delivering a robust cryptographic primitive for next-generation secure communications.

</details>


### [157] [Beyond Distortion Robustness: Rethinking Severe Cropping as Erasure-Resilient Message Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18567)
*Bo Pang,Weibin Kong,Juntu Dong,Minghan Li,Zhongping Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CREST是一个基于擦除-鲁棒性（erasure-resilience）视角的框架，将LT喷泉编码与神经嵌入和碎片恢复相结合，解决图像中严重裁剪导致的鲁棒消息嵌入问题。实验表明其在严重裁剪和混合失真条件下显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒消息嵌入方法主要基于"失真鲁棒"范式，即嵌入信号在空间上仍然存在但被噪声、模糊或压缩降质。然而严重裁剪会直接移除载体本身的一部分，导致部分载荷消失而非单纯信号损坏，这是一个根本不同的挑战。现有方法对此类问题缺乏有效建模，因此需要从擦除鲁棒性的新视角重新审视鲁棒消息嵌入问题。

Method: CREST框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 通过LT喷泉编码将紧凑的QR消息扩展为冗余的空间载荷，引入编码理论层面的冗余；(2) 裁剪感知的嵌入机制，使得嵌入过程对裁剪区域具有鲁棒性；(3) 碎片恢复机制，利用冗余信息从保留下来的图像碎片中恢复完整消息。

Result: 在COCO、DIV2K和VOC2012数据集上的实验表明：(1) 在混合失真且面积保留率0.7的条件下，CREST将TRA从18.52%提高到68.45%，将EMR从13.88%降低到4.21%，优于最强基线；(2) 在COCO2017上，仅保留30-50%图像面积时CREST仍能达到48.55-65.12%的TRA，而所有基线均无法恢复消息。

Conclusion: 严重裁剪应被理解为擦除问题而非传统失真问题。将神经嵌入与编码理论相结合的联合设计是解决严重裁剪鲁棒消息嵌入的有效途径，为版权保护和内容溯源等多媒安全应用提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Robust message embedding in images is important for multimedia security applications such as copyright protection and content tracing. Existing methods are largely developed under a distortion robustness paradigm, where the embedded signal remains spatially present but is degraded by noise, blur, or compression. Severe cropping poses a fundamentally different challenge because it removes part of the carrier itself, causing partial payload disappearance rather than mere signal corruption. In this paper, we revisit robust message embedding from an erasure-resilience perspective and present CREST, a proof-of-concept framework for severe-cropping-robust embedding. CREST combines coding-theoretic redundancy with neural embedding and recovery by expanding a compact QR message into a redundant spatial payload via LT fountain coding and coupling it with cropping-aware embedding and fragment recovery. Experiments on COCO, DIV2K, and VOC2012 show that CREST improves recovery under severe cropping while maintaining competitive visual quality. Under mixed distortions with an area retention ratio of 0.7, CREST improves TRA from 18.52% to 68.45% and reduces EMR from 13.88% to 4.21% over the strongest baseline. On COCO2017, CREST still achieves 48.55--65.12% TRA when only 30--50% of the image area is retained, whereas all compared baselines fail to recover the message. These results suggest that severe cropping is better understood as an erasure problem rather than a conventional distortion problem, motivating the joint design of neural embedding and coding-based recovery.

</details>


### [158] [VQC-ZTI: Variational Quantum Control for Zero Trust Protection of the Tactile Internet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18572)
*Mubassir Serneabat Sudipto,Shakil Ahmed,Ashfaq Khokhar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出VQC-ZTI，一种基于分裂平面变分量子分类器的零信任防护框架，用于触觉互联网服务。该框架将异常评分（离路径VQC分析加密流遥测）与策略执行（在路径上的确定性动作缓存）解耦，在保持控制路径可预测性的同时提高风险判别能力。在CESNET衍生流量上的评估表明，全混合量子神经网络在三种数据划分下平均AUC达0.994-0.998，相比ExtraTrees将假阳性率降低了44.6%-67.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉互联网将网络事件直接耦合到物理执行，安全决策必须在不扰动控制路径的前提下提高风险判别能力。传统方案难以在满足触觉互联网实时性要求的同时实现有效的异常检测与零信任防护。

Method: 采用分裂平面架构：离路径的变分量子分类器（VQC）分析加密流遥测数据并对异常进行评分；在路径上的策略引擎应用缓存的确定性动作（授予、限制、升级、拒绝）。通过解耦异常评分与策略执行，可独立调节检测器灵敏度和策略攻击性。实现采用混合PyTorch-PennyLane框架，并在CESNET聚合流量上使用随机、实体组和时间三种划分进行评估。

Result: 全混合量子神经网络在三种划分下平均AUC分别为0.9981、0.9974和0.9941；相比ExtraTrees将假阳性率分别降低44.6%、49.6%和67.9%。组件时间分解表明批量VQC评分位于异步证据路径而非即时执行路径中。

Conclusion: VQC-ZTI通过量子分类器与策略引擎的分裂平面设计，在保持控制路径确定性与可预测性的同时，显著提升了零信任框架下的异常检测性能，验证了量子机器学习在触觉互联网安全应用中的可行性。

Abstract: Tactile Internet services couple cyber events directly to physical actuation, so security decisions must improve risk discrimination without perturbing the control path. This paper presents VQC-ZTI, a split-plane Variational Quantum Classifier framework for zero-trust protection of Tactile Internet services, in which an off-path VQC analyzes encrypted-flow telemetry while an on-path policy engine applies cached deterministic grant, restrict, step-up, and deny actions. By decoupling anomaly scoring from enforcement, VQC-ZTI preserves predictable control behavior and allows detector sensitivity and policy aggressiveness to be tuned independently. We evaluate the framework on CESNET-derived aggregated traffic using random, entity-group, and temporal holdouts with a hybrid PyTorch-PennyLane implementation. The full-hybrid Quantum Neural Network achieves mean areas under the receiver operating characteristic curve of 0.9981, 0.9974, and 0.9941 and reduces the false-positive rate relative to ExtraTrees by 44.6%, 49.6%, and 67.9%, respectively. A representative component-timing decomposition further illustrates that batched VQC scoring remains in the asynchronous evidence path rather than the immediate enforcement path.

</details>


### [159] [Finality Before Disclosure for Ledger Authenticators in the Quantum Random Oracle Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18605)
*Maja Lie,Benjamin Marsh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"账本认证器"（ledger authenticators）概念及LAEUF不可伪造性实验框架，用于分析基于公共账本（如区块链）的响应式授权协议的安全性。作者识别出两个条件性资源边界，并在量子随机预言机（QROM）模型下证明了公开最终确定目标集的安全性界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的签名方案仅依赖公钥、消息和可移植签名来授权状态转换，但现代公共账本日益依赖先前交易、最终确定的状态、时序和排序来授权。这种范式转变缺乏统一的安全模型来捕捉权威性认证的安全性特性，包括账本活性与认证安全性的分离、规范的转换新鲜度、自适应腐败、包含前暴露、审查和对抗性排序等问题。

Method: 作者引入账本认证器概念，并设计LAEUF（Ledger Authenticator Unforgeability）实验。该模型将认证安全性与账本活性分离，捕获规范转换新鲜度、自适应腐败、包含前暴露、审查和对抗性排序。他们识别两类条件性资源边界：（1）满足单事件条件时产生上下文一次性签名；（2）在可重绑定揭示类中，安全性要求计算性发布后不可采纳性。随后定义联合账本与量子随机预言机执行模型，其中量子状态跨经典最终化切割和账本上的预言机评估持续存在，并证明大小至多K的闭合最终确定目标集的安全性界限。

Result: 对于大小至多K的闭合最终确定目标集，作者证明了安全性界限为 3β_cut²+3c_co KQ²/2^λ+6ℓ/2^λ，其中β_cut考虑切割时已存在的新鲜打开。此外，一个提交-关闭-揭示（commit-close-reveal）认证器实例化了该框架，并获得了多用户生命周期QROM界限。

Conclusion: 该工作为基于账本的响应式授权认证提供了系统性的安全框架，明确了安全性所需的资源条件，并在后量子（QROM）设置下给出严格的可证明安全性界限，为设计安全的账本认证协议奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Public ledgers increasingly authorize state transitions using prior transactions, finalized state, timing, and ordering rather than only a public key, message, and portable signature. We introduce ledger authenticators and $\LAEUF$, an unforgeability experiment for reactive authorization protocols whose public judgment algorithm reads a finalized transcript. The model separates authentication safety from ledger liveness and captures canonical transition freshness, adaptive corruption, exposure before inclusion, censorship, and adversarial ordering. We identify two conditional resource boundaries. An authenticator satisfying our single event conditions yields a contextual one-time signature. Within our rebindable reveal class, safety requires computational post-disclosure non-admissibility. When precursor admission uses only public computation and ledger scheduling, this condition is enforced by closing the evidence eligible to use a disclosed credential. If newly constructed evidence remains admissible after disclosure, censoring the honest reveal gives a forgery. We then define a joint ledger and quantum random oracle execution model in which quantum state persists across classical finalization cuts and oracle evaluations made through the ledger are charged. For a closed finalized target set of size at most $K$, we prove the bound $3β_{\mathsf{cut}}^2+3c_{\mathsf{co}}KQ^2/2^λ+6\ell/2^λ$, where $β_{\mathsf{cut}}$ accounts for fresh openings already present at the cut. A commit, close, reveal authenticator instantiates the framework and obtains a multi-user lifetime QROM bound.

</details>


### [160] [IriSig-Spoof: A Real-World Benchmark for Time-Robust Satellite RF Fingerprinting and Spoofing Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18642)
*Shichang Guo,Yuanyu Zhang,Shuangrui Zhao,Ji He,Pinchang Zhang,Yulong Shen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出IriSig-Spoof，一个包含5.17百万条来自66颗Iridium卫星、跨越32天的真实射频指纹数据集，以及由软件定义无线电生成的室内外欺骗信号，并建立三个基准任务（时间鲁棒性、开放集识别、跨场景欺骗检测）以系统评估LEO卫星射频指纹识别方法的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星互联网日益成为关键通信基础设施，但其开放无线链路易受卫星身份伪造和信号欺骗攻击。射频指纹（RFF）技术利用发射机硬件瑕疵可提供防御，但现有卫星RFF方法缺乏统一的数据集和基准来支持时间维度、开放集和跨场景的可靠评估，导致其实际可靠性难以衡量。

Method: 构建真实世界的Iridium数据集（IriSig-Spoof），包含32天内从66颗卫星收集的517万条消息，以及室内和室外环境下SDR生成的欺骗信号。建立三个基准任务：(1)时间鲁棒性评估；(2)带未知信号拒绝的开放集RFF识别；(3)跨场景欺骗检测。使用多尺度注意力卷积神经网络（MACNN）作为基准模型进行实验。

Result: MACNN在时间鲁棒性实验中最佳配置达到97.75%的跨天平均准确率；在开放集评估中AUROC达到0.9715，同时发现有效的未知信号拒绝并不必然保证可靠的未知身份分配；跨场景实验在低假阳性率下显示出差异。

Conclusion: IriSig-Spoof为在时间变化和不断变化的攻击条件下评估鲁棒的卫星RFF方法提供了可复现的基准，揭示了不同评估维度下RFF方法的性能差异，为该领域的标准化评测奠定了基础。

Abstract: Low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite Internet is becoming critical communications infrastructure, yet its open wireless links remain vulnerable to satellite impersonation and signal spoofing. Radio frequency fingerprinting (RFF) offers a potential defense by exploiting transmitter-specific hardware imperfections manifested in received signals. However, the reliability of existing satellite RFF methods remains difficult to assess because no unified dataset and benchmark support temporal, open-set, and cross-scenario evaluation. To address this gap, we introduce IriSig-Spoof, a real-world Iridium dataset comprising 5.17 million messages collected from 66 satellites over 32 days, together with software-defined radio (SDR)-generated spoofing signals from indoor and outdoor settings. We further establish three benchmark tasks: temporal robustness evaluation, open-set RFF identification with unknown-signal rejection, and cross-scenario spoofing detection. Experiments using a multi-scale attention convolutional neural network (MACNN) show that temporal robustness varies across configurations, with the best configuration achieving 97.75% average cross-day accuracy. In open-set evaluation, MACNN achieves an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) of 0.9715, while showing that effective unknown-signal rejection does not necessarily ensure reliable identity assignment. Cross-scenario experiments reveal differences at low false-positive rates. IriSig-Spoof provides a reproducible basis for evaluating robust RFF methods under temporal variation and changing attack conditions.

</details>


### [161] [Improving LLM-Based SSH Honeypots Through Prompting and Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18686)
*Muris Sladić,Veronica Valeros,Eman Alibalić,Sebastian Garcia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究如何使用提示设计和监督微调来提升本地开源LLM驱动的SSH蜜罐的shell模拟准确性，通过对8个模型（原始GPT-3.5及7个本地开源模型）的微调和34项自动化单元测试评估，发现提示设计影响显著、微调效果依赖数据集覆盖度，且二者效果不能简单叠加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的SSH蜜罐通常依赖闭源云LLM获取逼真的shell交互效果，但云模型存在版本不稳定、提供商侧变更、攻击者驱动的成本以及模型停用等部署问题。本地开源模型虽能避免这些问题，但性能较差且容易暴露蜜罐身份。因此需要研究如何改进和评估本地模型的shell模拟准确性。

Method: 采用两种技术手段：提示设计（prompt design）和监督微调（supervised fine-tuning）。共微调并评估8个模型，包括shelLM中使用的原始GPT-3.5模型和7个开源本地模型（均与其基础模型对比）。同时测试提示结构跨模型族的可迁移性。使用34个自动化单元测试，在单会话和新会话两种设置下衡量shell模拟准确性。

Result: 发现提示设计对结果有显著影响；微调效果取决于数据集覆盖度。基于原始112个会话的数据集进行微调未能提升整体通过率，而基于蜜罐日志扩展的数据集则能明显增强本地模型性能。提示设计和微调各自都能独立改善本地LLM蜜罐，但两者效果不会简单叠加，因为强基于规则的提示与监督适应可能因处理重叠的shell行为约束而产生冲突。

Conclusion: 提示设计和微调均可独立提升本地LLM蜜罐的shell模拟效果，但需要仔细协调二者的使用，因为它们处理重叠的约束时可能互相冲突。构建更大规模、覆盖面更广的训练数据集是提升本地模型性能的关键路径，而闭源云模型的不可靠性促使本地化部署成为值得探索的方向。

Abstract: LLM-based SSH honeypots often use closed cloud LLMs because they give strong shell realism, but cloud models create deployment problems. These include no stable versioning, provider-side changes, attacker-driven cost, and model decommissioning. Local open-weight models avoid these problems, but they usually perform worse and make mistakes that reveal the honeypot. These mistakes include malformed outputs, command echoing, inconsistent filesystem state, and AI-style artifacts. This paper studies how to improve and evaluate the shell emulation accuracy of local LLM-based SSH honeypots using prompt design and supervised fine-tuning. We fine-tune and evaluate eight models in total: the original fine-tuned GPT-3.5 model used in shelLM and seven open-weight local models, each compared to its base model. We also test how prompt structure transfers across model families. Using 34 automated unit tests that measure shell emulation accuracy in single-session and fresh-session settings, we find that prompt design has a large effect and that fine-tuning depends on dataset coverage. Fine-tuning on the original 112-conversation dataset does not improve aggregate pass rate, while an expanded dataset built from honeypot logs produces clearly stronger local models. Taken together, the results suggest that prompting and fine-tuning can each improve local LLM honeypots on their own, but their effects do not combine straightforwardly, since strong rule-based prompting and supervised adaptation can also conflict by addressing overlapping shell-behavior constraints.

</details>


### [162] [CauSec: Unboxing the Causal Drivers of Static Vulnerability Analysis Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18876)
*Md Akram Khan,Daniel Rodriguez-Cardenas,Alejandro Velasco Dimate,Denys Poshyvanyk,Adwait Nadkarni*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CAUSEC框架，将SAST工具的设计假设形式化为可测试的安全假设，并通过因果建模与效应估计来验证假设的有效性，从而回答"牺牲检测能力是否真的换来性能提升"这一核心问题。作者通过对57个假设的系统文献综述和4个主流工具的实验验证，得出3条关键结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAST工具广泛使用，但常常通过牺牲检测能力（如降低精度、减少运行时间、提升可扩展性）来换取性能提升。这些设计选择依赖于对目标代码或分析技术的隐式假设，而假设的有效性直接影响检测结果。目前缺乏系统的方法来验证这些假设是否真实成立——即牺牲检测能力是否真能带来预期的性能收益。

Method: CAUSEC框架包含三个核心环节：(1) 将SAST工具的设计假设形式化为"安全假设"（security assumption）抽象；(2) 采用假设驱动的因果建模（assumption-driven causal modeling），结合效应估计（effect estimation）和验证（validation）来检验假设的有效性并分析影响因素；(3) 通过对检测crypto-API误用的SAST工具进行系统文献综述，发现并定性分析57个假设，随后在4个相关工具上使用包含57,038条告警的人工标注数据集验证一个流行假设。

Result: 系统文献综述揭示了57个安全假设，并在4个主流工具的实验中测试了一个流行假设。分析产生了关于假设有效性及其因果效应的关键发现，最终提炼为3条对未来工作的启示（takeaways）。

Conclusion: SAST工具的性能妥协所依赖的假设并非总是有效，CAUSEC提供了一个可测试因果框架来验证这些假设，帮助研究人员和实践者理解检测能力与性能之间的真实权衡关系。

Abstract: Static Application Security Testing (SAST) tools are widely used in both industry and academia. Such tools often make design choices that sacrifice detection to achieve higher performance, i.e., increased precision, decreased runtime, or increased scalability. These design choices rely on certain assumptions regarding the target code or the analysis technique itself. Hence, the assumptions directly impact the detection outcome through the design choices they influence. This motivates a key question: do the sacrifices in the detection capabilities actually help tools achieve the expected performance gains? That is, are the underlying assumptions valid?
  This paper seeks to address this question by relying on a key observation that the assumptions made by these tools are generally of a causal nature. We propose CAUSEC, a causal analysis framework that makes SAST assumptions testable and explains why the performance changes given certain assumptions, beyond simple correlations. CAUSEC formalizes the assumptions of the SAST tool into the abstraction of a security assumption and combines assumption-driven causal modeling with effect estimation and validation to test its validity and investigate the factors affecting it. To understand what security assumptions generally entail, we perform a systematic literature review of SASTs that detect crypto-API misuse, leading to the discovery and qualitative analysis of 57 assumptions. We then demonstrate the utility and robustness of CAUSEC by testing a popular assumption in four highly relevant tools, using a manually labeled ground truth dataset consisting of 57,038 alerts. Our analysis leads to several key findings that represent insights regarding assumptions and causal effects, which we distill into 3 takeaways for future work.

</details>


### [163] [Who Can Make the Action Happen? An Authority-Decomposition Framework for High-Risk Automated Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18965)
*Mengting Wu,Lin Wang,Yong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向动作（action-relative）的方法，用于推导哪些信任域联盟足以引发受保护执行（即特定受保护状态转换的发生）。该方法建模组件、权力、资源、边界和替代实现结构，涵盖更新、恢复、覆盖、禁用和替代调用等机制，并区分对执行的因果控制与对操作权威记录的控制。通过跨域分析案例（如Havenlon协议）验证，该方法可推导出包含最小充分联盟，并检验声明的执行边界是否独立于指定上游域。作者明确该框架是概念与分析工具，不认证实现或保证部署安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的"授权""已批准""特权""受保护"等标签无法回答一个基本因果问题：哪些行为者实际上能够使某个重大动作发生？在高风险自动化系统中，控制权分散于服务、凭证、受保护组件和生命周期机制之间，需要一种方法来准确识别对受保护执行的因果控制权。

Method: 提出基于动作（action-relative）的框架，建模组件、权力、资源、边界和替代实现结构；纳入更新、恢复、覆盖、禁用和替代调用机制；将执行控制与操作的权威记录控制分离；推导包含最小充分联盟（inclusion-minimal sufficient coalitions），并测试声称的执行边界是否独立于指定上游域。通过跨域分析案例（Havenlon协议，包括split-control、release-intended、open-state、source-bounded模型）进行验证。

Result: 在Havenlon协议模型中，普通见证者需要五个信任域，而证书替换产生一个包含三个域的已知最小需求集（在源枚举协议见证者中）；Linux域对完整转换仍不充分。已部署的全局不可绕过性和边界否决覆盖仍未解决。

Conclusion: 该框架是一种概念与分析工具，可帮助推导信任域联盟与受保护执行之间的因果充分性关系。但它不认证实现、不建立部署安全、不保证完全发现隐藏权力，也不定义证据验证语义，存在明确的应用边界与局限性。

Abstract: High-risk automated systems distribute control across services, credentials, protected components, and lifecycle mechanisms. Labels such as authorized, approved, privileged, or protected therefore do not answer a basic causal question: which actors can actually make a consequential action occur? This paper provides an action-relative method for deriving which trust-domain coalitions are sufficient to cause protected execution, defined as the occurrence of a designated protected state transition. The framework models components, powers, resources, boundaries, and alternative realization structures; includes update, recovery, override, disablement, and alternative invocation; and separates causal control over execution from control over the authoritative account of an operation. It derives inclusion-minimal sufficient coalitions and tests whether claimed execution boundaries remain independent of designated upstream domains. Cross-domain analytical cases illustrate the method. In a split-control, release-intended, open-state, source-bounded Havenlon protocol model, the ordinary witness requires five trust domains, while certificate replacement yields a three-domain inclusion-minimal known requirement set among source-enumerated protocol witnesses; the Linux domain remains insufficient for the complete transition. Deployed global non-bypassability and boundary-bound veto coverage remain unresolved. The framework is a conceptual and analytical tool. It does not certify implementations, establish deployment security, guarantee complete discovery of hidden powers, or define evidence-verification semantics.

</details>


### [164] [Catastrophic Learning: A New Attack Vector on Continual Learning Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18976)
*Benedikt Kluss,Niklas Bunzel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现了一种新的持续学习安全漏洞——"学习阻断器"（learning blockers），攻击者通过操纵数据降低模型当前或未来迭代的可学习性（破坏可塑性），而非传统地重新引发灾难性遗忘。作者形式化该场景，提出威胁模型和六种攻击策略，并在MNIST和Split-CIFAR10上对三种CL策略进行超过4,480次模拟评估，证明攻击者可以同时损害模型获取新知识和保持旧知识的能力，导致"灾难性学习"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对持续学习（CL）的对抗性研究主要集中于重新引发灾难性遗忘（攻击稳定性/降低可用性）。作者识别出一个新的安全缺陷：攻击者可通过操纵数据降低当前或未来迭代的可学习性（攻击可塑性），这类攻击难以在训练过程中被检测，因为它们可以针对模型尚未遇到的数据的迭代。这种攻击与灾难性遗忘叠加将导致"灾难性学习"（catastrophic learning）。

Method: 作者形式化了学习阻断器场景并定义了威胁模型，提出六种攻击策略：Label-Exchange、Tensor-Exchange、Attraction-Coincident、Attraction-Preceding、Repulsion-Coincident和Repulsion-Preceding。Attraction变体通过最小化投毒迭代与受害迭代标签之间的损失，在特征空间中将它们的表示拉近；Repulsion变体则最大化该损失，将其推远，使稳定性机制抵抗所需的参数偏移。Coincident变体中投毒迭代与受害迭代重合，仅使用干净参考迭代作为标签来源；Preceding变体中投毒迭代先于受害迭代，通过扭曲表示使其不可学习。

Result: 在MNIST和Split-CIFAR10上，针对DER、ER-ACE和iCaRL三种CL策略进行了超过4,480次模拟。实验结果显示出强漏洞：攻击者可以选择性地阻碍可塑性以妨碍新知识的获取，同时促进旧知识的丢失，从而诱导灾难性学习场景。

Conclusion: 持续学习模型存在一个此前未被探索的安全漏洞——学习阻断器，它攻击的是CL的可塑性而非稳定性。攻击者可以在不触发当前迭代可检测异常的情况下，通过数据操纵系统性削弱模型的学习能力，并与灾难性遗忘协同作用造成整体的"灾难性学习"。这提示CL安全研究需要同时关注稳定性和可塑性两个维度。

Abstract: Continual Learning (CL) enables deep learning models to iteratively learn from a stream of data without forgetting prior knowledge. Existing adversarial research on CL primarily aims to re-enable catastrophic forgetting, attacking stability and reducing availability. We identify a novel security flaw: data manipulated by an attacker can reduce the learnability of current or upcoming iterations. We term such manipulations learning blockers, as they attack the plasticity of CL algorithms. They are particularly harmful because they are difficult to detect during training of the current iteration, since they can target iterations whose data the model has not yet encountered. When learning blockers additionally induce catastrophic forgetting, the resulting overall degradation is what we call catastrophic learning. We formalize this scenario, define a threat model and propose six attack strategies: Label-Exchange, Tensor-Exchange, Attraction-Coincident, Attraction-Preceding, Repulsion-Coincident, and Repulsion-Preceding. The Attraction variants minimize the loss between the poisoned and the victim iteration label, pulling their representations together in feature space; the Repulsion variants maximize this loss, pushing them apart so stability mechanisms resist the required parameter shift. In the Coincident variants, the poisoned and the victim iteration coincide, using a clean reference iteration only as a label source; in the Preceding variants, the poisoned iteration precedes the victim, leaving it unlearnable due to distorted representations. We evaluate on MNIST and Split-CIFAR10 against three CL strategies - DER, ER-ACE, and iCaRL - across more than 4,480 simulations. Our results demonstrate a strong vulnerability: an adversary can selectively impede plasticity to hinder the acquisition of new knowledge, while promoting loss of prior knowledge, inducing a catastrophic learning scenario.

</details>


### [165] [A 12-Step Process for Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18991)
*Victor Kebande*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对工业物联网（IIoT）环境下的数字取证挑战，提出了一套十二步取证流程（TSP），从取证准备到调查结案，确保潜在数字证据（PDE）的采集、分析及法律合规性，以提升证据可采性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IIoT在制造、医疗、交通等关键基础设施中的广泛部署带来了新的数字取证挑战，传统取证方法论无法有效应对IIoT环境的复杂性、规模性和异构性，亟需一套专门针对IIoT事件的取证流程。

Method: 论文提出一个综合性的十二步流程（Twelve-Step Process, TSP），每一步从取证就绪（forensic readiness）到调查收尾（investigation closure）均针对IIoT环境定制设计，涵盖潜在数字证据（PDE）的收集、分析与法律合规性保障。

Result: 该流程旨在确保IIoT事件中PDE的稳健采集与分析，并通过法律合规设计提高证据在数字取证场景中的可采性（admissibility）概率。

Conclusion: 论文认为引入针对IIoT定制的十二步取证流程能够弥补传统DF方法在IIoT动态环境中的不足，为工业物联网事件的数字取证调查提供系统化解决方案。

Abstract: The increasing deployment of the Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) in critical infrastructure sectors like manufacturing, healthcare, and transportation has shown new challenges for Digital Forensics (DF). Traditional DF methodologies are not well equipped to handle the complexity, scale, and heterogeneity of IIoT environments. This paper introduces a comprehensive Twelve-Step Process (TSP) tailored specifically for IIoT incidents, addressing the need for effective investigation and Potential Digital Evidence (PDE) handling in such dynamic environments in DF. We begin by exploring the importance of IIoT and its role in industrial ecosystems, followed by an examination of existing DF challenges. Each step of the process, from forensic readiness to investigation closure, is designed to ensure robust PDE collection, analysis, and legal compliance to increase chances of admissibility from a DF scenario

</details>


### [166] [From Threat Intelligence to Detection: Knowledge-driven Enrichment and Template-based Rule Grounding for Automated Sigma Rule Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19011)
*Sepehr Ghaffarzadegan,Boubakr Nour,Makan Pourzandi,Mourad Debbabi,Chadi Assi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AUTOSIGMA，一个自动将非结构化网络威胁情报（CTI）报告转化为可执行的Sigma规则的解决方案。它结合知识库驱动的信息增强、基于模板的规则落地和LLM-as-a-Judge多阶段验证，在真实APT报告和多个安全博客上的评估中，在规则有效性、相关性、MITRE ATT&CK技术覆盖率和鲁棒性方面均优于现有方案和纯LLM模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高级持续性威胁（APT）快速演化，亟需将CTI动态转化为可检测能力。传统手工编写Sigma规则易出错且需专业知识，难以规模化；现有开源规则库无法及时跟上新威胁，且需针对不同环境频繁定制。因此需要一种能适应不断演化的攻击技术及特定用例的动态规则生成机制。

Method: AUTOSIGMA采用三阶段架构：(1) 利用结构化知识库对不完整的CTI输入进行信息增强；(2) 将增强后的内容与现有Sigma规则库进行匹配，基于模板进行规则落地（grounding）；(3) 采用LLM-as-a-Judge机制对生成的规则进行迭代验证与优化，确保规则准确、上下文相关且有效。

Result: 在多个真实世界APT报告和多个安全博客上进行了全面评估，结果显示AUTOSIGMA在规则有效性、规则相关性、MITRE ATT&CK技术覆盖率以及对输入质量的鲁棒性等指标上均优于替代方案和纯LLM模型。

Conclusion: 通过结合知识驱动的增强、模板驱动的规则落地和多阶段验证机制，AUTOSIGMA能够实现准确、上下文感知且高度相关的Sigma规则自动生成，有效解决了传统手工规则编写和静态规则库难以跟上APT威胁演化的问题。

Abstract: Mechanisms for dynamically converting cyber threat intelligence (CTI) into actionable detection capabilities are necessary due to the rapid evolution of Advanced Persistent Threats (APTs). Sigma rules are an essential part of contemporary threat detection workflows because they offer a platform-independent framework for expressing detection logic that can be converted into particular queries across SIEM systems. Conventional techniques for manually crafting Sigma rules are prone to mistakes, and necessitate extensive knowledge, which restricts their scalability. Although there are open-source and industry-maintained Sigma rule repositories, they often fail to keep pace with emerging threats and require frequent customization to fit diverse operational environments. This emphasizes the necessity of dynamic rule generation that is adapted to evolving attack techniques as well as particular use cases. In this work, we design AUTOSIGMA, an automated solution for transforming unstructured CTI reports into relevant Sigma rules. Rather than relying solely on language models, AUTOSIGMA leverages a structured knowledge base to enrich partial inputs, matches the enriched content against a repository of existing Sigma rules, and then employs an LLM-as-a-Judge mechanism to iteratively validate the rules. By combining knowledge-driven enrichment, template-based rule grounding, and a multi-stage solution, AUTOSIGMA enables accurate, context-aware, and relevant rule generation. Evaluations across multiple real-world APT reports and multiple security blogs demonstrate that AUTOSIGMA outperforms alternative solutions and LLM models in rule validity, rule relevancy, MITRE ATT&CK technique coverage, and robustness to input quality.
  AUTOSIGMA's Demo: https://youtu.be/iSr6IurQ6BM

</details>


### [167] [Malformer: A Multi-Modal Malware Detector Using Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19052)
*Samuel Howard,Kshitiz Aryal,Mahmoud Abdelsalam,Maanak Gupta,Andrew Wheeler,Pradip Kunwar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Malformer是一个四模态（文本、图像、图、音频）恶意软件检测模型，通过多模态Transformer融合，在201,549个Windows可执行文件样本上达到98.3%准确率和0.9833的F1分数，较单模态和双模态检测器提升4.6-17.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统恶意软件检测系统仅依赖单一表示（模态）来识别恶意软件，往往无法识别新型威胁，且单一表示所提供的信息不足以区分所有样本。此外，个别表示会引入新的失败模式，例如某些模态的提取依赖于反汇编是否成功。过去的尝试要么增加模态数量，要么使用更具判别力的表示，但尚未充分利用多模态融合的优势。

Method: Malformer采用四模态架构：两个RoBERTa编码器分别处理文本和图模态（图可能以文本序列表示），一个改进的Vision Transformer处理图像模态，WavLM处理音频模态，并通过自适应损失加权方案融合各模态特定表示，构建多模态Transformer融合的恶意软件检测模型。

Result: 在201,549个二进制样本的数据集上，Malformer达到98.3%的准确率和0.9833的F1分数，优于所有单模态基线和双模态检测器，性能提升幅度为4.6至17.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 多模态融合为应对日益增长的恶意软件威胁提供了有前景的检测基础，赋予防御者更通用且更具韧性的检测能力。

Abstract: Traditional malware detection systems that rely on a single representation of malware often fail to identify novel threats. These representations of malware binaries, also known as modalities, do not provide the models with sufficient information to discriminate among all samples. Additionally, individual representations introduce new failure modes, with some modality extraction being dependent upon the success of disassembling. Past works have integrated either additional modalities or more discriminative representations for classification. In this work, we present Malformer, a quadrimodal malware detection model that incorporates text, image, graph, and audio representations of Windows executables. We demonstrate that multimodal transformer fusion can enhance the performance of Windows malware detectors over that of unimodal and bimodal detectors. Malformer employs a combination of two RoBERTa encoders paired with a modified Vision Transformer for image data, WavLM for audio data, and an adaptive loss-weighting scheme to fuse modality-specific representations. Evaluated on a dataset of 201,549 binary samples, Malformer achieved 98.3% accuracy and an F1 score of 0.9833, outperforming both unimodal baselines and bimodal detectors by 4.6-17.6 percentage points. Malformer demonstrates that multimodal fusion provides a promising foundation for countering the growing scale of malware threats, equipping defenders with generalized and resilient detection capabilities.

</details>


### [168] [Toward Quantum Advantage in Learning Parities with Structured Noise via Lower Bound Optimization of the Condition Number](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19122)
*Yusen Han,Xuelian Li,Juntao Gao,Bo Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对Macaulay线性系统的量子求解新约简方法，通过推导包含缩放因子的条件数下界，优化了求解布尔系统的量子算法的时间复杂度上界，并进一步将该算法应用于带结构噪声的奇偶学习（LPSN）问题，显著降低了样本复杂度，通过量子资源估算和量子-经典算法对比验证了其在特定参数区间下的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算中求解非线性布尔系统通常需要将其转化为Macaulay线性系统并通过量子线性系统算法求解，但该过程严重受限于条件数。现有方法在条件数控制方面存在瓶颈，亟需一种新的约简方法来降低条件数下界，从而优化量子算法的时间复杂度和资源开销。

Method: 提出了一种针对Macaulay线性系统的新型约简方法。在Ding等人的假设下，推导了包含缩放因子的条件数下界；利用该约简保证高效的量子态制备；利用Macaulay系统的解结构降低LPSN问题的样本复杂度；并通过逻辑级量子资源估算和系统的量子-经典算法对比建立算法选择策略。

Result: 该约简方法在条件数区间上展现出相对约简右端向量的显著优势，降低了条件数下界并优化了量子算法的时间复杂度上界；应用于LPSN问题后显著降低了样本复杂度；逻辑级资源估算表明优化后的条件数直接转化为电路宽度、深度和门数量的减少；对比分析显示在特定参数区间量子算法有超越经典算法的潜力。

Conclusion: 所提出的Macaulay线性系统约简方法有效优化了量子求解布尔系统的条件数控制，并在LPSN问题上实现了样本复杂度的降低和量子资源的缩减，在特定参数区间具有超越经典算法的潜力，为量子算法在结构化噪声学习问题中的实际应用提供了理论和资源层面的支撑。

Abstract: Learning Parities with Structured Noise (LPSN) can be reduced to solving nonlinear Boolean systems. In quantum computing, such systems are typically transformed into Macaulay linear systems and solved via quantum linear system algorithms, a process severely limited by the condition number. To address this, we propose a novel reduction method for Macaulay linear systems. Under the assumptions of Ding et al., we derive a condition number lower bound incorporating a scaling factor.
  This reduction not only guarantees efficient quantum state preparation but also exhibits a distinct advantage regarding the condition number interval relative to the reduced right-hand side vector, thereby reducing the lower bound of the condition number and ultimately optimizing the upper bound on the time complexity of the quantum algorithm for solving Boolean systems.
  Furthermore, applying this improved quantum algorithm to LPSN significantly reduces sample complexity by exploiting the Macaulay system's solution structure. We further provide a concrete logical-level quantum resource estimate, demonstrating that the optimized condition number translates directly into a reduction in circuit width, depth, and gate count. Finally, we establish an algorithm selection strategy by systematically comparing quantum and classical approaches across noise pattern adaptability, sample complexity, and time complexity. Results demonstrate that our quantum algorithm exhibits the potential to outperform classical counterparts under specific parameter regimes.

</details>


### [169] [Autonomous Cyber Defense in Connected Vehicles: A Multi-Agent Approach to V2X Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19135)
*Krishna Teja Medam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种三层多智能体架构，用于在100毫秒的V2X基本安全消息处理时限内检测虚假消息，将时间约束作为硬性设计需求而非性能指标。车载层在10ms内对消息做出接受/丢弃/隔离/升级四类决策，边缘层在50ms内融合多车威胁评估并解决安全-安全冲突，云端通过拜占庭容错联邦学习精炼检测模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有入侵检测系统按单车、单消息、静态规则运行，无法处理安全系统与安保系统耦合的问题——虚假紧急制动警报可能触发真实刹车。它们对跨车队或跨时间出现的攻击模式视而不见，也无法在丢弃可疑消息与丢弃真实紧急警报之间做出恰当权衡。

Method: 提出三层多智能体架构：1）车载层在10ms预算内将每条V2X消息分类为Accept、Drop、Quarantine、Escalate四种动作，不确信时偏向升级而非丢弃；2）边缘层在50ms预算内跨路侧单元区域融合多车威胁评估，利用互补传感器观察解决安全-安全冲突；3）云端通过拜占庭容错联邦学习精炼检测模型并分发更新权重。所有时限直接源自SAE J2735和ETSI EN 302 637-2规定的100ms基本安全消息周期。

Result: 该框架是首个同时为三个部署层级赋予基于标准的时间预算，并将安全-安全冲突解决作为一流设计约束的机制。剩余开放问题包括边缘端的对抗性投毒以及自主安全响应缺乏监管框架。

Conclusion: 本文的核心贡献在于将100ms消息周期作为跨车载、边缘、云端三层的端到端硬性时间约束，通过不确定时升级的策略避免误丢合法警报，并以联邦学习实现分布式检测模型更新，为V2X安全检测提供了标准驱动的分层设计范式。

Abstract: A connected vehicle has roughly 100 milliseconds to decide whether an incoming Basic Safety Message is real or fabricated. If a false emergency braking alert reaches the planning pipeline in time, the car brakes - a safety failure triggered by a security failure. Existing intrusion detection systems are not designed to handle that coupling. They operate per vehicle, per message, with static rules - blind to attack patterns that only emerge across a fleet or over time, and blind to the fundamental tension between dropping a suspicious message and dropping a real emergency alert. We propose a three-tier multi-agent architecture that treats this timing constraint as a hard design requirement, not a performance target. At the vehicle level, an onboard agent classifies each incoming V2X message into one of four actions - Accept, Drop, Quarantine, or Escalate - within a 10-millisecond budget, deliberately biased toward Escalate when uncertain, passing ambiguous cases to the roadside edge agent rather than risking a dropped legitimate alert. The edge agent operates across a roadside unit zone with a 50-millisecond budget, fusing threat assessments from multiple vehicles and resolving safety-security conflicts using complementary sensor observations. The cloud tier refines detection models through Byzantine fault-tolerant federated learning and redistributes updated weights to the fleet. Every timing constraint derives directly from the 100-millisecond Basic Safety Message cycles mandated by SAE J2735 and ETSI EN 302 637-2. No existing framework simultaneously assigns standards-grounded latency budgets to all three deployment tiers while treating safety-security conflict resolution as a first-class design constraint. Remaining open problems - adversarial poisoning at the edge and the absence of regulatory frameworks for autonomous security response - are discussed as future work.

</details>


### [170] [FedGuard-DC: Privacy-Preserving Federated Load Forecasting and Cyber-Attack Detection for Data-Center Loads in Transmission Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19155)
*Md Kibria Saroare,Md Rubel Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出FedGuard-DC，一个基于联邦学习的隐私保护框架，用于大型数据中心（DC）的负载预测和本地虚假数据注入攻击（FDIA）检测。每个数据中心在本地训练双头模型（预测头+重构头），仅共享模型更新而不共享原始兆瓦测量数据，从而保护敏感的工作负载信息。框架在IEEE 39节点新英格兰系统上使用4个150-350 MW的数据中心负载的EMT仿真数据进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型数据中心负载快速增长给电力系统的可视性、隐私和网络物理安全带来挑战。系统运营商需要准确的短期负载信息，但数据中心运营商不愿分享原始兆瓦级测量数据，因为这可能泄露敏感的工作负载和使用模式信息。需要一种既能提供准确预测又保护隐私、同时能检测网络攻击的解决方案。

Method: 提出联邦学习框架FedGuard-DC：每个数据中心的本地模型包含一个共享编码器，支撑预测头和重构头两个输出头。局部异常分数结合预测残差和重构误差来检测被篡改的本地测量数据。原始测量和绝对MW需求数据保留在各数据中心本地，仅模型更新上传至全局控制器。可选差分隐私机制和鲁棒的裁剪均值聚合分别用于评估隐私-效用的权衡和抵抗中毒客户端攻击。

Result: 在IEEE 39总线新英格兰系统上基于4个150-350 MW的大型直流负载的EMT仿真数据验证：0.5秒提前预测的归一化RMSE为0.023-0.038 pu（持续性预测为0.32-0.34 pu）；FDIA检测的ROC-AUC为0.979，F1值为0.930，精度为0.988；鲁棒聚合将中毒客户端的RMSE影响从0.042降到0.035 pu。

Conclusion: FedGuard-DC成功实现了隐私保护的数据中心负载预测与本地FDIA检测，在准确性和安全性上均表现优异。该框架平衡了隐私保护和预测性能，并通过鲁棒聚合机制增强了对中毒客户端攻击的抵抗力。

Abstract: The rapid growth of large data-center (DC) loads is creating new challenges for power-system visibility, privacy, and cyber-physical security. System operators need accurate short-term information about these fast-varying loads, while DC operators may avoid sharing raw megawatt measurements because they can reveal sensitive workload and utilization patterns. This paper presents FedGuard-DC, a federated learning (FL) framework for privacy-preserving DC load forecasting and local false-data-injection attack (FDIA) detection. Each DC trains a dual-head model on its own measurements, where a shared encoder supports both a forecasting head and a reconstruction head. A calibrated anomaly score combines forecast residual and reconstruction error to detect corrupted measurements locally. Raw measurements and absolute MW demand remain at each DC, while only model updates are shared with the global controller. Optional differential privacy and robust trimmed-mean aggregation are included to evaluate privacy-utility behavior and poisoned-client resilience. The framework is validated using EMT simulation data from four large DC loads rated between 150 and 350 MW integrated into the IEEE 39-bus New England system. Results show a 0.5 s-ahead normalized forecast RMSE of 0.023-0.038 pu, compared with 0.32-0.34 pu for persistence. FedGuard-DC detects FDIA with ROC-AUC of 0.979, F1 = 0.930, and precision of 0.988, while robust aggregation reduces the poisoned-client RMSE impact from 0.042 to 0.035 pu.

</details>


### [171] [SiNMULI: Novel Signed Network Approach for Malicious URL Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19190)
*Avijit Gayen,Sayan Mondal,Angshuman Jana*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于符号网络（signed network）的恶意URL识别方法SiNMULI，将恶意URL检测建模为符号网络上的二分类问题，利用URL反向链接构建符号网络，并基于社会平衡理论的推理机制传播边符号和分类未标记域名，在真实数据集上达到99.89%的准确率，优于传统机器学习和深度学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习和深度学习方法在恶意URL检测中主要依赖静态数据分析，难以应对恶意网站快速演化带来的挑战，导致检测效果不佳。为此，需要一种能够适应恶意在线实体动态变化的新方法。

Method: 1) 基于外部超链接（反向链接）构建符号网络，节点代表URL，边代表超链接；2) 利用社会网络分析和社会平衡理论，通过平衡理论推理机制在网络中传播边的符号信息；3) 采用51%多数投票规则对未标记域名进行分类。

Result: 在真实数据集上，SiNMULI达到99.89%的准确率、99.62%的精确率和99.80%的F1分数，优于传统机器学习和深度学习基线模型。

Conclusion: SiNMULI不仅具有高精度，还具备可解释性、对抗混淆鲁棒性以及对训练数据的独立性，是一种轻量级、可扩展的真实世界网络防御解决方案。

Abstract: In today's era of rapid advancements in artificial intelligence, computer security and online safeguarding measures have undergone significant improvements. However, malicious websites continue to facilitate the spread of phishing schemes, fraudulent activities and unsolicited communications. Conventional methodologies in machine learning, deep learning and counterfeit website detection predominantly depend on static data analysis, which frequently proves ineffective against the evolving nature of malicious online entities. In response to these challenges, in this work, we propose a signed network-based approach for malicious URL identification, SiNMULI. We introduce an innovative framework that conceptualises the identification of harmful URLs as a signed network-based binary classification problem strongly rooted in the fundamental principles of social network analysis and social balance theory. In this approach, a signed network is constructed based on the backlinks, i.e., external hyperlinks of URLs, wherein each node symbolises a URL and the hyperlinks function as signed edges. Utilising a balance-theoretic inference mechanism, our methodology propagates edge signs and classifies unlabeled domains by employing a 51% majority rule across incoming links. Experimental results on this real-world dataset demonstrate that SiNMULI achieves 99.89% accuracy, 99.62% precision, and 99.80% F1-score, outperforming traditional ML and deep learning baseline models. Beyond high accuracy, SiNMULI offers interpretability, resilience against adversarial obfuscation, and independence from training data, making it a lightweight and scalable solution for real-world cyber defence.

</details>


### [172] [The Structured Totient Preimage Problem: Reconstruction, Collisions, and Cryptographic Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19191)
*Luis Adrián Lizama-Pérez*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文定义并研究了结构化欧拉函数原像（STP）问题，这是一种受限重建关系。作者证明了在x有分解时固定k的直接重建是多项式的，给出了穷举重建与碰撞算法，并基于28组参数对的广泛普查数据量化了STP的非单射性，最终提出结构化欧拉函数原像假设（STPA）作为候选原像抗性关系的计算基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STP问题源于密码学的直接动机：给定形如x=∏(p_i-1)的乘积（其中p_i是等比特长度的不同素数），重建这些素数本身在计算上是非平凡的。研究STP的逆向重建复杂度对于判断其能否作为密码学原像抗性关系的基础至关重要，特别是需要将其与一般逆欧拉函数问题区分开来。

Method: 论文采用三管齐下的方法：(1) 对已分解的x进行精确的组合分析，推导有序指数分配的数量并给出直接重建的多项式上界；(2) 设计穷举重建与碰撞分析算法；(3) 对28组参数对（2≤k≤5，λ≤16）进行系统性穷举普查（最大包含4,588,935个素数集合），量化碰撞参与率、最大重数及条件模糊度。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 当x有分解且Ω(x)=O(log λ)时，固定k的直接重建是多项式的，从而排除了该参数区域作为强硬度声明的基础；(2) 建立了穷举重建与碰撞分析算法；(3) 通过大规模普查量化了STP的非单射性特征，包括碰撞参与、最大重数和条件比特模糊度。这些结果将STP与一般逆欧拉函数计算区分开来，并为明确增长的参数族上的STP假设（STPA）提供了实证支持。

Conclusion: 论文确立了STP作为候选原像抗性关系的计算基础和参数约束，可用于承诺方案、乘法见证的知识证明和认证协议。但作者明确指出，论文不声称提供安全性规约或后量子硬度证明，这些密码学构造的完整安全性分析留待后续工作。

Abstract: We define and study the Structured Totient Preimage (STP) problem as a restricted reconstruction relation with a direct cryptographic motivation. Let $p_1,\ldots,p_k$ be distinct primes of the same bit length and reveal only $x=\prod_{i=1}^k(p_i-1)$. Given $(x,λ,k)$, STP asks for any set of $k$ distinct $λ$-bit primes satisfying this product. The relation is efficiently verifiable, but its reconstruction complexity is not known. We establish three concrete results. First, for factored $x$ we derive the exact number of ordered exponent allocations and a bound showing that direct reconstruction is polynomial for fixed $k$ when $Ω(x)=O(\logλ)$; this rules out that regime as a basis for a strong hardness claim. Second, we give exhaustive algorithms for reconstruction and collision analysis. Third, we exhaustively evaluate 28 parameter pairs, with $2\leq k\leq5$, up to $λ=16$ for pairs and 4,588,935 prime sets in the largest census. The data quantify non-injectivity through collision participation, maximum multiplicity, and conditional ambiguity in bits. These results isolate STP from general inverse-totient computation and motivate a Structured Totient Preimage Assumption for explicitly growing parameter families. Under such an assumption, STP becomes a candidate preimage-resistant relation whose implications for commitments, proofs of knowledge of multiplicative witnesses, and authentication can be stated precisely. The paper establishes the computational foundation and parameter constraints for those constructions; it does not claim a security reduction or post-quantum hardness.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [173] [Diffusion Models for High-Dimensional Clustered Data: Intrinsic-Dimension Adaptivity via Bayesian Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19067)
*Yuga Iguchi,Paul Fearnhead*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究扩散模型对多模态高维数据（由多个具有各自低维结构的簇组成的K-混合高斯分布）的自适应性，建立了两个理论结果：将去噪过程解释为动态贝叶斯分类器，证明后验类别概率在信噪比达到Θ(log(KD)/D)时高概率集中于单一簇；通过分别分析混合阶段和簇承诺阶段，证明KL误差界仅依赖于簇的最大本征维度（至多对数因子），优于环境维数界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型的经验成功激发了理论工作，包括定量误差界和定性分析（刻画去噪的不同阶段），但这两类工作此前是分离的。作者希望将二者统一，研究扩散模型对具有结构化几何的多模态高维数据的自适应性。

Method: 采用K-混合高斯分布作为捕捉簇结构和簇间分离的规范框架。方法包括：(1) 将混合分数据分器解释为簇间分数向量的后验加权平均，将去噪解释为动态贝叶斯分类器，证明后验类别概率的信噪比阈值；(2) 将去噪过程拆分为混合阶段和簇承诺阶段分别分析，推导KL误差界。

Result: 证明了后验类别概率在SNR达到Θ(log(KD)/D)时以高概率集中于单一簇；KL误差界与簇的最大本征维度线性相关（至多对数因子），即使K随D多项式增长也成立，优于环境维数相关界，将低维自适应性分析推广到具有异质近似低秩协方差的多模态分布。

Conclusion: 本文弥合了扩散模型定量误差界与定性相分析之间的鸿沟，证明去噪过程在簇承诺阶段可自动适应数据的本征低维结构，实现了对环境维数界的内在改进，为多模态高维扩散模型提供了更精确的理论保障。

Abstract: The empirical success of diffusion models in generative modelling has motivated theoretical work, including quantitative error bounds and qualitative analyses that characterise the different phases of denoising. We bring these two areas together by studying the adaptivity of diffusion models to the structured geometry of multimodal high-dimensional data that consists of multiple clusters in $\mathbb{R}^D$, each with its own low-dimensional structure, and inter-cluster separation depending on $D$. We employ $K$-mixture Gaussian distributions as a canonical framework to capture this geometry and establish two theoretical results. First, we interpret denoising as a dynamical Bayesian classifier: the mixture score is a posterior-weighted average of cluster-wise scores, and we show that, with high probability, the posterior class probabilities concentrate on a single cluster once the signal-to-noise ratio reaches the scale $Θ(\log (KD)/D)$. Second, by separately analysing the denoising process in its mixing and cluster-commitment phases, we prove that the KL error bound depends linearly on the maximum intrinsic dimension of a cluster, up to a logarithmic factor, even when $K$ grows polynomially with $D$. This improves on ambient-dimensional bounds and extends existing low-dimensional adaptivity analyses to multimodal distributions with heterogeneous, approximately low-rank covariances.

</details>


### [174] [Fair Multi-View Determinantal Coresets via Adaptive NEPv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18181)
*Richard Yi Da Xu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出公平多视图行列式点过程（fair multi-view DPP）选择问题，通过最大化"最弱视图"的对数行列式来实现兼顾多视图多样性。作者将非光滑目标平滑后松弛到Stiefel流形，导出一个规范不变的、权重依赖特征向量的非线性特征值问题，并设计了带阻尼和level shifting的自洽场（SCF）求解器，结合leverage-score筛选与公平局部改进进行舍入。该方法仅需特征映射内积，在冲突性视图合成实验和USPTO多模态协议上进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在商标策展等场景中，从大候选池中选择小而多样的子集需要同时平衡多种不相容的多样性概念（如语言描述与视觉徽标空间）。单个DPP核会在某一视图上掩盖失败，而平均核则把多视图松弛退化成普通单核谱问题，无法保证各视图的公平覆盖。

Method: 将多视图DPP选择形式化为最大化大小为k的子集在每个视图上的对数行列式中的最弱值。对非光滑目标进行平滑处理并松弛到Stiefel流形上，使每个离散子集都能精确嵌入。推导出平稳条件为一个规范不变的非线性特征值问题，其权重随特征向量自适应变化。据此设计带阻尼和level shifting的自适应SCF求解器，随后通过leverage-score筛选和公平局部改进对得到的子空间进行舍入，方法仅需特征映射内积。

Result: 在冲突性视图的合成实验上报告了结果，并指定了多模态USPTO协议。真实多模态数据结果需要对齐的徽标嵌入，本文版本不予声称。

Conclusion: 公平多视图DPP选择是一个新颖且有理论意义的问题，其放松形式缺乏单视图情况下的闭式谱解，需要专门设计的迭代求解器与舍入策略。该方法在合成实验中验证有效，真实商标数据实验因嵌入对齐限制留待后续版本。

Abstract: Selecting a small, diverse subset from a large candidate pool often means balancing several incompatible notions of diversity. In trademark curation, for instance, a subset should cover both the language used to describe marks and the visual space of their logos. A single determinantal point process (\DPP) kernel can hide failure in one view, and averaging kernels replaces the multi-view relaxation by an ordinary single-kernel spectral problem. We formulate \emph{fair multi-view determinant selection}: maximize the weakest per-view log determinant of a size-$k$ subset. We smooth this nonsmooth objective and relax it to the Stiefel manifold. The relaxation embeds every discrete subset exactly, but unlike its single-view counterpart it has no closed-form spectral solution in general. Its stationarity condition is a gauge-invariant nonlinear eigenvalue problem with eigenvector-dependent, view-adaptive weights. We derive an adaptive self-consistent-field (\SCF) solver with damping and level shifting, and round the resulting subspace by leverage-score screening followed by fair local refinement. The solver needs only feature-map products for each view. We report conflicting-view synthetic experiments and specify a multimodal USPTO protocol; the real-data multimodal results require aligned logo embeddings and are not claimed in this version.

</details>


### [175] [Sobolev Regularized Score Difference Estimation in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18237)
*Chenghan Xie,Jose Blanchet,Renyuan Xu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Sobolev正则化的统计一致且可扩展的分数差估计器，用于生成建模中的分数差估计问题（如迁移学习和扩散模型后训练）。该方法在数学上提供了收敛率和极小极大下界，在经验上于小样本场景中表现出更好的稳定性，并在ECG信号生成的迁移学习任务中显著提升了下游分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 估计两个Stein分数函数的差值（分数差）是生成建模中的一个基础问题，在迁移学习和扩散模型的后训练方法（如判别器引导）中自然出现。然而，现有的分数差估计器要么缺乏统计一致性，要么难以在高维场景中扩展。这促使作者提出一种兼顾统计一致性与可扩展性的新方法。

Method: 提出基于Sobolev正则化的分数差估计器。Sobolev正则化在保证估计一致性方面起关键作用，同时在小样本条件下稳定训练过程。估计器支持高维扩展，并通过数学推导建立了收敛率为O(n^{-(s-1)/(d+2s-2)})的理论保证，其中d为维度、s为底层密度的光滑度。同时给出了极小极大下界Ω̃(n^{-2(s-1)/(d+2s)})。

Result: 理论上，建立了明确的收敛率并给出匹配的极小极大下界（在均方误差意义下）。经验上，该方法在小样本场景中相比现有方法展现出显著改善的稳定性。在实际任务中，特别是ECG信号生成的迁移学习任务，该方法在下游分类性能上大幅优于非正则化的分数差估计器。

Conclusion: 本文提出的Sobolev正则化分数差估计器在理论上兼具统计一致性和最优收敛速率，在实践上具备可扩展性和小样本稳定性，并在真实世界迁移学习任务中证明了其有效性，为分数差估计提供了一种可靠且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Estimating the difference of two Stein's score functions is a fundamental problem in generative modeling. In particular, score differences arise naturally in transfer learning, where the score difference provides the mechanism for adapting a pre-trained model to a new target distribution, and in diffusion model-based post-training methods such as discriminator guidance. Existing estimators for score differences in these settings either lack of statistical consistency or are difficult to scale up in high-dimensions. We propose a statistically consistent and scalable estimator for score differences based on Sobolev regularization, which plays a crucial role in ensuring consistency and stablizing the training in the small-sample regime. Mathematically, we establish a convergence rate of $O(n^{-\frac{s-1}{d+2s-2}})$ where $d$ is the dimension and $s$ denotes the smoothness of the underlying densities, and provide a minimax lower bound of $\tildeΩ(n^{-\frac{2(s-1)}{d+2s}})$ (in mean-squared error). Empirically, our estimator exhibits significantly improved stability in small-sample regimes compared to existing methods. We demonstrate its effectiveness on real-world tasks, including transfer learning for ECG signal generation, where it substantially outperforms non-regularized score difference estimators in downstream classification performance.

</details>


### [176] [Inference and Uncertainty Quantification for Streaming $r$-PCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18374)
*Haoshu Xu,Hongzhe Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文解决了Oja算法在流式PCA中的两个开放问题：一般秩下亚高斯数据的算子范数收敛速率，以及子空间估计量的分布推断。理论分析消除了残差项并获得尖锐收敛速率，在稠尾尖峰协方差模型中匹配极小极大速率（至多相差对数因子），并证明了匹配下界。该分析导出了Oja迭代的线性化形式，进而实现了一般秩子空间估计误差的高维高斯逼近和逐行高斯逼近，并开发了在线乘子自助法用于实际推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Oja算法收敛分析（即使在秩一情形）要么假设数据有界，要么留下不消失的残差项，导致无法适应多项式衰减的尾部谱；现有分布推断结果仅限于秩一情形。本文旨在解决这些开放性理论缺口。

Method: 本文通过消除收敛分析中的残差项获得尖锐的算子范数收敛速率，并在稠尾与稀疏尾尖峰协方差体制下证明（至多对数因子的）匹配下界。分析的关键是导出Oja迭代的线性化刻画，基于此建立一般秩子空间估计误差的高维高斯逼近（含显式极限协方差）、逐行高斯逼近，以及在线乘子自助法算法并证明其一致性。

Result: 获得了与极小极大速率匹配（至多对数因子）的尖锐收敛速率；建立了稠尾与稀疏尾体制下匹配下界；实现了一般秩子空间估计的高维高斯逼近和凸集上的逐行高斯逼近（恢复秩一情形为特例）；证明了在线乘子自助法的相合性。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅完善了流式PCA中Oja算法的收敛理论与分布推断理论，其技术贡献还延伸到非凸随机逼近的高斯逼近与自助法推断这一更广泛领域。

Abstract: We address two open questions in streaming PCA via Oja's algorithm: sharp operator-norm convergence for general rank under sub-Gaussian data, and distributional inference for the resulting subspace estimator. Existing convergence analyses, even in the rank-one case, either assume bounded data or leave non-vanishing remainder terms that prevent adaptation to a polynomially vanishing tail spectrum, while existing distributional results are confined to the rank-one case. Our convergence theory removes these remainder terms and yields a sharp rate. In the dense-tail spiked covariance regime, this rate matches the minimax rate up to logarithmic factors. More generally, we prove a matching lower bound, up to logarithmic factors, across both dense-tail and sparse-tail regimes under a mild nondegeneracy condition. The analysis yields a linearization of Oja's iterates, which in turn enables a high-dimensional Gaussian approximation for the general-rank subspace estimation error with an explicit limiting covariance. We also establish a row-wise Gaussian approximation over convex sets for the aligned difference, recovering prior rank-one results as special cases. For practical inference, we develop an online multiplier bootstrap algorithm and prove its consistency. Beyond streaming PCA, our techniques contribute to Gaussian approximation and bootstrap inference for nonconvex stochastic approximation.

</details>


### [177] [Sharper Regret Bounds for Time-Varying Gaussian Process Bandits with Constant Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18863)
*Matthias Mandl,Hanne Kekkonen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究时变环境中高斯过程漂移模型下的贝叶斯优化。通过使用逐轮局部置信事件，作者证明GP-UCB可以使用恒定探索参数运行，并获得期望遗憾上界，其系数取决于漂移率。同时推导了更尖锐的时变最大信息增益界，对平方指数核在持续漂移机制下得到$\tilde{\gamma}_T/T=\widetilde{\mathcal O}(\varepsilon^{1/2})$和期望平均遗憾$\widetilde{\mathcal O}(\varepsilon^{1/4})$的结果，并提供仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GP-UCB在时变环境中的分析通常要求探索参数随视界增长以维持统一置信界，这导致算法在实践中需要预先知道视界长度且探索参数设置不灵活。作者希望展示使用恒定探索参数即可获得良好的遗憾保证，从而简化算法实现并更符合实际部署需求。

Method: 关键思想是放弃传统的全局统一置信界，转而使用逐轮的局部置信事件（per-round local confidence events）。这使得GP-UCB可以在不扩大探索参数的条件下维持有效的不确定度控制。作者还推导了更尖锐的时变最大信息增益上界，用于分析平方指数核在持续漂移（persistent-drift）机制下的遗憾表现。

Result: 对于平方指数核，得到$\tilde{\gamma}_T/T=\widetilde{\mathcal O}(\varepsilon^{1/2})$以及期望平均遗憾界$\widetilde{\mathcal O}(\varepsilon^{1/4})$。相同的恒定探索分析也适用于实现遗憾（realized regret）保证。仿真实验验证了理论预测的探索参数与$1/\varepsilon$之间的对数依赖关系。

Conclusion: GP-UCB在时变高斯过程漂移环境下可以使用恒定探索参数运行，并取得与漂移率相关的遗憾界。这放宽了以往对探索参数必须随视界增长的要求，同时通过更尖锐的信息增益界获得了改进的遗憾收敛率，理论与仿真一致。

Abstract: We study Bayesian optimization in a time-varying environment where the unknown reward function evolves according to a Gaussian process drift model. Existing GP-UCB analyses in this setting typically require the exploration parameter to grow with the horizon to maintain uniform confidence bounds. Using per-round local confidence events, we show that GP-UCB can instead be run with a constant exploration parameter and obtain an expected-regret bound whose coefficient depends on the drift rate. We also derive a sharper time-varying maximum-information-gain bound. For the squared exponential kernel, it yields $\tildeγ_T/T=\widetilde{\mathcal O}(ε^{1/2})$ and expected average regret $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(ε^{1/4})$ in the persistent-drift regime. The same constant-exploration analysis also yields realized-regret guarantees. Simulations support the predicted logarithmic dependence of the bound-suggested exploration parameter on $1/ε$.

</details>


### [178] [Learning Random Geometric Graphs Drawn in Probabilistic Metric Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19082)
*Dalia Chakrabarty,Kangrui Wang,Chuqiao Zhang,Ye Liu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据驱动的随机几何图（RGG）学习方法，将图绘制在概率度量空间中。通过引入"差异变量"（disparity variable）的闭合形式累积分布函数作为距离函数，在概率空间中构建图，使得每条边以确定的概率存在（构成Soft RGG），并利用拒绝采样技术学习边的概率，期望度分布被识别为局部的且依赖于互观测相关矩阵，后者可通过闭合形式后验概率密度函数从数据中学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的随机几何图学习方法通常假设数据位于确定性的度量空间，难以处理通用数据集（不受观测量类型、概率分布或数据规模限制）。作者希望提出一种通用的数据驱动图学习方法，使图构建不依赖特定的数据分布假设，同时能天然地为每条边赋予一个存在概率。

Method: 作者引入一个"差异变量"（disparity variable）来度量图中两顶点连接性与对应两个随机变量之间相关性之间的差异。该变量的闭合形式cdf被用作宿主空间的概率距离函数，当节点间距离小于选定截止概率时则存在边。由于图绘制在概率空间中，所得图为Soft RGG，每条边以识别出的概率存在。作者提出基于拒绝采样（Rejection Sampling）的技术来学习任意边的概率，并推导出顶点期望度分布的局部性及其对互观测相关矩阵的依赖。若相关矩阵未知，可通过闭合形式的后验概率密度函数从数据中学习。

Result: 作者通过在高维真实数据集上学习多个RGG来演示该方法的有效性，展示了所提方法能够处理通用数据集并成功构建概率度量空间中的Soft RGG，实现每条边带概率的图表示。

Conclusion: 本文所提出的数据驱动RGG学习方法具有通用性，适用于任意类型的多变量数据集。通过概率度量空间和Soft RGG框架，图的每条边都携带明确的存在概率，且期望度分布具有局部性特征。该方法为多变量数据的图结构学习提供了一种新的、灵活且理论基础完备的工具。

Abstract: We present a new data-driven learning of a Random Geometric Graph (RGG) of a multivariate dataset, where the graph is drawn in a probabilistic metric space. This graph learning works for generic datasets, irrespective of the type of the observables; their probability distributions; or size of the data. We identify a metric of the space that the graph is drawn in, as a probability distribution of a random variable that we introduce, namely, a variable that represents the disparity between the connectedness of two vertices of the graph, and the correlation between the two random variables that are attached to the respective vertex. It is the closed-form {\it{cdf}} of this disparity variable that we advance as the distance function of the host space of the learnt RGG, such that the edge exists between any two nodes, if this inter-nodal distance falls short of a chosen cutoff probability. Drawing the RGG in this probabilistic space leads to the graph being an Soft RGG, such that any edge - if it exists - exists with an identified probability. We forward a simple Rejection Sampling-based technique for learning the probability of any edge. The expected degree distribution of a vertex of this RGG is identified as local, and dependent on the inter-observable correlation matrix. If said correlation matrix is not known, it can be learnt given the data, using its closed-form posterior probability density function, that we forward. We illustrate our graph learning method by learning multiple RGGs of highly multivariate real datasets.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [How far are $d$-dimensional copulas with uniform $(d-1)$-marginals from (total) independence?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18286)
*Damjana Kokol Bukovšek,Nik Stopar,Wolfgang Trutschnig*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究所有d维copula族C_d^{Π_{d-1}}，其中所有(d-1)维边际均等于乘积copula Π_{d-1}，并探索这些copula距d维乘积copula Π_d的最大距离。作者在一致度量d_∞和基于条件作用的更强度量D_1下给出了确定性答案，结果表明该copula族比预期更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一个自然且重要的问题是：当所有低维边际都是独立copula（即乘积copula）时，高维联合分布还能在多大程度上偏离独立性？这关系到对高维依赖结构的理解，揭示了低维边际信息对高维结构的约束程度。

Method: 作者考虑所有(d-1)维边际均等于Π_{d-1}的d维copula族C_d^{Π_{d-1}}，分别在一致度量d_∞和基于条件作用的度量D_1下，研究该族中元素与乘积copula Π_d的最大距离，给出精确的极值结果。

Result: 在两种度量d_∞和D_1下，作者均给出了C_d^{Π_{d-1}}中元素与Π_d距离的确定性定量答案（极值界限），并发现该copula族在结构上比通常预期的更为丰富和庞大。

Conclusion: 即使所有低维边际都表现为独立（乘积copula），高维copula仍然可以显著偏离d维独立性，这揭示了一个重要且非平凡的依赖结构空间，对高维Copula理论和建模有启示意义。

Abstract: We consider the family ${\mathcal{C}}_d^{Π_{d-1}}$ of all $d$-dimensional copulas whose $(d-1)$-dimensional marginals are all equal to the $(d-1)$-dimensional product copula $Π_{d-1}$ and tackle the natural question, `how far away' from the $d$-dimensional product copula $Π_d$ elements in ${\mathcal{C}}_d^{Π_{d-1}}$ can be. We provide definitive answers for both, the uniform metric $d_\infty$ as well as the stronger, conditioning-based metric $D_1$. The established results clearly indicate that the family ${\mathcal{C}}_d^{Π_{d-1}}$ is larger than one might expect.

</details>


### [180] [Algorithms for adaptive and heteroskedastic linear regression at the computational threshold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18402)
*Spencer Compton,Tselil Schramm*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了异方差和自适应线性回归在有限样本下的标签噪声问题。对于异方差回归，提出了一种多项式时间估计器，在m≫d^{3/4}n^{1/4}条件下达到Õ((nd^3/m^4)^{1/6})的速率，并给出近似匹配的下界。对于自适应回归，引入了一种计算上低效但统计上最优的自适应估计器（适用于k个对称对数凹密度的混合）。最后提出了种植线性回归问题，并猜想其存在信息-计算差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实数据中标签噪声往往是异方差的（不同样本质量不同）且分布未知，传统L1回归等方法在样本量较小时表现不佳。作者希望理解在这些噪声条件下的有限样本线性回归的基本困难程度，并设计高效的估计器。

Method: 1) 对异方差线性回归，设计了多项式时间估计器，利用样本质量差异的结构实现Õ((nd^3/m^4)^{1/6})速率，其中m为噪声方差σ_i²≤1的样本数。2) 对自适应线性回归，引入基于分布混合物假设的自适应估计器，通过对称对数凹密度混合实现与"知道p的最优估计器"相当的误差。3) 引入种植线性回归问题作为计算极限的理论框架，并结合统计查询（SQ）下界分析信息-计算差距。

Result: 异方差回归：当d=O(1)时，估计器在m≫n^{1/4}时达到o(1)误差，而L1回归等传统方法需要m≫n^{1/2}。自适应回归：对k个对称对数凹密度的混合，自适应估计器达到与最优估计器（拥有n/k样本）相当的误差；k=1时Lq回归（数据依赖的q）给出多项式时间算法。种植线性回归：提出猜想存在m=d+1到m~d^{3/4}n^{1/4}之间的信息-计算差距，由近匹配的估计器和SQ下界支持。

Conclusion: 本文系统刻画了异方差和自适应线性回归在噪声分布未知条件下的统计极限与计算极限，表明传统的L1回归等方法并非最优，而利用问题结构（如样本质量的分布）可以显著改进样本复杂度。种植线性回归问题为理解此类问题的信息-计算差距提供了重要框架。

Abstract: We study finite-sample linear regression in the presence of varied and unknown label noise, focusing on the heteroskedastic and adaptive linear regression models.
  Heteroskedastic linear regression models settings where the labels are of varying quality.
