While large language models (LLMs) have enabled automated reward generation, existing methods generate and revise reward functions as monolithic programs, making it difficult to reliably preserve and reuse effective components discovered in earlier iterations, leading to unstable performance across iterations. To address this, we propose Module Level Reward Evolution Framework (MLREF). At the core of MLREF is a module pool, a persistent repository of reusable reward components. MLREF treats the module pool as the primary optimization object: the pool evolves across iterations by accumulating successful modules, refining underperforming ones, and reusing proven components; while reward functions are constructed as linear combinations of modules drawn from this pool. To drive this evolution, MLREF integrates three mechanisms: reflection-based refinement, hybrid credit assignment, and a merge strategy with rollback, which together improve the effectiveness and robustness of reward optimization. Experiments on 17 tasks show that MLREF outperforms strong baselines by 25.2% in locomotion and 6.6% in manipulation, with more stable optimization dynamics.

</details>


### [249] [Multi-stage neural operator learning with application for convolutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18851)
*Zhiping Mao,Zhenye Wen,Yong Zhang,Xiaofei Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种多阶段神经算子学习框架用于卷积积分运算：DCNO（监督式，通过学习残差迭代细化算子逼近）和DGNO（无监督式，利用PDE弱形式残差训练），通过多阶段逐步构建基算子来丰富逼近空间，实现接近单精度机器精度的高精度卷积计算，并扩展到多输入场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卷积积分在众多应用中广泛存在，传统求解器在大量查询或参数变化场景下计算效率低下，因此需要既能快速又高精度计算卷积的算子学习方法。

Method: 提出两种多阶段神经算子学习框架：DCNO采用监督学习方式，通过输入输出数据对学习残差进行迭代细化；DGNO采用无监督学习方式，利用PDE弱形式残差进行训练，适用于目标算子可由PDE表示的场景。两种方法均通过多个训练阶段逐步构建基算子以丰富逼近空间，并提供理论近似能力分析。

Result: 大量数值实验表明，DCNO和DGNO在卷积问题中均能达到接近单精度浮点机器精度的准确性，相比传统求解器在众多查询或参数变化场景下具有显著效率优势，并成功扩展到密度和核同时变化的多输入算子学习场景。

Conclusion: 多阶段神经算子学习框架能够显著提升算子逼近精度（超越单次算子学习），在卷积计算中实现接近机器精度的高准确度和大规模查询下的计算效率，且具备可扩展性。

Abstract: Convolution integrals widely exist in applications, and to enable fast and accurate computations, this paper introduces two general multi-stage neural operator learning frameworks. The first, Deep Collocation Neural Operator (DCNO), is a supervised approach that iteratively refines the operator approximation by learning residuals from input-output data pairs. The second, Deep Galerkin Neural Operator (DGNO), is an unsupervised framework applicable when the target operator can be represented by a PDE, leveraging the weak form of the PDE residual for training. Both methods progressively construct basis operators through multiple training stages to enrich the approximation space, leading to significantly improved accuracy over standard one-shot operator learning. We provide theoretical analysis for their approximation capabilities and implement them for learning convolutions. Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate that both DCNO and DGNO achieve high accuracy, approaching machine precision under single float for convolution problems, and offer substantial efficiency gains for numerous queries or parametric variations compared to traditional solvers. We also extend these frameworks to handle multi-input operator learning scenarios involving variations in both the density and kernel of a convolution.

</details>


### [250] [Graph-Based Approaches to Learning Epileptogenic Zone Localization Using Stereo-EEG Recordings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18887)
*Daniel Wendelken,Brian Ervin,Ravindra Arya,Ali A. Minai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了图拓扑结构对基于静息态sEEG的致痫区（EZ）定位的影响。在40例患者中，通过控制每节点入边数来公平比较多种图构建方法，提出Region-Bridge-c拓扑，在约30%边保留率下达到最高PR-AUC（0.371±0.015），比Dense图少用约69%的边。结果表明图构建应被显式评估而非视为固定预处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 致痫区（EZ）是癫痫手术的靶区，从立体脑电图（sEEG）定位EZ对手术规划至关重要，但人工判读耗时且依赖发作期记录。基于静息态功能连接的图学习方法是一种有吸引力的替代方案，但性能严重依赖于所选的网络拓扑结构，而现有研究对此缺乏系统的对照评估。

Method: 在40例患者的静息态sEEG数据上进行受控的图模型对比研究：保持相同的简单可学习模型和留一患者交叉验证，系统比较稠密图、解剖/几何先验图、预算稀疏化方法以及可学习稀疏化方法，并提出了新的Region-Bridge-c拓扑。通过控制每节点入边数量来公平比较不同图构建方法，并系统变化图的稀疏度。

Result: 在约30%边保留率下，Region-Bridge-c达到最高的平均PR-AUC（0.371±0.015）和ROC-AUC（0.743±0.010），比Dense图（PR-AUC 0.349±0.014）少约69%的边。Spatial-k方法具有竞争力；随机剪枝需要接近稠密保留率才能有效；可学习稀疏化方法受益于解剖节点元数据，但平均表现未超过最佳固定先验。不同患者的首选拓扑各不相同。

Conclusion: 图拓扑结构对EZ定位性能有显著影响，且不同患者的最优拓扑选择存在差异。图构建不应被视为固定的预处理步骤，而应作为模型设计中的显式评估对象，同时应报告图选择策略以增强研究的可重复性和临床实用性。

Abstract: The epileptogenic zone (EZ) is the brain region that generates seizures in an individual, and is the target of epilepsy surgery. Localizing the EZ from stereo-EEG (sEEG) recordings supports surgical planning, but manual interpretation is time-consuming and focuses on seizure recordings. Graphical learning models of resting-state functional connectivity among the recorded brain regions are an attractive alternative, but depend crucially on the network topology chosen for the model.
  We present a controlled study of graph-based models to explore how graph topology affects EZ localization from resting-state sEEG in 40 patients. Using the same simple learnable model and leave-one-patient-out evaluation, we compare dense graphs, anatomy- and geometry-informed priors, budgeted sparsification methods, and learned sparsification, including the proposed Region-Bridge-$c$ topology. To compare graph constructions fairly, we control the number of incoming edges per node and vary graph sparsity.
  At $\approx 30\%$ edge retention, Region-Bridge-$c$ achieves the highest observed mean PR-AUC ($0.371\pm0.015$; ROC-AUC $0.743\pm0.010$) while using $\approx 69\%$ fewer edges than Dense (PR-AUC $0.349\pm0.014$). Spatial-$k$ is competitive, whereas random pruning requires near-dense retention. Learned sparsification benefits from anatomical node metadata but, on average, does not surpass the best fixed prior. Across all topologies, the best choice varies by patient. These results suggest that graph construction should be evaluated explicitly rather than treated as fixed preprocessing.

</details>


### [251] [A FEM-Based Surrogate Modelling and Optimization Framework for Physics-Constrained Electromagnetic Coil Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18903)
*Yucheng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了七参数电流激励线圈-铁芯基准问题在各种约束下的代理辅助优化，比较了EI-BO、COBYLA和BOBYQA三种优化方法在有限FEM评估预算下的表现，发现优化方法的排名取决于可用FEM评估预算，不同方法在早期进度、最终响应、信息利用和计算成本上各有优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对受物理约束的仿真驱动设计问题，现有文献缺乏对不同优化算法在有限FEM评估预算下的系统比较。本研究旨在厘清在曲面平滑、预算有限的实际工程场景中，贝叶斯优化与经典无导数优化方法孰优孰劣，以及排名是否随预算改变而改变。

Method: 采用Python-MPh-COMSOL工作流，将二维轴对称有限元模型与Matern 5/2高斯过程概率代理相结合。使用期望改进(EI)的序贯贝叶斯优化进行候选排序，所有报告的incumbent均通过FEM验证。设计了五组成对运行比较EI-BO、COBYLA和BOBYQA在不同评估预算阶段的表现，并进行了回顾性有限池研究比较EI与后验均值排序。

Result: 在小预算下EI-BO进步迅速，COBYLA在最早检查点表现更强，BOBYQA获得最高的平均终端响应。回顾性研究表明在该平滑响应曲面上EI相对于后验均值排序没有稳健的端点优势。在共同总电流下的选定设计检查中保持了BOBYQA-COBYLA-EI-BO的排序。

Conclusion: 早期进度、终端响应、信息利用和墙钟成本可能在不同仿真驱动设计中偏好不同方法，不存在单一最优方法。结论受限于该轴对称基准问题，不确立固定电流最优、固定功率性能或电效率优越性。

Abstract: This work evaluates surrogate-assisted optimization of a seven-parameter current-excited coil--core benchmark subject to geometric, manufacturing, and separate core and copper mass constraints. A Python--MPh--COMSOL workflow couples a two-dimensional axisymmetric finite-element method (FEM) model to a Matern 5/2 Gaussian-process (GP) probabilistic surrogate. Here, physics-constrained denotes a design problem evaluated by a governing-equation FEM model and restricted by explicit physical, geometric, manufacturing, and material-allocation constraints; it does not denote a physics-informed GP architecture. Sequential Bayesian optimization (BO) ranks candidates using expected improvement (EI), and every reported incumbent is verified by FEM. Five paired runs show that optimizer ranking depends on the available FEM-evaluation budget: EI--BO improves rapidly at small continuation budgets, COBYLA is stronger at the earliest checkpoint, and BOBYQA attains the highest mean terminal response. A retrospective finite-pool study further finds no robust endpoint advantage of EI over posterior-mean ranking on this smooth response surface. The broader result is that early progress, terminal response, information use, and wall-clock cost can favor different methods in simulation-driven design. A selected-design check at a common total current preserves the observed BOBYQA--COBYLA--EI-BO ordering. The conclusions nevertheless remain conditional on this axisymmetric benchmark and do not establish a fixed-current optimum, fixed-power performance, or electrical-efficiency superiority.

</details>


### [252] [On the Slow Convergence to Trivial Solutions of Algorithms for Hard Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18910)
*Ali Hussaini Umar,Jean Barbier,Matthieu Jonckheere,Manuel Sáenz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了随机实例上组合优化算法在有限规模下的行为，发现算法虽然在理论上最终会收敛到渐近预测界的下界，但这种收敛可能极为缓慢。在中等约束密度区间，局部算法能达到显著优于高约束密度极限下预测的结果，表明即使渐近理论预测算法注定失败，精巧的算法设计仍然至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有大量负面结果表明：对足够困难的随机实例（由图连通性/约束密度控制），在问题规模和约束密度同时趋于无穷的双重渐近极限下，没有已知的多项式时间算法能显著超越朴素启发式。然而，这种渐近视角可能掩盖了有限规模下的重要现象，作者希望重新审视有限规模的算法行为，弥合理论与实践之间的鸿沟。

Method: 采用大图渐近的严格理论分析与数值实验相结合的方法。以最大独立集和最大K-SAT这两个典型问题为研究对象，系统地考察算法在宽松（easy）、中等（intermediate）和困难（hard）三个约束密度区间的有限规模行为，比较算法实际表现与高约束密度极限下的理论预测之间的差距。

Result: 算法最终确实会收敛到理论预测的界限，但收敛速度可能极其缓慢。在中等约束密度区间（实例已被高度约束但仍未达到极限），局部算法能够获得明显优于高约束密度极限下预测性能的解，即有限规模行为与渐近行为之间存在显著的系统性差距。

Conclusion: 有限规模下算法性能与渐近极限之间的差距具有重要的实践意义：即便渐近理论预测算法不可避免会失败，精巧的算法设计在有限规模的实际问题上仍然不可或缺和富有成效。

Abstract: Hard combinatorial optimization problems, many of which are NP-hard, present fundamental algorithmic challenges. Average-case analysis on random instances has emerged as a powerful framework for understanding typical algorithmic performance beyond worst-case guarantees. A substantial body of work has established negative results: for sufficiently hard instances (often controlled by the underlying graph connectivity/constraints density), no known polynomial-time algorithm can significantly outperform naive heuristics in the double asymptotic limit where both problem size and constraints density tend to infinity. We revisit this picture by studying the finite-size behavior of some optimization algorithms across easy, intermediate, and hard regimes. Through rigorous analysis of large-graph asymptotics combined with numerical experiments on canonical problems (maximum independent set and maximum $K$-SAT), we demonstrate that while algorithms do eventually converge to theoretically predicted bounds, this convergence can be remarkably slow. In the intermediate regime where instances are already highly constrained, local algorithms achieve solutions substantially better than their predicted performance in the high-constraint-density limit. This gap between finite-regime and asymptotic behavior has important practical implications: sophisticated algorithmic design remains crucial even when asymptotic theory predicts inevitable failure.

</details>


### [253] [Converting Expert Deliberation into Financial Signals Through A Context-Aware NLP Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18911)
*Vivek Batra,Kristin Chen,Sanjiv Das,Samuel Judge,Harshad Khadilkar,Sukrit Mittal,Amir Nasrollahzadeh,Daniel Ostrov,Jacob Sisk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CDSP（上下文条件审议信号管道），将投资委员会会议记录转换为结构化预测特征，用于预测全球股票相对于全球债券的未来月度表现。模型准确率在62%-73%之间，最佳模型（句子嵌入+CDSP特征）达到73%准确率和0.73的F1分数，但统计显著性不足。情感信号比提及频率在多个分类类别中承载更强预测信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投资委员会会议记录中蕴含专家的前瞻性判断和审议信息，但非结构化文本难以直接被预测模型利用。现有方法通常直接使用原始文本或简单嵌入，缺乏对会议内容进行结构化、上下文感知的特征工程，因此需要一种能将专家审议转化为可量化预测特征的系统化框架。

Method: CDSP包含四个步骤：(1) 将会议记录按主题分段；(2) 使用大语言模型为各段落分配资产类别上下文标签；(3) 将金融关键词映射到预定义标签分类体系；(4) 构建补充特征（情感极性与提及频率）。在48个月度委员会会议数据集上，将该特征工程框架与原始文本、句子嵌入及组合表征进行对比实验，目标是预测次月全球股市相对债市的相对表现。

Result: 在多种特征组合下，预测准确率范围为62%-73%，高于基准（始终选择股票，正确率60.4%）。最佳模型（73%准确率）结合了句子嵌入与工程化CDSP特征，F1分数为0.73，但该结果在统计上与始终选择股票的基准无显著差异。情感极性在多个分类类别中比提及频率携带更强的预测信号。

Conclusion: 研究表明专家审议记录中确实包含前瞻性信息，且上下文感知的NLP技术（尤其是通过CDSP进行特征工程并结合句子嵌入）能够提取这些信息用于资产配置预测。尽管改进幅度有限且未达到统计显著性，但情感信号的有效性提示了进一步研究的方向，可探索更精细的情感分析或更大规模的数据集以验证并增强预测能力。

Abstract: We introduce the CDSP (context-conditional deliberation signal pipeline), converting an investment committee's meeting transcripts into structured predictive features. CDSP segments the meeting transcripts into topical chunks, assigns asset-class context labels using a large language model (LLM), maps financial keywords to a pre-determined taxonomy of labels, and constructs complementary features: sentiment polarity and mention frequency. This feature engineering framework is applied to a dataset spanning 48 monthly committee meetings to predict if global equities will perform better or worse than global bonds in the following month. In experiments with engineered features, raw transcript text, sentence embeddings, and combined representations, the prediction accuracy ranges from 62% to 73%, compared to always choosing stocks, which outperforms bonds 60.4% of the time. The best (73% accurate) model combines sentence embeddings with engineered CDSP features, achieving a 0.73 F1 score (although this is not statistically significant compared to always choosing stocks). Sentiment carries a stronger signal than mention frequency for several taxonomy categories. These findings suggest that experts' deliberations may contain forward-looking information that context-aware NLP can extract.

</details>


### [254] [Score the Algebra, Not the Span: Dimension Reduction for Transfer Operator Models of Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18918)
*Mark Kozdoba,Shie Mannor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"线性掩蔽"（linear masking）问题：在由弱耦合组件组成的动力系统中，传统的基于秩（谱）的降维方法（如Koopman算子的主导模态）要么需要指数数量的模态，要么完全遗漏某个组件，导致该组件无法被任何函数预测。作者提出用坐标生成的σ-代数来替代基于秩的评分，使乘积与幂次"免费"获得，并以χ²散度作为嵌入标准，带有预算保证：两倍内在维度的坐标即足以承载算子的完整谱。该准则在变分形式下可转化为可估计的目标，且限制critic为双线性类时退化为VAMP分数，因此秩方法属于同一家族的一端。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中大量物理与生物系统由多个弱耦合组件构成，而标准的谱（Koopman）降维方法在这种结构下存在根本缺陷：要么需要指数增长的模态，要么完全丢失某个组件（即"线性掩蔽"）。这意味着基于秩的方法无法以任何精度预测被掩蔽组件的任何函数，这是一个严重的实际障碍。

Method: 放弃按秩（每个坐标一个模式）的评分方式，改为对坐标生成的σ-代数进行评分，使乘积和幂次不再消耗额外模式。以嵌入的现在与未来之间的χ²散度作为准则，并给出预算保证：两倍内在维度即足够嵌入，其代数能承载算子的全部谱（含完整无限秩）。变分形式下该准则可借助现成估计器求解，将critic限制为双线性类即还原为VAMP分数，表明基于秩的方法是同一目标族的特例。

Result: 在复合了多个已发表基准系统的测试中，当秩k<100时，基于秩的方法完全遗漏被掩蔽的组件；而仅10个代数坐标即可恢复所有组件。此外，代数表示支持从少量标签预测被掩蔽组件，而直接从高维观测回归或从VAMP特征回归均失败。

Conclusion: 基于代数（σ-代数）而非基于秩的降维策略能有效解决弱耦合多组件系统中的"线性掩蔽"问题，以更少的坐标承载更完整的谱信息，并更好地支持下游预测任务。这为动力系统降维提供了超越谱方法的新范式。

Abstract: Dimension reduction for dynamical systems is standard practice, and the standard route is spectral: model the transfer (Koopman) operator by its leading modes. We show that on systems assembled from several weakly interacting components --- a structure common in physical and biological settings --- this may either require an exponential number of modes, or drop an entire component: the component is absent from the model rather than modeled coarsely, and no function of it can be predicted at any accuracy. We call this linear masking.
  The cause is that a rank-based model pays one coordinate per mode. We propose to score instead the $σ$-algebra the coordinates generate, so that products and powers come free and a component's cost is governed only by its generators rather than by all its interactions. The criterion is a $χ^2$-divergence between the embedded present and future, and it carries a budget guarantee: twice the intrinsic dimension of the dynamics is enough coordinates for an embedding whose algebra carries the operator's entire spectrum, with its full infinite rank.
  In variational form the criterion admits off-the-shelf estimators, and restricting its critic to the bilinear class returns the VAMP score on the span, so rank-based methods are one end of the same family. We demonstrate the proposed objective on a composite of published benchmark systems. We exhibit examples where the rank-based methods completely miss the masked components at all ranks $k<100$, while ten algebra coordinates recover all of them. In addition, the resulting algebra representation supports predicting the masked components from few labels, while direct regression from the high-dimensional observation or from the VAMP features fail.

</details>


### [255] [Lost in Aggregation: How Benchmarks Overlook Irreplaceable Model Strengths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18919)
*Andrej Tschalzev,Stefan Lüdtke,Heiner Stuckenschmidt,Christian Bartelt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出表格机器学习基准评测应关注数据-centric的峰值性能前沿（peak performance frontier），而非仅依赖聚合指标。框架将模型划分为不可替代、充分、冗余和易失效四类。在TabArena基准上发现常用聚合指标高度相关且主要衡量一致性与避错能力，与数据集层面的不可替代性关联较弱，导致"万金油"模型被高估而具有独特数据集优势的模型被低估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统表格基准评测通过平均分数、排名或成对胜场来汇总性能，适合选择稳健默认模型，但掩盖了一个关键问题：在特定数据集上达到最优性能需要哪些模型？需要从数据-centric视角评估峰值性能前沿。

Method: 提出数据-centric的峰值性能前沿框架，将模型依据其在前沿上的位置分类为不可替代（irreplaceable）、充分（sufficient）、冗余（redundant）或易失效（fallible）；并在TabArena基准上对比聚合指标与数据集级不可替代性的相关性。

Result: 发现常用聚合指标间高度相关且主要衡量一致性与规避失败能力，与数据集级不可替代性一致性较低；在多个数据集上表现尚可但从未最优的模型会被聚合指标奖励，而具有独特数据集优势的模型在聚合下显得平庸。

Conclusion: 基准进展不仅要看聚合指标的提升，还应评估新模型是否扩展了各数据集可达到的峰值性能集合。

Abstract: Tabular machine learning benchmarks typically summarize performance by averaging scores, ranks, or pairwise wins across datasets. Such aggregates are useful for selecting robust default models, but they can obscure a different question: which models are necessary to attain peak performance on particular datasets? We argue that benchmark evaluation should also consider the data-centric peak performance frontier, defined by the best statistically supported performance achieved on each dataset. From this perspective, a model may be irreplaceable, sufficient, redundant, or fallible depending on where it lies on the frontier relative to other models. Applying this framework to the TabArena benchmark, we find that common aggregation metrics are highly correlated and largely measure consistency and avoiding failures, while being much less aligned with dataset-level irreplaceability. Consequently, models performing decently across datasets without ever being the best choice are rewarded while models with unique dataset-specific strengths appear mediocre under aggregation. Hence, benchmark progress should be measured not only by improvements on aggregation metrics but also by whether new models expand the set of attainable peak performances across datasets.

</details>


### [256] [Transportable Causal Effect Estimation across Networks under Interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18932)
*Xiaojing Du,Jiuyong Li,Lin Liu,Debo Cheng,Jixue Liu,Thuc Duy Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的因果效应迁移方法TranCE，解决网络干扰下的跨网络因果效应迁移问题。通过将选择图扩展到网络设置，分别处理协变量偏移和结构网络偏移，推导出直接效应、溢出效应和总效应的迁移公式，并设计了双重鲁棒的估计算法。在半合成基准和真实野外实验上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果效应估计方法假设训练网络与部署网络相同，但在实际应用中，干预针对一个群体进行，而研究问题关注另一个群体，两者在拓扑结构、节点协变量组成和溢出路径上往往不同。跨网络迁移因果效应是一个现有算法无法解决的数据融合问题。

Method: 将选择图（selection diagram）扩展到网络设置，使得协变量偏移和结构网络偏移作为独立的"选择器"进入模型；从该图中推导出部署群体中直接效应、溢出效应和总效应的迁移公式，明确每种公式假设不变的干预机制和需要重新加权的观测分布；基于这些公式设计TranCE算法，结合干预结果模型、域密度比校正和交叉拟合推断，实现双重鲁棒估计。

Result: 在两个基于真实社交网络的半合成基准以及一个完全真实的天气保险野外实验上进行了大量实验，其中迁移效应与留出的随机化估计进行了对比验证，结果证实了该方法在跨网络因果效应迁移中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究为网络干扰下的因果效应跨群体迁移提供了首个系统性解决方案，有望改善社交网络和公共卫生等联网系统中的干预策略设计。

Abstract: Estimating causal effects under network interference typically assumes that the network used for training and the network used for deployment coincide. In practice, an intervention is run on one population while the question of interest concerns a different population, and the two generally differ in topology, node-covariate composition, and spillover pathways. Transporting a causal effect across networks is therefore a data-fusion problem that no existing algorithm solves. We employ a selection diagram, extended to the network setting so that covariate shift and structural network shift enter as separate selectors, and derive from it a transport formula for the direct, spillover, and total effects in the deployment population. Each formula makes explicit which interventional mechanism is assumed invariant and which observational distribution must be reweighted. We then turn the formulas into TranCE (Transported Causal Effects), a doubly-robust algorithm combining an interventional outcome model, a domain density-ratio correction, and cross-fitted inference. Extensive experiments on two semi-synthetic benchmarks derived from real-world social networks and on a fully real weather-insurance field experiment, where the transported effects are checked against held-out randomized estimates, confirm the effectiveness of our approach. Our findings have the potential to improve intervention strategies in networked systems, particularly in social networks and public health.

</details>


### [257] [Graphical Design of Interpretable Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18936)
*Pietro Barbiero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Penrose张量表示法的图形化记号系统，用于设计和表示可解释AI架构。该图形记号既能提供架构的全局视图，又能与PyTorch einsum代码一一对应。作者用此记号描述了多种可解释架构（概念瓶颈、稀疏探针、原型网络、神经加性模型、线性模型混合等），并剖析了前沿可解释语言模型Steerling-8B的架构，揭示了其残差模型特性，且可直接翻译为33行PyTorch代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释架构的表示方法存在两难：符号方程缺乏全局视角，而概率图模型和流程图无法描述实际的张量操作，导致关键洞察被隐藏且可复现性受限。因此需要一种既能全局概览又能精确映射到具体张量运算的形式化表示语言。

Method: 引入一种改编自Penrose张量表示法的图形化记号系统，用于设计可解释AI架构。该记号提供架构的全局视图，并与PyTorch einsum代码建立一一对应关系。作者先用此记号描述多种可解释架构，再用其剖析Steerling-8B可解释语言模型的关键架构组件。

Result: 该图形记号成功描述了概念瓶颈、稀疏探针、原型网络、神经加性模型和线性模型混合等代表性可解释架构；对Steerling-8B的图形化剖析揭示了其残差模型本质，给出了每个操作的几何解释，并实现了33行PyTorch代码的直接翻译。

Conclusion: 该图形记号弥合了高層架构设计与底层张量运算之间的鸿沟，兼具全局可读性与精确可复现性，为可解释AI架构的设计、实现与比较提供了统一的形式化语言。

Abstract: Designing, implementing, and comparing interpretable architectures requires a formal language to represent them. The most common representations fall short in one of two ways. Symbolic equations give no global view of an architecture at a glance. Probabilistic graphical models and flowcharts do not describe actual tensor manipulations, thus hiding key insights and limiting reproducibility. To close this gap, we introduce a graphical notation for designing interpretable AI architectures, adapted from Penrose tensor notation. This graphical notation gives a global view of an architecture and maps one to one onto PyTorch einsum code. We first use this notation to describe architectures that are interpretable by construction, including concept bottlenecks, sparse probes, prototype networks, neural additive models, and mixtures of linear models. We then diagram the key architectural components of Steerling-8B, a frontier interpretable language model. The diagram yields global insights into the architecture (e.g., showing that Steerling is a residual model), a geometric interpretation of each individual operation, and a direct translation into 33 lines of PyTorch code.

</details>


### [258] [Training Chemical Plausibility-Aware Large Language Models for Single-Step Retrosynthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18940)
*Bogdan Zagribelnyy,Ivan Ilin,Nikita Bondarev,Maksim Kuznetsov,Mathieu Reymond,Vladimir Aladinskiy,Alex Aliper,Alex Zhavoronkov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Top-K提示范式以更好地捕捉逆合成反应预测中天然的一对多性质，构建了约4560万条验证反应的大规模数据集CREED-CCV-2+USPTO-XL，训练了化学约束一致的C3LM语言模型，整合ChemCensor和面向新颖性的奖励微调，在OOD URSA-expert-2026基准上达到最先进性能，并发现LLM与常规模型探索互补的反应空间，支持基于集成的逆合成系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单步逆合成是计算机辅助合成规划的核心，但其本质上的一对多特性（一个产物对应多个合理反应路径）未被现有单一答案评估和基准测试协议所充分捕捉，现有方法难以反映反应预测的多样性与合理性。

Method: 1) 提出Top-K提示作为训练和推理范式，捕捉多样化且合理的反应预测；2) 编译超大规模数据集CREED-CCV-2+USPTO-XL（约4560万条验证反应）；3) 训练C3LM化学约束一致语言模型；4) 结合ChemCensor和面向新颖性的奖励进行微调。

Result: 在OOD URSA-expert-2026基准上达到最先进性能；反应独特性分析表明LLM和传统模型探索互补的反应空间，支持集成式逆合成系统。

Conclusion: Top-K与合理性感知训练是面向稳健的未来LLM合成规划的一个实用新方向，且集成多种模型可以受益于它们互补的反应空间探索能力。

Abstract: Single-step retrosynthesis is a central component of computer-aided synthesis planning, yet its intrinsically one-to-many nature is poorly captured by single-answer evaluation and benchmarking protocols. To address this, we introduce Top-K prompting as a robust training and inference paradigm to better capture diverse, plausible reaction predictions. We compile CREED-CCV-2+USPTO-XL, an ultra-large-scale dataset of ~45.6 million verified reactions to train the C3LM (Chemistry Constraint-Consistent Language Model). By integrating fine-tuning with ChemCensor-based and novelty-oriented rewards, our model achieves state-of-the-art performance on the OOD URSA-expert-2026 benchmark. Further analysis of reaction uniqueness shows that LLMs and conventional models explore complementary reaction spaces, motivating ensemble-based retrosynthesis systems. Overall, our results establish Top-K, plausibility-aware training as a practical new direction for robust future LLM-based synthesis planning.

</details>


### [259] [Fuzzy Accuracy Compensates for Label Subjectivity in Classification of Skin Tone Using Wearable Photoplethysmography Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18969)
*Padmini Krishnadas,Urs Hackstein,Alen Bosnjakovic,Philip J. Aston*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究利用光电容积脉搏波（PPG）信号进行Fitzpatrick六分类肤色预测。由于标注存在主观误差，传统准确率仅40-55%，但采用"模糊准确率"（预测与标注相差不超过一类即视为正确）后准确率大幅提升。三种机器学习方法中，基于原始PPG信号的深度学习配合模糊交叉熵损失函数效果最佳（模糊准确率可达96%），表明PPG信号确实受肤色影响且可被有效建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肤色分类对可穿戴设备（如PPG传感器）的准确测量至关重要，但传统分类准确率仅有40-55%的较差水平。此外，Fitzpatrick肤色标签是人工对照色卡主观确定的，存在广泛的小尺度标注误差，这使得传统评估指标无法合理反映模型真实性能。因此需要引入更符合标签噪声特点的评估方式和更优的建模方法。

Method: 采用三种机器学习方法：(1) 对原始PPG信号使用深度学习或基于树的方法（其中深度学习使用模糊版本的交叉熵损失函数，并引入"模糊准确率"评估指标，即预测与标注差不超过一个类别即视为正确）；(2) 将PPG信号通过对称投影吸引子重构（SPAR）方法转换为图像表示，再使用深度学习分类；(3) 对从PPG信号提取的特征使用传统机器学习分类。

Result: 基于原始信号配合模糊交叉熵损失的深度学习获得最佳结果；树模型在原始信号上准确率最高55%、模糊准确率最高96%；SPAR图像深度学习方法准确率44%、模糊准确率85%；PPG特征机器学习方法准确率42%、模糊准确率87%。

Conclusion: 研究表明PPG信号能够以高模糊准确率进行肤色分类，这意味着模型能在观测者所选类别的一个类别范围内准确预测肤色类别，从而证明PPG信号确实以可辨别的方式受到肤色影响。模糊准确率指标比传统准确率更能合理反映在存在标注噪声情况下的模型性能。

Abstract: We consider the problem of classification of skin tone using photoplethysmography (PPG) signals with labels of the ordinal six-class Fitzpatrick skin tones. A typical accuracy for this task is a poor 40-55 %. However, the labels are subjectively determined by comparing the skin with a colour chart, and hence contain widespread small-scale inaccuracies. By working with a "fuzzy accuracy", which deems a prediction of skin tone class to be correct if its difference from the labelled class is not greater than one, much higher accuracy is obtained which provides more convincing evidence that skin tone can be accurately predicted from PPG signals. Three machine learning approaches were used, namely deep learning or tree-based approaches on raw PPG signals, deep learning on image representations of the signals generated by the Symmetric Projection Attractor Reconstruction (SPAR) method, and machine learning on features extracted from the signals. The first method also employed a fuzzy version of the cross entropy loss function, which gave the best results. Tree-based models on raw signals give accuracies up to 55 % and higher fuzzy accuracies up to 96 %, while deep learning models on the SPAR images obtained lower results of 44 % accuracy and 85 % fuzzy accuracy. The machine learning on PPG features gave similar results to the SPAR method with accuracy of 42 % and fuzzy accuracy of 87 %. We have shown that classification of skin tone using PPG signals is possible with high fuzzy accuracy which implies that our modelling approach enables accurate prediction of skin tone class within at most one class of the observer's choice of class, from which we conclude that PPG signals are affected by skin tone in a discernible way.

</details>


### [260] [Monroe: A Molecular Foundation Model for In-Context Probabilistic Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18982)
*Blazej Banaszewski,Andrew W. Fitzgibbon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Monroe，一种大规模分子基础模型（MFM），在超过8100万分子的PM6量子化学数据集上预训练，融合了立体化学图表示改进、构象去噪与嵌入去相关训练损失、多任务学习改进以及基于先验数据拟合模型（TabPFN）的下游上下文预测。在Polaris基准上与现有MFM持平或超越，在活性悬崖基准上显著提升，且PFN下游适配策略可泛化至MiniMol和CheMeleon，产生新的SOTA变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物发现中的生物活性预测常受限于数据稀缺，因为数据生成依赖耗时昂贵的湿实验。现有分子基础模型虽旨在编码通用化学知识以应对数据受限场景，但仍存在表征与训练策略上的不足，需要更强且更具泛化能力的MFM。

Method: Monroe采用四大创新：(1)扩充规模，在PM6量子化学数据集的8100万分子上预训练；(2)改进立体化学的图表示；(3)改进训练损失，包括构象去噪（conformer denoising）和嵌入去相关（embedding decorrelation）；(4)改进多任务学习，并使用TabPFN（prior-data-fitted model）进行下游上下文预测。评估采用成对比较框架，衡量统计显著性差异。

Result: 在Polaris基准上Monroe匹配或超越现有MFM；在活性悬崖基准上相较此前方法取得显著改进。消融与迁移实验表明PFN下游预测器也使MiniMol和CheMeleon大幅提升，形成MiniMol_PFN和CheMeleon_PFN两个新SOTA变体。

Conclusion: Monroe通过规模扩展、表征与训练优化以及PFN下游适配策略，在数据受限的生物活性预测任务上取得了显著优于现有方法的性能。其PFN下游适配策略具有良好的泛化性，可推广至其他MFM，为分子发现领域提供了一种新的通用下游预测范式。

Abstract: Bioassay activity prediction is often data-limited because drug-discovery datasets rely on time-consuming and expensive wet-lab experiments for data generation and evaluation. This challenge has inspired recent research into molecular foundation models (MFMs), which aim to encode general-purpose chemical knowledge into molecular representations that generalize well in data-constrained scenarios. This paper presents Monroe, a new MFM with several innovations over the existing state of the art: increased scale allowing pre-training on over 81 million molecules from the PM6 quantum chemistry dataset; improved graph representation of stereochemistry; improved training losses including conformer denoising and embedding decorrelation; improved multi-task learning; and the use of a prior-data-fitted model (TabPFN) for downstream in-context prediction. Our evaluations use a principled pairwise comparison framework that measures statistically significant performance differences. Across established Polaris benchmarks, Monroe matches or exceeds existing MFMs, while on activity cliff benchmarks, designed to assess utility for molecular discovery, it achieves significant improvements over prior methods. Finally, ablation and transfer experiments show that PFN-based downstream predictors also substantially improve two leading existing models, MiniMol and CheMeleon, yielding new state-of-the-art variants we call MiniMol_PFN and CheMeleon_PFN, suggesting that our downstream adaptation strategy generalizes beyond Monroe. Source code is at github.com/blazejba/monroe.

</details>


### [261] [Harness Continual Learning: Continual Adaptation Beyond Model Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19013)
*Borui Kang,Jinrui Gu,Junhan Lv,Wenbin Li,Lei Wang,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"Harness Continual Learning (HCL)"，一种新的持续学习范式，在冻结基础模型的前提下，通过演化外部"harness"（如提示、记忆、工具、技能和路由规则）来实现持续适应，并引入守卫式演化机制来解决harness级遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统持续学习以模型为中心，将模型参数视为随序列经验变化的状态。但现代智能体还可通过提示、记忆、工具、技能和路由规则等harness进行适应。由于这些内容共同决定后续执行，更新harness可能会破坏先前可靠的行为，即使模型本身已冻结。这提出了一个新问题：智能体如何在不改变模型的情况下持续改进其外部状态，同时保留先前获得的行为？

Method: 论文形式化定义了HCL范式（harness围绕冻结基础模型演化），并定义了harness级遗忘。具体实现了四个执行面向组件：任务接口（Task Interface）、经验记忆（Experience Memory）、能力地图（Capability Map）和自适应路由器（Adaptive Router）。引入"守卫式harness演化"（guarded harness evolution），将更新生成与状态提交分离：持续优化器（Continual Optimizer）从执行后反馈中提出候选harness，持续评估器（Continual Evaluator）仅在验证当前改进、历史保留和有效性后才提交候选harness。

Result: 在文本推理、多模态感知和开放世界交互三个场景中，实验表明该方法能够实现能力积累和失效恢复，在多个设置下相对基线提升超过10%。组件消融实验评估了每个harness组件的贡献，受控保留扫描实验揭示了可测量的harness级遗忘，并表明稳定性-可塑性权衡可以被显式调整。

Conclusion: HCL为冻结模型时代下智能体的持续适应提供了全新范式，通过将演化从模型参数转移到外部harness，并设计守卫式提交机制来平衡稳定性与可塑性，有效缓解了harness级遗忘问题，实现了能力的持续积累与可靠恢复。

Abstract: Continual learning has largely been model-centric, treating model parameters as the state that changes with sequential experience. Modern agents can also adapt through a harness of prompts, memories, tools, skills, and routing rules. Because these contents jointly shape later execution, a harness update can disrupt previously reliable behavior even when the model is frozen. This raises a new question: how can an agent continually improve its state outside the model while retaining behavior acquired earlier? We formulate Harness Continual Learning (HCL), a new continual learning paradigm in which the harness evolves around a frozen foundation model, and define the resulting loss of earlier behavior as harness-level forgetting. We instantiate HCL with four execution-facing components: the Task Interface, Experience Memory, Capability Map, and Adaptive Router. We further introduce guarded harness evolution to separate update generation from state commitment. A Continual Optimizer proposes candidate harnesses from post-execution feedback, and a Continual Evaluator commits the resulting candidate harness only after checking current improvement, historical retention, and validity. Experiments on textual reasoning, multimodal perception, and open-world interaction demonstrate capability accumulation and failure recovery, with relative gains exceeding 10% over corresponding baselines in multiple settings. Component ablations assess the contribution of each harness component, while controlled retention sweeps reveal measurable harness-level forgetting and show that the stability--plasticity trade-off can be explicitly adjusted.

</details>


### [262] [Multi-Agent Off-Policy Deep Reinforcement Learning for Smart Campus Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19049)
*Omar Rady,Mohamed Ayman,Ali Arafa,Mohamed Shalma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在非凸校园拓扑中利用深度强化学习(DRL)实现毫米波基站的最优部署。将基站放置问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程(MDP)，并系统对比了四种DRL方案（离散单智能体DQN、空间分区多智能体DQN、连续单智能体DDPG、地理分区多智能体DDPG）。结果表明多智能体DDPG在密集场景下显著优于单智能体方案，实现了全覆盖，Jain公平指数达0.94，并在400个用户密集场景中展现出高效的收敛性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波基站的部署优化是一个NP难问题，因其最大最小公平目标具有非凸非光滑的特性，传统优化方法难以有效求解。DRL因其实时适应性和在复杂优化问题中的有效性而受到关注，本文旨在利用DRL智能算法解决这一难题，探索在真实非凸校园拓扑中的最优基站部署方案。

Method: 将毫米波基站放置问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程(MDP)，系统性地基准测试四种DRL方案：(1) 离散单智能体深度Q网络(DQN)；(2) 空间分区多智能体DQN；(3) 连续单智能体深度确定性策略梯度(DDPG)；(4) 地理分区多智能体DDPG框架。通过对这些方案的系统对比评估不同架构在求解最优基站部署上的有效性。

Result: 数值评估表明：多智能体DDPG方法在密集场景中显著优于单智能体方法；实现了全覆盖；获得了0.94的Jain公平指数；在400个用户的密集场景中展现出高度高效的计算收敛性能。

Conclusion: 多智能体DDPG框架是在复杂非凸校园拓扑中部署毫米波基站的最优DRL方案，其在覆盖率、公平性和计算效率方面均表现优异，为实际毫米波网络规划提供了可行的智能化解决方案。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) has recently gained a great attention due to its real-time adaptation and effectiveness in complex optimization problems. This paper investigates the optimal deployment of millimeter-wave (mmWave) base stations (BSs) in a realistic, non-convex campus topology. The optimization problem is NP-hard, due to the non-convex, non-smooth nature of the max-min fairness objective. To overcome these constraints, we formulate the BS placement as a Markov Decision Process (MDP) and systematically benchmark four DRL schemes: a discrete single-agent Deep Q-Network (DQN), a spatially partitioned Multi-Agent DQN, a continuous single-agent Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (DDPG), and a geographically partitioned multi-agent DDPG framework. Numerical evaluations reveal that the multi-agent DDPG approach substantially outperforms single-agent in dense scenarios. Additionally full coverage is achieved, and a fairness Jain's index of 0.94 is obtained. Finally, the multi-agent demonstrates highly efficient computational convergence of dense scenarios with $400$ users.

</details>


### [263] [Does Mapping Non-Maximal Probabilities to GMM Components Matter for S-JEPA Encoder Representations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19084)
*Wenxuan He,Yunpeng Li,Shan Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过两个匹配对照实验（FIXED-RANDPERM和UNIFORM-TAIL）验证了S-JEPA中软高斯混合模型后验概率的结构性作用。结果表明，除概率数值外，非最大概率在GMM分量间的映射关系也显著影响学到的Encoder表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: S-JEPA使用软GMM后验而非硬聚类标签以保留不确定性，但此前并不清楚仅概率数值是否足以决定编码器学习，还是非最大概率分配给哪些GMM分量同样重要。

Method: 设计两种匹配对照：FIXED-RANDPERM保留top-1分量及其概率并保留非最大概率值的多重集，但用每个物理帧固定的映射重新分配这些非最大值；UNIFORM-TAIL保留top-1分量、其概率及非最大总质量但将质量均匀分配。在三个独立随机种子下，对冻结的Encoder读出进行对比，并开展暴露实验与Phase 2轨迹的描述性跟踪。

Result: 三个种子下REAL SOFT在两个冻结Encoder读出任上均优于两种对照；REAL SOFT能更好地恢复原始GMM尾部，并在控制当前帧完整频谱后，在短时间尺度上展现出更佳的频谱动态可及性。两个暴露实验中，更多帧保留原始映射时两个读出均整体提升。

Conclusion: 软目标的数值概率结构并不能完全决定学到的Encoder表示，非最大概率到GMM分量的映射关系同样对表示学习起关键作用。

Abstract: S-JEPA uses soft Gaussian mixture model (GMM) posteriors instead of hard cluster labels to preserve uncertainty. It remains unclear whether the probability values alone are sufficient, or whether it also matters which GMM components receive the non-maximal probabilities. We test this with two matched controls. FIXED-RANDPERM keeps the top-1 component and probability together with the multiset of non-maximal probability values, but reassigns those non-maximal values using a mapping fixed for each physical frame. UNIFORM-TAIL keeps the top-1 component, its probability, and total non-maximal mass but distributes that mass uniformly. Across three independent seeds, REAL SOFT outperforms both controls on two frozen Encoder readouts. It provides better recovery of the original GMM tail and greater accessibility of spectral dynamics over short time scales after controlling for the complete spectrum of the current frame. In two exposure experiments, both readouts improved overall as more frames retained the original mapping. We also descriptively follow one Phase 2 trajectory after the switch to the online GMM. These results show that the numerical probability structure of the soft target does not fully determine the learned Encoder representation. The mapping of non-maximal probabilities to GMM components also matters.

</details>


### [264] [Open-MOPD: Diagnosing and Fixing Capability Imbalance in Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19098)
*Huan-ang Gao,Haohan Chi,Yong Yan,Shiyuan Feng,Hanlin Wu,Zheng Jiang,Bingxiang He,Wei-Ying Ma,Ya-Qin Zhang,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过受控基准实验发现标准多教师on-policy蒸馏(M-OPD)仅能恢复35.6%的领域路由oracle集成的能力上限，且失败根源在于token级别优化预算的错配（由序列长度差异、非均匀学习率导致的收敛漂移、异步更新的奖励陈旧性三个因素驱动），并提出Open-MOPD框架（token份额平衡、gap感知动态预算分配、学生奖励刷新），将恢复率提升至83.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多教师on-policy蒸馏(M-OPD)将多个领域专精的强化学习专家整合为单一通才学生，在实践中已取得成功，但其优化动力学机制和跨领域能力整合的规律尚不清晰，且缺乏开放、可严格复现的实验配方，亟需系统性的基准和理论化分析。

Method: 在SmolLM3-3B-Base上构建受控M-OPD基准，使用oracle路由将能力整合与路由模糊性隔离解析。通过机制分析识别出三类关键影响因素（序列长度结构性差异、非均匀学习率引发的收敛漂移、异步策略更新导致的多步奖励陈旧性），并据此提出Open-MOPD框架，包含token份额平衡(token-share balancing)、gap感知动态预算分配(gap-aware dynamic budget allocation)和学生奖励刷新(student reward refresh)三个核心机制。

Result: 标准M-OPD仅捕获35.6%的可用能力上限（相对领域路由oracle集成），而Open-MOPD在单一可部署学生模型中将该恢复率提升至83.4%，显著缓解了简洁任务（如指令跟随）的严重性能退化与过早停滞问题。

Conclusion: M-OPD的能力整合缺口源自token级优化预算的系统性错配而非梯度冲突，通过预算再平衡的三项正交机制可以有效修复。作者已全量开源端到端后训练配方、训练轨迹与评估套件，在学术可负担的硬件预算内提供可复现的研究范式。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (M-OPD) has emerged as a promising paradigm for consolidating domain-specialized reinforcement learning (RL) experts into a single generalist student via dense, token-level reward supervision. Despite its practical success, the optimization dynamics governing multi-teacher capability integration remain poorly understood, and open, rigorously reproducible recipes are conspicuously lacking. In this work, we establish a controlled M-OPD benchmark on SmolLM3-3B-Base with oracle routing, isolating capability integration from routing ambiguity. Our investigation reveals a pronounced capability integration gap: standard M-OPD captures only 35.6% of the available headroom relative to a domain-routed oracle ensemble, with concise tasks such as instruction following suffering severe degradation and premature stagnation. Crucially, we show that this failure stems not from gradient conflict, but from a severe misallocation of the token-level optimization budget. This pathology is driven by three orthogonal factors: structural sequence-length disparities across domains, dynamic convergence drift due to non-uniform learning rates, and multi-step reward staleness from asynchronous policy updates. To resolve these imbalances, we introduce Open-MOPD, a principled framework incorporating token-share balancing, gap-aware dynamic budget allocation, and student reward refresh. Together, these mechanisms systematically restore cross-domain balance, elevating headroom recovery from 35.6% to 83.4% in a single deployable student. We fully open-source our end-to-end post-training recipe, training trajectories, and evaluation suites on an academically accessible hardware budget.

</details>


### [265] [Pretraining Reusable Inference Across Views with Synthetic Task Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19115)
*Jielong Lu,Zhihao Wu,Jiajun Yu,Zhaoliang Chen,Haishuai Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SIMPLE，一种基于先验拟合的多视图上下文学习器（prior-fitted multi-view in-context learner），通过将多视图学习重塑为可复用的、任务条件化的推理过程而非固定融合函数，在小标注支持集条件下预测查询标签。通过构造可控的合成任务先验，并采用层级推理架构，在冻结参数和轻量适配器校准两种模式下均取得竞争性/领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预训练编码器使异构视图的表征越来越可复用，但决定视图效用和组合证据的过程仍然为每个下游任务重新学习。关于视图相关性、互补性、可靠性及缺失性的知识在任务间被反复丢弃而非迁移，导致跨任务的知识浪费和效率低下。

Method: 将多视图学习重构为学习可复用的任务条件化推理过程。具体包括：(1) 在嵌入空间构造可控的合成任务先验，生成多样化的支持-查询episodes，覆盖不同类别结构、共享/视图特有因子、表征几何、跨视图依赖、可靠性、缺失模式和分布偏移；(2) 设计层级推理架构，在视图内、跨视图以及支持/查询样本间进行推理；(3) 推理参数可冻结复用，也可通过轻量适配器校准进行任务特定对齐。

Result: 在多视图和多组学基准上，SIMPLE的冻结变体在无需更新推理骨干的情况下达到竞争性性能；采用轻量适配器校准时在大多数评估数据集上取得领先性能。在冻结、one-shot和缺失视图设置下均验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，多视图推理本身可以被预训练并跨任务复用，支持中心假设——多视图推理能力可以通过预训练获得并迁移，而轻量适配器校准仅在需要时提供任务特定的对齐。

Abstract: Modern pretrained encoders make representations from heterogeneous views increasingly reusable, but the procedure that determines view utility and combines evidence is still relearned for each downstream task. Consequently, knowledge about view relevance, complementarity, reliability, and missingness is repeatedly discarded rather than transferred across tasks. We therefore reformulate multi-view learning as learning a reusable, task-conditioned inference procedure rather than a fixed fusion function. Based on this perspective, we propose SIMPLE, a prior-fitted multi-view in-context learner that predicts query labels by conditioning on a small labeled support set. Since existing real-world datasets cover only a limited range of view configurations and task structures, we construct a controllable synthetic task prior in embedding space. It generates diverse support-query episodes with varying class structures, shared and view-specific factors, representation geometries, cross-view dependencies, reliability levels, missingness patterns, and distribution shifts. A hierarchical inference architecture then performs reasoning within views, across views, and across support and query samples. Experiments on multi-view and multi-omics benchmarks demonstrate that the frozen variant of SIMPLE achieves competitive performance without updating the inference backbone, while lightweight adapter calibration attains leading performance on most evaluated datasets. Together, the results under frozen, one-shot, and missing-view settings support the central hypothesis that multi-view reasoning itself can be pretrained and reused, while lightweight adapter calibration provides task-specific alignment when needed.

</details>


### [266] [Enhancing EBSD throughput of battery electrode materials using super-resolution generative adversarial networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19117)
*John Mangum,Andrew Glaws,Francois Usseglio-Viretta,Steven Spurgeon,Donal Finegan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用生成对抗网络（SRGAN）对锂离子电池正极材料NMC的EBSD数据进行超分辨率增强，将低分辨率EBSD图像计算性地提升至高分辨率，从而显著提高EBSD采集通量。研究表明在5倍放大（对应25倍采集速度提升或25倍视野范围）下，SRGAN在保持晶粒尺寸和形貌等关键微观结构指标精度的同时，系统性地优于传统插值方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EBSD是优化锂离子电池电极材料微观结构的核心表征手段，但其固有的缓慢采集速度限制了分析通量，难以获得具有统计代表性的微观结构数据。需要开发一种方法在不牺牲数据质量的前提下大幅提升EBSD采集效率。

Method: 采用基于生成对抗网络的超分辨率框架（SRGAN），在LiNixMnyCozO2（NMC）正极颗粒的EBSD数据上进行训练，将低分辨率数据集计算性地增强至高分辨率。模型性能与经典插值方法在2x至12x多种放大倍数下进行比较，使用定性图像指标和定量微观结构分析（晶粒面积等效直径、晶粒最大内切球直径、晶界长度）进行验证。

Result: SRGAN在各种放大倍数下系统性优于经典插值方法，尤其在保留小晶粒和维持真实晶界方面表现突出。5倍放大因子（对应25倍采集速度提升或25倍视野）在晶粒尺寸和形貌等关键指标上保持可接受精度，具体相对误差为：晶粒面积等效直径+5.7%、晶粒最大内切球直径+8.2%、晶界长度-14.6%。

Conclusion: SRGAN方法显著提升了EBSD采集效率，使EBSD能够作为高通量表征工具应用于材料研究和工业过程开发，从而获得更具统计鲁棒性的微观结构数据集。

Abstract: Quantitative microstructural characterization of Li-ion battery electrode materials using electron backscatter diffraction (EBSD) has been proven as a critical method for optimizing cell performance. However, the inherently slow nature of EBSD can hinder the throughput of analyses needed for statistical representation of a material microstructure being developed. This work demonstrates a machine learning super-resolution framework using a generative adversarial network (SRGAN) to significantly increase EBSD throughput. The SRGAN model was trained on EBSD data of LiNixMnyCozO2 (NMC) cathode particles to computationally enhance low-resolution datasets and its performance is compared against classical interpolation methods across various upscaling factors (2x to 12x). Both qualitative image metrics and quantitative microstructural analysis verified that the SRGAN systematically outperformed classical methods, particularly in preserving small grains and maintaining realistic grain boundaries. We demonstrate that a 5x upscaling factor, corresponding to a 25x speed-up in acquisition time or a 25x larger field of view, is practical while maintaining acceptable accuracy in key metrics like grain size and shape. For instance, at 5x upscaling, relative errors were +5.7%, +8.2%, and -14.6% on grain area-equivalent diameter, grain maximum sphere-inscribed diameter, and grain boundary length, respectively. The SRGAN methodology developed in this work significantly enhances the efficiency of EBSD acquisition for more statistically robust microstructural dataset, enabling EBSD as a high-throughput characterization tool for materials research and industrial process development.

</details>


### [267] [Discretizing Continuous Time Series for Imputation with Masked Diffusion Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19119)
*Dongbin Kim,Seungyun Lee,Geonwoo Shin,Jaewook Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MDTIM（掩码扩散时间序列插补模型），利用掩码扩散模型的训练范式进行时间序列插补。其核心创新包括：MASK token与有效观测在表征空间上结构正交，模型直接预测原始值而非噪声；引入随机离散化（Stochastic Discretization）将连续值映射为有序感知token，弥合离散掩码扩散与连续时序数据之间的鸿沟。实验表明MDTIM在多种缺失场景下优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列插补方法存在两大局限：一是缺失值与观测值被嵌入同一表征空间，缺乏明确的结构性分离；二是基于连续扩散的方法训练目标是预测添加的噪声而非原始信号，与插补任务目标不一致。这些问题导致插补效果不佳，亟需提出更贴合插补任务本身的设计。

Method: MDTIM采用掩码扩散模型训练范式进行插补。具体地：(1) MASK token在表示空间中与有效观测结构化正交，实现缺失与观测信息在表征层面的显式分离；(2) 模型直接预测原始值而非噪声，使学习目标与插补任务自然对齐；(3) 提出Stochastic Discretization机制，将连续时间序列值映射为具有有序感知的离散token，同时保留连续动态信息，从而弥合离散掩码扩散与连续有序时序数据之间的鸿沟。

Result: 在多个多样化基准数据集上，MDTIM在多种缺失场景下表现出优异的鲁棒性和可扩展性，一致优于最先进的确定性方法和生成式基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过从表征空间和学习目标两个层面实现任务对齐，并引入随机离散化解决离散-连续差异，MDTIM为时间序列插补提供了更自然且更强大的解决方案，验证了掩码扩散范式在时序插补任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Time series imputation is a crucial area for reliable time series analysis, yet it remains challenging due to the complex temporal dynamics and noise of real-world data. Existing approaches, however, exhibit two limitations: missing and observed values are embedded within the same representation space without explicit structural separation, and continuous diffusion-based methods are trained to predict added noise rather than the original signal. To address these, we propose the Masked Diffusion Time-series Imputation Model (MDTIM), which leverages the training paradigm of masked diffusion model for imputation tasks. The MASK token is structurally orthogonal to valid observations, and the model directly predicts the original values, naturally aligning both the representation and the learning objective with the imputation task. To bridge the gap between discrete masked diffusion and the continuous, ordinal nature of time series, we further introduce Stochastic Discretization, which maps continuous values to ordinal-aware tokens while preserving continuous dynamics. Our experiments on diverse benchmarks confirm that MDTIM achieves superior robustness and scalability, consistently outperforming state-of-the-art deterministic and generative baselines across various missing scenarios.

</details>


### [268] [PGFS++: Molecular Property Improvement under Synthesis and Diversity Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19121)
*Boqiao Zhang,Godbless James,Sai Krishna Gottipati,Andrew Fitzgibbon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PGFS++，一个合成感知的强化学习框架，用于改善分子性质的同时保持输出多样性并保证可合成性。针对PGFS在 reactant 选择上不够直接的问题，先改进为PGFS+（引入可训练embedding查找表），但发现其存在reward-hacking问题导致输出多样性坍塌，最终提出PGFS++将输入分子视为前向合成轨迹起点，应用学习到的反应模板和兼容的库存砌块，同时改善目标性质、提供合成路线并保持结构相似性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物发现早期阶段需要改进分子性质（如类药性、结合亲和力），但在无约束化学空间中优化的分子若不可合成则实用价值有限。已有方法PGFS是合成感知的强化学习方法，但其通过对反应物嵌入的预测来间接选择反应物，降低了学习效率。

Method: 首先开发PGFS+，用可训练embedding查找表表示反应模板和第二反应物，结合更有效的评分函数和RL算法提升目标性质。发现PGFS+存在reward-hacking问题（强大的反应物搜索将不同输入映射到同一高奖励的"磁铁"分子，导致输出多样性坍塌）。因此提出PGFS++：将输入分子作为前向合成轨迹的起点，应用学习的反应模板与兼容的库存砌块，生成具有改进性质、显式合成路线且与输入结构相似的分子。

Result: 在分子改进任务上的实验表明，PGFS++在提升目标性质的同时保持了高输出多样性。

Conclusion: PGFS++解决了合成感知分子优化中学习效率问题和reward-hacking导致的多样性坍塌问题，实现了性质改善、可合成性、结构相似性与输出多样性的兼顾。

Abstract: Improving molecular properties, such as drug-likeness or binding affinity, is a recurring task in early-stage drug discovery. However, molecules optimized in an unconstrained chemical space have limited practical value if they cannot be synthesized. Policy Gradient for Forward Synthesis (PGFS) is a synthesis-aware reinforcement learning method for molecular improvement, but its use of reactant embedding prediction makes reactant selection indirect, which, as we show, limits learning effectiveness. We first develop PGFS+, in which reaction templates and second reactants are represented by trainable embedding lookup tables. Combined with a more effective scoring function and RL algorithm, PGFS+ significantly improves the desired property. However, it exposes a reward-hacking failure mode: a powerful reactant search can map diverse input molecules to the same high-reward magnet molecule, improving the reward while collapsing the output diversity. We therefore introduce PGFS++, a synthesis-aware reinforcement learning framework for input-specific molecular improvement. Given an input molecule, PGFS++ treats it as the start of a forward-synthesis trajectory, applies learned reaction templates with compatible in-stock building blocks, and produces a molecule with improved target properties, an explicit synthesis route, and structural similarity to the input. Experiments on molecular improvement tasks show that PGFS++ improves target properties while preserving high output diversity.

</details>


### [269] [Leaf Values as Coordinates: Exact Contrastive Explanation for Gradient-Boosted Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19127)
*Emanuele Luzio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将梯度提升集成模型中每个树的叶值视为坐标而非中间结果，使每个样本成为R^M空间中的点，模型以线性方式（坐标求和）作用于该点。基于这一视角转变，对比解释变得精确：两个样本的差异向量仅在共享叶子的位置为零，因此被拒申请者与被接受者之间的差距由少数可追溯的坐标承载。作者在此基础上构建了反事实推荐方法（recourse method），在五个表格数据集上进行了重复交叉验证评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对比解释（contrastive explanation）和反事实推荐方法通常需要拟合、采样或假设特征可加性，缺乏精确性且无法保证推荐的可行性。标准评估方法从不询问推荐是否可执行（例如不能改变年龄或已结清的不良记录），导致评估结果与实际可用性脱节。

Method: 作者重新解读梯度提升集成模型中叶值的数学含义：将每个样本的叶值集合视为R^M空间中的坐标向量，模型的得分即坐标总和。在这种表示下，两个样本的差异向量可分解为各个叶子坐标上的变化，每个非零坐标均可追溯到真实的树分裂。基于此精确表示构建反事实推荐（recourse method），并通过重复交叉验证在五个表格数据集上评估。

Result: 所提方法的重建精度达到6.2×10^-15（可审计者无需模型即可重新核对算术）；在信用数据集上，该方法在努力程度（effort）对真实感（realism）方面Pareto非支配；当推荐限制为使用者实际可执行的变更时（排除年龄、已结清欠款等不可改变项），方法保留58%的有效性，而最强基线仅保留41%。

Conclusion: 将梯度提升模型的叶值视为坐标的数学重构，使对比解释和反事实推荐变得精确、可追溯且可验证，同时考虑了推荐的实际可行性——这一维度是标准评估方法所忽略的。这种视角转变为解释性与可操作推荐提供了更可靠的框架。

Abstract: A gradient-boosted ensemble predicts by summing one leaf value per tree. Read
  those values as coordinates rather than as intermediate results, and every
  instance becomes a point in R^M on which the model acts linearly: the score is
  the sum of the coordinates.
  This small change of view makes contrastive explanation exact. The difference
  between two instances is a vector that is identically zero wherever they share
  a leaf, so the gap between a rejected applicant and an accepted one is carried
  by a handful of coordinates, each traceable to a real split in a real tree.
  Nothing is fitted, sampled, or assumed additive in features -- the additivity
  is already there, in the right space.
  We build a recourse method on this representation and evaluate it on five
  tabular datasets under repeated cross-validation. Its recommendation
  reconstructs the model's own decision to 6.2 x 10^-15, so an auditor can
  re-check the arithmetic without the model. On the credit datasets it is
  Pareto-non-dominated on effort against realism. And when recommendations are
  restricted to changes the subject could actually make -- not their age, not a
  settled delinquency -- it retains 58% of its validity where the strongest
  baseline retains 41%, a distinction the standard evaluation cannot see because
  it never asks whether a recommendation can be carried out.

</details>


### [270] [Beyond Trial Averaging: Anchoring Neural and Visual Representations for Few-Repetition Brain-to-Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19128)
*Zhenyao Cui,Siyuan Kan,Dingkun Liu,Dongrui Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NEAR框架，通过将高重复次数的神经信号中心作为锚点，分别从查询信号和图像表征两侧逼近该锚点，解决脑-图像检索在低重复次数（few-repetition）场景下精度急剧下降的问题，在EEG、MEG和fMRI四个数据集上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑-图像检索方法通常需要平均多达80次神经试验记录才能获得良好性能，重复刺激采集带来高延迟、高成本和用户负担。当仅有一或少数几次重复时检索精度急剧下降，以往认为这单纯由查询噪声导致，但作者发现存在非传递对齐模式：低重复查询信号和高重复中心以及图像表征各自与高重复中心对齐，但彼此不直接对齐，表明查询噪声只是部分原因，图库（gallery）放置也影响检索性能。

Method: 提出神经锚定检索（NEAR）框架：将高重复中心的神经表征作为锚点，从两侧逼近——（1）降噪器（denoiser）将含噪的低重复查询信号拉向真实锚点；（2）一个小型网络从每张候选图像预测其伪锚点（pseudo anchor），将图像表征也拉向锚点。通过这种双侧锚定策略，同时缓解查询噪声和图像表征与神经中心不对齐的问题。

Result: 在EEG、MEG和fMRI四个数据集上的few-repetition场景下均一致提升检索性能。在THINGS-EEG2数据集上，200-way Top-1准确率在平均1次和4次重复时分别提升5.7和9.3个百分点。

Conclusion: NEAR通过将神经表征和视觉表征锚定到高重复中心，减少了对重复采集的依赖，使神经检索更接近实际部署，同时指出few-repetition检索精度下降不只是噪声问题，图像侧（gallery）的对齐也是关键因素。

Abstract: Decoding visual information from brain signals probes neural representations and enables neuro-rehabilitation and dream decoding. Recent brain-to-image retrieval approaches have achieved promising performance, typically by averaging many (up to 80) neural trials per image, requiring repeated stimulus presentation that increases latency, cost, and user burden. When only one or a few repetitions are available, the retrieval accuracy drops sharply. This drop is commonly attributed to query noise because averaging suppresses noise and increases signal stability. However, we find a non-transitive alignment pattern: the low-repetition query signal and the image representation each align with the high-repetition center, but not directly with each other. This pattern shows that query noise is only part of the problem and that gallery placement also affects retrieval. We therefore propose a neural-anchor-based retrieval (NEAR) framework that treats the high-repetition center as an anchor and approaches it from both sides: a denoiser pulls the noisy query toward the true anchor, and a small network predicts each candidate's pseudo anchor from its image and pulls the image toward it. Across four datasets spanning EEG, MEG and fMRI, NEAR consistently improved retrieval in the few-repetition regime. On THINGS-EEG2, it improved 200-way Top-1 accuracy by 5.7 and 9.3 percentage points respectively, when averaging one and four repetitions. By anchoring neural and visual representations, NEAR reduces reliance on repeated acquisition and brings neural retrieval closer to real-world deployment.

</details>


### [271] [SCORE: Subject Coordinate Recovery for Label-Free Cross-Subject EEG-to-Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19134)
*Zhenyao Cui,Siyuan Kan,Siyang Li,Ziwei Wang,Dongrui Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SCORE（Subject Coordinate Recovery）框架，通过恢复新用户的EEG坐标方向，实现无需目标标签和编码器更新的跨受试者EEG-to-image检索。该方法在THINGS-EEG2和Alljoined-1.6M两个公开基准上大幅超越现有方法，使基于EEG的视觉解码向实用化部署迈进一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG-to-image检索方法对于无标注校准的新用户表现远低于受试者内性能，限制了实际部署。通过跨受试者分析发现，不同受试者保持相似的概念间关系，但沿着不同的坐标方向表达，这一发现促使作者提出一种无需目标标签即可恢复目标受试者坐标方向的解决方案。

Method: SCORE框架包含两个阶段：(1) 训练阶段：将源受试者EEG与公共图像空间对齐，并通过source-only episode模拟未见受试者的坐标恢复过程；(2) 部署阶段：在编码器冻结的情况下，利用hubness校正的匹配选择可靠的EEG-图像地标，并估计正交变换以恢复目标EEG坐标，整个过程不需要源数据或目标标签。

Result: 在200-way检索任务中，SCORE对每个目标受试者均优于未适应的基线。在THINGS-EEG2上达到53.23%/83.55%的Top-1/Top-5准确率，在Alljoined-1.6M上达到12.01%/32.16%，分别超越最强基线17.45/15.70和3.08/4.62个百分点。

Conclusion: SCORE在无需目标标签或编码器更新的条件下，显著提升了跨受试者EEG视觉解码的性能，为该技术在真实场景中的稳健、实用、低延迟部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Accurate visual decoding can reveal how the brain represents visual information and recover perceived content from neural signals such as electroencephalography (EEG), with potential for neural communication. However, current EEG-to-image retrieval methods perform far below their within-subject counterparts for new users without labeled calibration, limiting real-world deployment. To understand this gap, we analyze EEG features across subjects and find that different subjects preserve similar relationships among concepts but express them along different coordinate directions. We therefore propose Subject Coordinate Recovery (SCORE), a target label-free framework combining recovery-aware source training with coordinate alignment at deployment. During training, SCORE aligns source subject EEG with a common image space and simulates unseen-subject recovery through source-only episodes. At deployment, with both encoders frozen, SCORE selects reliable EEG-image landmarks through hubness-corrected matching and estimates an orthogonal transformation to recover target EEG coordinates without source data or target labels. In 200-way retrieval on two public benchmarks, SCORE outperforms the unadapted baseline for every target subject and achieves the best overall accuracy. It reaches 53.23%/83.55% and 12.01%/32.16% Top-1/Top-5 on THINGS-EEG2 and Alljoined-1.6M, respectively, surpassing the strongest baselines by 17.45/15.70 and 3.08/4.62 percentage points. Without target labels or encoder updates, SCORE brings brain-based visual decoding closer to robust, practical, low-latency deployment across users.

</details>


### [272] [Bridge Graphical Models: Coupling, Projection, and Current-Preserving Dynamics for Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19144)
*Tiantian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出马尔可夫化间隙（Markovization gap）概念，用于量化连续时间生成模型中bridge-to-decoder压缩带来的不可约信息损失。通过引入Bridge Graphical Models (BGMs)框架统一建模扩散模型、流匹配、整流流、薛定谔桥等多种模型族，并证明该间隙可作为训练前的设计诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间生成模型通常由端点条件桥构建，但生成过程需要非预测性的马尔可夫解码器，桥到解码器的压缩是各类生成模型共有的结构性瓶颈。现有研究缺乏一个统一的、可在训练前量化的指标来评估和比较不同桥与耦合设计选择带来的信息损失。

Method: 1) 提出Markovization gap——桥速度在马尔可夫状态条件下的时间积分条件方差，即从采样器可用信息预测端点条件运动的MMSE；2) 定义Bridge Graphical Models (BGMs)框架，将端点耦合、桥律、马尔可夫投影和电流保持动力学表征分离为独立设计选择；3) 将泊松和静电模型统一表示为场线桥核；4) 在CIFAR-10和Fashion-MNIST上通过特征空间代理间隙进行验证。

Result: 在合成、潜在和像素空间的实验中，训练前几分钟即可估计的特征空间代理间隙，能够在固定架构、桥、采样器和计算资源的条件下，以与下游训练损失和FID相同的方向对设计选择进行排序。

Conclusion: Markovization gap可作为训练前的诊断指标，为桥和耦合设计提供指导，能够预测下游生成性能的相对排名，避免了仅靠试错或训练后才能评估设计优劣的局限。

Abstract: Continuous-time generative models are often built from endpoint-conditioned bridges, but generation requires a different object: a non-anticipative Markov decoder that only observes the current state and time. We identify this bridge-to-decoder compression as a structural bottleneck shared by diffusion models, flow matching, rectified flow, Schrödinger bridges, and field-based generative models. We introduce the \emph{Markovization gap}, the time-integrated conditional variance of the bridge velocity given the Markov state. It is the MMSE of predicting endpoint-conditioned motion from the information available to a sampler, and it measures an irreducible loss incurred before any neural network is trained. To make this bottleneck comparable across model families, we define \emph{Bridge Graphical Models} (BGMs), which separate endpoint coupling, bridge law, Markovian projection, and current-preserving dynamics representation as independent design choices. The same formalism also represents Poisson and electrostatic models as field-line bridge kernels with a corresponding field-line Markovization gap. Across synthetic, latent, and pixel-space pilots on CIFAR-10 and Fashion-MNIST, a feature-space proxy gap estimated in minutes before training ranks design choices in the same direction as downstream training loss and FID under fixed architecture, bridge, sampler, and compute. These results support the Markovization gap as a pre-training diagnostic for bridge and coupling design.

</details>


### [273] [Learned, Then Lost: A Measured Single-Example Counterfactual in Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19168)
*Zachary Speck,Asa Shepard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过24次小规模反事实实验，测量（而非估计）单个训练样本对一个完成GPT-2模型（124M参数，32个模型，OpenWebText）的贡献。在训练第200步注入一个194-token的固定文段，研究发现该文段在一次暴露后会被学习但随后衰减，50步后注入组在文段上的交叉熵显著优于未注入组（8/8种子，p<10^-4），但最终步骤检测不到该差异。所有几何测量均在衰减后进行，结果显示注入使模型在其basin内重新定位（权重位移达到种子间欧氏距离的44.1%），但不会将模型移出其basin（barrier仅达到3.0%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单个训练样本对最终模型的贡献通常只能估计而非直接测量，因为直接测量需要在仅一行数据不同的条件下进行两次昂贵的完整预训练。作者希望打破这种估计范式，通过小规模运行实际的反事实实验来精确测量单个样本的贡献。

Method: 在124M参数的GPT-2模型上，以4种条件×8种种子训练32个模型。在训练第200步（共9,536步，处于峰值学习率），将256行batch中的一行替换为含194-token固定文段的注入，共三个注入条件（语料真实主语流畅散文、与真实主语梯度增量匹配0.14%内的虚构主语流畅散文、随机键盘字符）和一个未注入孪生条件。预注册了插值loss barrier、留出交叉熵和逐层中心核对齐等指标。

Result: 注入50步后，看到文段的模型对文段的预测优于未注入模型0.039和0.044 nats（8/8种子，p<10^-4），但在最终步骤检测不到该差异（p=0.25, p=0.71）或两文段间差异（p=0.54）。插值loss barrier为+0.0068（p=0.509），留出交叉熵为-0.00044（p=0.310），逐层CKA无显著分离。权重位移达种子间欧氏距离的44.1%，且在训练中途已92%稳定，而barrier仅为种子间barrier的3.0%，二者相差约15倍。

Conclusion: 单个训练样本对最终模型的影响在学习后会衰减至无法检测，但它在训练过程中确实将模型在其损失landscape的basin内重新定位。该影响不改变basin归属，权重位移与barrier之间的15倍差距表明单个样本的影响被限制在basin内部，模型不会被推到另一个泛化解。

Abstract: A single training example's contribution to a finished model is normally estimated rather than measured, because measuring it takes two expensive full pre-training runs that differ in one row of one batch. We ran that counterfactual 24 times at a small scale. We trained 32 GPT-2 models at 124M parameters from scratch on OpenWebText, over four conditions and eight seeds. At step 200 of 9,536, at peak learning rate, we replaced one row of a 256-row batch with a fixed context injection carrying a 194-token passage. The three injected conditions are: 1. fluent prose with a corpus-attested subject, 2. fluent prose with a fabricated subject matched to it within 0.14% on full-batch gradient delta, and 3. random keyboard characters. The fourth condition is an uninjected twin. The passage is learned from one exposure and then decays. Fifty steps after injection, the arm that saw a passage predicts it better than the arm that did not by 0.039 and 0.044 nats of cross-entropy on the passage, at eight of eight seeds with p < $10^{-4}$. At the final step we do not detect that difference for either passage, at p = 0.25 and p = 0.71, against minimum detectable effects of 0.025 and 0.079 nats, nor between the two passages, at p=0.54. Every geometric measure we report is taken after that decay. Our pre-registered contrast on interpolation loss barrier is +0.0068 with p = 0.509, against a minimum detectable effect of 0.032 barrier units. Held-out cross-entropy is $-0.00044$ with p = 0.310. Per-layer centered kernel alignment does not detectably separate any condition at any layer. Weight displacement reaches 44.1% of the seed-to-seed Euclidean distance and is 92% settled by the midpoint of training, while the barrier reaches 3.0% of the seed-to-seed barrier. Those two figures sit roughly 15 times apart, and that is a lower bound. The injection relocates the model within its basin without moving it out.

</details>


### [274] [Lévy Attention: Single-Pass Predictive Uncertainty for Continuous-Time Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19171)
*Sotirios P. Chatzis,Loukas Papadoulas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Lévy Attention，一种基于非均匀泊松随机测度的交叉注意力算子，使不规则采样时间序列模型的每个预测不仅输出结果，还能以零额外成本闭式给出置信度（通过方差恒等式组合证据质量与值域离散度），无需额外训练头。经验上该不确定性信号优于20-pass MC Dropout，并支持校准高斯分布与分裂共形包装达到名义覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度模型能对不规则采样时间序列在任意连续时间戳上作答，但无法告知每个答案的可信程度。现有不确定性估计通常需要额外训练头或多个前向采样，成本高昂。本文希望利用注意力层本身弥合这一差距，让产生预测的那次前向传播同时输出闭式的信任度。

Method: 提出 Lévy Attention：一种交叉注意力算子，其输出是对非均匀泊松随机测度的随机积分。查询-键兼容性在连续（时间×通道）索引空间上构造强度函数，泊松测度在该强度下散播原子，输出对原子处的插值值域求平均。在期望上等价于磨光的余弦核注意力，可替换softmax层并通过精确梯度训练。泊松构造保留了softmax丢失的闭式证据量Λ_q（总兼容性质量）和值散度trΣ_V(q)，通过精确方差恒等式组合成根均方偏差σ̂(q)，由确定性前向传播零成本输出。

Result: 在t-PatchGNN上，算子替换最多牺牲5.6%准确率（对照匹配实验），在最稀疏数据集上无损失。分歧信号在五种子匹配套件上优于20-pass MC dropout；σ̂缩放校准高斯分布，其零采样CRPS优于五十次采样器；分裂共形包装在每个水平达到名义覆盖率；单次前向在1.4秒内为3,383个未见患者的信任度排序。

Conclusion: Lévy Attention证明注意力层本身即可同时担当预测器与不确定性估计器：通过随机泊松公式化，预测与闭式置信度在同一前向传播中同时产生，且无需训练额外头。分歧信号在稀疏数据上携带主要不确定性信息，而证据因子从密集数据的无信息平滑过渡到稀疏数据的强信息，实现了高效、无损（尤其在稀疏场景）的不确定性估计。

Abstract: Deep models for irregularly-sampled time series answer queries at arbitrary continuous timestamps, yet report nothing about how far each answer should be trusted. We show the attention layer itself can close that gap: with the right stochastic formulation, the pass that makes each prediction also reports, in closed form and at no extra cost, how far it should be trusted. We introduce Lévy Attention, a cross-attention operator whose output is a stochastic integral against an inhomogeneous Poisson random measure: query-key compatibilities assemble an intensity over a continuous (time x channel) index space, the measure scatters atoms under it, and the output averages an interpolated value field at those atoms. In expectation it reduces to a mollified cosine-kernel attention, so it replaces a softmax layer and trains with exact gradients.
  What softmax discards, the Poisson construction preserves in closed form: the evidence $Λ_q$ (total compatibility mass) and the disagreement $\mathrm{tr}\,Σ_V(q)$ (value spread). An exact variance identity makes their combination $\hatσ(q)=\sqrt{\mathrm{tr}\,Σ_V(q)\,\varphi(Λ_q)}$ the root-mean-square deviation of the sampled operator, emitted by the deterministic pass with no trained head.
  Empirically, disagreement carries the signal, while the evidence factor swings from uninformative on dense data to strongly informative on sparse. On t-PatchGNN the operator swap costs at most 5.6% accuracy against a matched control and nothing on the sparsest dataset. The free disagreement signal improves on 20-pass MC dropout across matched five-seed suites, and $\hatσ$ scales a calibrated Gaussian whose zero-sample CRPS beats a fifty-draw sampler; a split-conformal wrapper reaches nominal coverage at every level, and one pass ranks 3,383 unseen patients by trust in 1.4 seconds.

</details>


### [275] [Beyond Teacher Likelihood: Group-Calibrated On-Policy Distillation for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19181)
*Zhu Zhang,Jixun Wang,Xiaoang Xu,Xiaorong Wang,Zihan Zhou,Zhiyuan Wang,Shuo Wang,Chaojun Xiao,Yuezhi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GC-OPD（分组校准的在线蒸馏）方法，通过在每组rollout内分别归一化验证奖励和轨迹级OPD分数，用它们的差值作为带符号的教师-验证器分歧残差，并通过相对优势信用分配（RACA）将轨迹级残差分配到各token上。在五个长上下文基准上，GC-OPD将Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B的平均分从29.08/35.12提升到40.47/44.65，分别优于普通OPD的39.31/43.56。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线蒸馏（OPD）在长上下文任务中存在根本性错配：token级教师指导偏向局部合理的响应，可能遗漏分布在整个输入中的证据或违反全局任务约束；而任务特定的验证器在响应级评估任务完成度并返回反映部分成功的分级奖励。作者在长输入范围的证据聚合任务上诊断出轨迹级OPD分数与验证奖励的渐行渐远，表明教师与验证器之间的分歧越来越大，因此需要一种方法将两者有机结合。

Method: GC-OPD包含两个核心组件：（1）分组校准：在每组rollout内分别对验证奖励和轨迹级OPD分数进行归一化，用归一化后的差值作为带符号的教师-验证器分歧残差；（2）相对优势信用分配（RACA）：根据每个token的相对OPD优势（保留原始OPD信号）将轨迹级残差分布到各令牌上，实现轨迹级验证信息到token级指导的传递。

Result: 在五个长上下文基准上，GC-OPD使官方Qwen3-4B检查点的五基准平均分从29.08提升到40.47（普通OPD为39.31），Qwen3-8B从35.12提升到44.65（普通OPD为43.56）。消融实验显示带符号残差优于额外OPD派生项或直接组归一化验证奖励加法，RACA优于均匀token分配。

Conclusion: 分组相对残差校准能够在保留密集token级指导的同时有效整合验证器结果，解决了长上下文任务中教师token级指导与验证器响应级评估之间的错配问题，为在线蒸馏在长上下文场景下的应用提供了更有效的训练策略。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own responses using dense token-level guidance from a stronger teacher. In long-context tasks, however, token-level teacher support can favor locally plausible responses that omit evidence distributed across the input or violate global task constraints. Task-specific verifiers, in contrast, evaluate task completion at the response level and may return graded rewards that reflect partial success. We diagnose this mismatch on fixed responses from two representative long-context evidence-aggregation tasks. Across longer input ranges, trajectory-level OPD scores become progressively less aligned with verifier rewards, indicating teacher-verifier disagreement. Motivated by this observation, we introduce Group-Calibrated On-Policy Distillation (GC-OPD). GC-OPD separately normalizes verifier rewards and trajectory-level OPD scores within each rollout group and uses their difference as a signed teacher-verifier disagreement residual. Relative-advantage-based credit assignment (RACA) distributes this trajectory-level residual across tokens according to their relative OPD advantages while preserving the original OPD signal. Across five long-context benchmarks, post-training with GC-OPD raises the five-benchmark averages of the official Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B checkpoints from 29.08 to 40.47 and from 35.12 to 44.65, respectively. Vanilla OPD reaches 39.31 and 43.56 under the same setup. Controlled ablations show that the signed residual is more effective than either an additional OPD-derived term or direct group-normalized verifier reward addition, while RACA further improves over uniform token allocation. Together, these results demonstrate that group-relative residual calibration can incorporate verifier outcomes without discarding dense token-level guidance. Code is available at https://github.com/SolereZhang/GC-OPD.

</details>
